Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) excel at many tasks but still struggle with spatial reasoning when critical information is not directly observable. Many such problems require imaginative perception: inferring what would be seen from an unseen viewpoint, tracing paths through occluded spaces, or integrating partial observations into a coherent spatial representation. We introduce Imaginative Perception Tokens (IPT), intermediate perceptual representations that externalize what a VLM would perceive under alternative spatial configurations while remaining consistent with the observed input.
  To study this capability, we formulate three tasks, Perspective Taking (PET), Path Tracing (PT), and Multiview Counting (MVC), and construct datasets of approximately 20K examples with ground truth imaginations, answers, and evaluation benchmarks. Using the unified VLM BAGEL as the backbone, IPT supervision consistently improves spatial reasoning and often outperforms textual chain of thought training, even without generating images at inference time. On MVC, IPT improves accuracy by 3.4% and achieves competitive performance with strong closed-source models on PT. We further find that combining IPT and label-only supervision yields additional gains, whereas textual chain of thought can substantially degrade performance, suggesting a modality mismatch when spatial computation is forced through language. Overall, IPT provides a principled supervision signal for reasoning about unobserved spatial structure, improving generalization while producing interpretable intermediate representations.

</details>


### [89] [VESTA: Visual Exploration with Statistical Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00384)
*William Rudman,Abhishek Divekar,Kanishk Jain,Sebastian Joseph,Stella S. R. Offner,Matthew Lease,Kyle Mahowald,Greg Durrett,Junyi Jessy Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VESTA框架通过为视觉语言模型配备动态增长的探索工具包，在数据转换、假设驱动的可视化和稳健统计测试方面显著提升了模型拟合性能，尤其在复杂领域任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量模型拟合是科学工作流程中的核心步骤，但自动化程度最低。现有基于代理的系统依赖语言模型和视觉语言模型进行模型迭代优化，但在更具挑战性的建模任务中表现不佳，主要原因是缺乏主动的数据探索能力。

Method: 提出VESTA框架，其核心创新在于：1）为VLM配备动态增长的探索工具包；2）在模型优化前后主动探索数据；3）选择或创建诊断工具（数据转换、假设驱动可视化、统计测试）；4）工具可累积在模型上下文中重复使用。在三种工具配置下进行评估：无工具、静态专家编写工具、动态模型生成工具。同时引入DAWN基准数据集，涵盖分布拟合、时间序列建模和天文学任务。

Result: VESTA的动态工具创建机制显著优于现有代理流程，在复杂和领域特定任务上优势最大。动态生成的工具比现有可视化工具创建系统更复杂，每个函数覆盖更多诊断类别，并强烈倾向于生成VLM评判器可直接推理的视觉输出。

Conclusion: 主动数据探索与动态工具创建相结合的方法有效提升了定量模型拟合的自动化水平，为科学工作流程的自动化提供了新方向，特别适用于复杂的领域特定建模任务。

Abstract: Fitting quantitative models to data is a central step in scientific workflows, yet it remains one of the least automated. Recent agent-based systems leverage language and vision-language models (VLMs) to iteratively propose and refine statistical models, but these systems struggle on more challenging modeling tasks. To address these limitations, we introduce VESTA: Visual Exploration with Statistical Tool Agents, a framework that equips VLMs with a dynamically growing exploration toolkit to guide model refinement through data transformations, hypothesis-driven visualizations, and robust statistical tests. Unlike prior systems that rely on iterative critique alone, VESTA actively explores data before and during refinement by selecting or creating diagnostic tools, which accumulate in the model's context and can be reused later. We evaluate VESTA against established baselines in three toolkit configurations: no tools, static expert-written tools, and dynamic model-written tools. To support this evaluation, we introduce DAWN (Dataset for Automated Workflows and Numerical Modeling), a benchmark targeting distribution fitting and time series modeling with varying difficulty tiers, and culminating in real-world astronomy tasks including modeling initial mass functions and gravitational-wave chirp signals. We find that VESTA's dynamic tool creation outperforms prior agentic pipelines, with the largest gains on complex and domain-specific tasks. We further show that dynamically generated tools are substantially more sophisticated than those produced by existing visual tool-creation systems, covering more diagnostic categories per function and strongly preferring visual outputs that the VLM critic can reason over directly.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [Human-in-the-Loop Contextual Bandits for Short-Term Rental Dynamic Pricing: Structural Equivalence of Historical Warm-Up and Approval-Gated Live Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02595)
*Oleg Miroshnichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出人机协同门控老虎机(HITL-GB)框架，利用历史数据热启动解决短租市场动态定价中的稀疏反馈和冷启动问题，证明人工审批约束可转化为统计优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短租市场动态定价面临三大挑战：定价决策具有显著财务风险、运营者需要可解释性、市场反馈稀疏（每晚仅一个预订结果）。纯在线老虎机学习在稀疏反馈市场中需要数周到数月的冷启动期，导致实际部署不可行。

Method: 引入人机协同门控老虎机(HITL-GB)框架：上下文老虎机算法生成价格推荐，人工代理保留接受、修改或拒绝推荐的权限。证明在审批约束下，历史定价数据（在先前确定性策略下收集）结构上等同于策略内热启动数据，用于初始化老虎机后验。形式化审批门控奖励信号，推导正则化岭回归热启动程序，并使用分层因子Thompson采样(HF-TS)算法族进行验证。

Result: 在真实短租生产数据（匿名化城市市场，2个房源，2022年4月至2026年4月，1,461个夜间定价回合）上验证，热启动程序将有效冷启动从约150个回合压缩至约30个回合。结构等价性结果具有领域无关性，适用于任何需要人工审批的高风险领域（临床药物剂量、信贷审批、内容审核、放射诊断等）。

Conclusion: 在受监管行业中，强制性人工监督是统计资产而非部署约束。该框架将审批约束转化为热启动优势，为高风险领域的人工智能部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Dynamic pricing in short-term rental (STR) markets presents a distinctive challenge for online learning algorithms: pricing decisions carry significant financial risk, operators require explainability, and market feedback is sparse (one booking outcome per listed night). We introduce the Human-in-the-Loop Gated Bandit (HITL-GB) framework, in which a contextual bandit algorithm generates price recommendations but a human agent retains authority to accept, modify, or reject each recommendation before it is applied. We show that under this approval constraint, historical pricing data -- collected under a prior deterministic policy -- is structurally equivalent to on-policy warm-up data for initialising the bandit's posterior, bypassing the weeks-to-months cold-start period that renders pure online bandit learning impractical in sparse-feedback markets. We formalise the approval-gated reward signal, derive a regularised ridge-regression warm-up procedure from historical episodes, and validate the approach on real STR production data (anonymised urban market, 2 rooms, April 2022 -- April 2026, 1,461 nightly pricing episodes). Our warm-up procedure compresses effective cold-start from ~150 episodes to ~30 episodes when initialising agents from the Hierarchical Factored Thompson Sampling (HF-TS) family. We further argue that the structural equivalence result is domain-agnostic: any high-stakes domain where human approval is legally or operationally required -- including clinical drug dosing, credit origination, content moderation, and radiological diagnosis -- satisfies the same conditions and benefits from the same warm-up strategy. In regulated industries, mandatory human oversight is thus a statistical asset rather than a deployment constraint.

</details>


### [91] [Spectral Asymptotics of Neural Network Loss Landscapes: An Exact Decomposition of the Curvature Exponent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02596)
*Anherutowa Calvo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了深度网络中曲率指数α（描述Hessian特征值与梯度奇异值的标度关系）随层类型系统变化的规律，提出了谱对齐分解公式和谱传递恒等式，并基于此开发了性能优于AdamW的架构自适应预条件器Spectral Newton。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到曲率指数α在不同层类型中呈现系统性变化：卷积层α≈2，Transformer注意力层α≈1，MLP上投影层α<1。为了理解这种变化的根本原因，作者旨在建立理论框架来解释α的变化规律及其与网络架构的几何关系。

Method: 1. 证明谱对齐分解定理：α = 2 + dlogΦ_k/dlogσ_k，将α的变化归结为Kronecker因子特征基与梯度奇异方向的对齐问题
2. 推导谱传递恒等式：s = αγ，连接曲率指数、有效梯度秩衰减率γ和Hessian衰减指数s
3. 利用zeta函数界定参与比，分析曲率方向集中性
4. 提出架构自适应预条件器T(σ;α)并实现Spectral Newton优化算法

Result: 1. 谱传递恒等式在93层、5种架构、3个数据集上以无自由参数方式恢复s值，中位误差约2%
2. 理论证明了曲率在每个层中集中到约一个有效方向
3. Spectral Newton在α≈2的视觉基准测试上性能优于AdamW
4. 成功解释了LayerNorm、残差连接和softmax头部对α的影响

Conclusion: 本研究通过谱对齐分解将曲率指数α的变化问题转化为可解的几何问题，建立了Hessian谱性质与梯度结构之间的代数联系。理论框架不仅解释了架构依赖的曲率现象，还为开发架构自适应优化器提供了理论基础，在视觉任务中展示了实际应用价值。

Abstract: The curvature exponent $α$ in $h_k \propto σ_k^α$ -- governing how Hessian eigenvalues scale with gradient singular values -- varies systematically across layer types ($α\approx 2$ for convolutions, $\approx 1$ for transformer attention, $< 1$ for MLP up-projections). Why? We prove the Spectral Alignment Decomposition: $α= 2 + d\logΦ_k / d\logσ_k$, where $Φ_k$ measures alignment between Kronecker factor eigenbases and gradient singular directions. This reduces "why does $α$ vary?" to a geometric question we answer for LayerNorm, residual connections, and softmax heads. The decomposition implies a spectral transfer identity $s = αγ$ linking curvature exponent, effective gradient rank-decay $γ$, and Hessian decay exponent $s$. The identity is algebraic; its empirical content is that $α$ and $γ$, fit on independent data (HVPs vs. SVD), recover $s$ to ~2% median error across 93 layers, five architectures, and three datasets -- with no free parameters. A zeta-function bound on participation ratio shows curvature concentrates onto effectively one direction per layer. As a proof of concept, we derive the architecture-adaptive preconditioner $T(σ;α)$ and show that Spectral Newton -- implementing $T$ in the gradient singular basis -- outperforms AdamW on vision benchmarks where $α\approx 2$.

</details>


### [92] [Making Brain-Computer Interfaces More Secure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02597)
*Md Fahimul Kabir Chowdhury,Gahangir Hossain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级自定义CNN架构，用于提升EEG脑机接口系统在对抗攻击下的鲁棒性，实验证明该模型在对抗扰动下表现优于EEGNet、DeepConvNet和SleepEEGNet等基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于EEG的脑机接口系统发展迅速，但现有研究主要关注分类准确率，忽视了安全性和鲁棒性。近期研究表明EEG-BCI系统容易受到对抗攻击，微小的扰动可能导致错误诊断，因此评估模型对对抗扰动的鲁棒性对于确保系统可靠部署至关重要。

Method: 提出一种轻量级自定义卷积神经网络(CNN)架构，在两个EEG数据集上进行评估，并在基于梯度的对抗攻击场景下与三种专为EEG定制的CNN模型(EEGNet、DeepConvNet和SleepEEGNet)进行对比。

Result: 实验结果表明，该提出的模型在对抗扰动下的分类性能持续优于基准模型，展现出更强的鲁棒性。相比EEGNet、DeepConvNet和SleepEEGNet等现有模型，在对抗攻击条件下保持更高的分类准确率。

Conclusion: 轻量级架构在提升EEG脑机接口系统对抗攻击鲁棒性方面具有重要潜力，为在对抗环境下实现可靠的EEG-BCI系统部署提供了有效解决方案，强调了在BCI系统设计中考虑安全性和鲁棒性的重要性。

Abstract: The development of brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) based on electroencephalograms (EEGs) has advanced significantly mainly to machine learning. Although the majority of earlier research has been on increasing classification accuracy, relatively little focus has been placed on security and robustness. According to recent research, EEG-based BCIs are susceptible to adversarial attacks, which can cause misdiagnosis due to minute, well-crafted disturbances. Evaluating model robustness against such perturbations is therefore critical for ensuring reliable deployment. In this study, we propose a lightweight custom Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) architecture to investigate adversarial robustness in EEG-based BCIs. The suggested method is assessed using two EEG datasets and contrasted with three novel CNN models tailored to EEG, namely EEGNet, DeepConvNet, and SleepEEGNet, under gradient-based adversarial attack scenarios. According to experimental findings, the suggested model continuously performs better in classification under adversarial perturbations compared to baseline models, indicating improved robustness. These findings highlight the potential of lightweight architectures for enhancing the reliability of EEG-based BCI systems under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [93] [Assessing Region-Level EEG Contributions to Cognitive Workload Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02598)
*Jacob Wong,Sohan Singh,Prannaya Gupta,Jin Xing Ang,Kritika Johari,U-Xuan Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过大规模跨数据集分析发现，额叶和额中央区域的电极在认知负荷预测中表现最佳，优于全脑电极基准约15-20%，为设计高效、可泛化的EEG监测系统提供了依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管EEG广泛用于认知负荷评估，但电极区域在不同任务、数据集和被试间的贡献一致性仍不明确。理解哪些脑区最稳定地包含负荷相关信息，对构建高效、通用的监测系统至关重要。

Method: 提出区域级评估框架，从解剖学定义的头皮区域提取特征进行模型训练和评估。在四个公开EEG负荷数据集上进行大规模分析，采用模型无关的基于性能的方法量化区域重要性，使用基于排名的聚合策略确保跨实验配置的稳健性。

Result: 在被试独立评估中，额叶电极组的相对排名位置优于全脑基准约15-20%，且使用电极数量大幅减少。额中央区域展现最稳定的预测效用，而后部和枕叶区域贡献一致性较低。

Conclusion: 认知负荷相关的EEG信息最稳定地保留在额叶和额中央电极组中，支持设计高效且可泛化的EEG负荷监测系统，可减少电极数量同时保持预测性能。

Abstract: Accurate and generalizable estimation of cognitive workload from electroencephalography (EEG) is critical for human-centered and safety-critical systems. Although EEG is widely used for workload assessment, the consistency of region-level EEG contributions across tasks, datasets, and subjects remains unclear. This paper presents a region-level evaluation framework for EEG-based workload prediction in which models are trained and evaluated using features extracted exclusively from electrodes belonging to anatomically defined scalp regions. We perform a large-scale analysis across four publicly available EEG workload datasets spanning diverse task demands, recording hardware, and electrode montages. Region importance is quantified using a model-agnostic, performance-based approach under both mixed-subject and subject-independent evaluation protocols, with results aggregated using a rank-based strategy to ensure robustness across experimental configurations. Across all datasets and subject-independent evaluations, frontal electrode groups outperform the full-scalp baseline by approximately 15-20% in relative rank position while using substantially fewer electrodes. Fronto-central regions exhibit the most stable predictive utility, whereas posterior and occipital regions contribute less consistently across experimental conditions. These findings indicate that workload-relevant EEG information is most consistently retained within frontal and fronto-central electrode groups, supporting the design of efficient and generalizable EEG-based workload monitoring systems.

</details>


### [94] [Testing the Test: Score-Direction Instability in Class-Split Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02601)
*Alejandro Ascarate,Leo Lebrat,Rodrigo Santa Cruz,Clinton Fookes,Olivier Salvado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示类分割异常检测评估协议在异常类与正常分布重叠时会失效，并提出一种无需训练的诊断方法来预测这种不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的类分割评估协议被广泛用作无条件下异常检测的代理，但当保留的异常类在表示空间中与正常分布重叠时，该协议可能变得不适定，导致异常分数崩溃甚至反转。作者旨在揭示这一根本性问题并提供诊断工具。

Method: 提出了一种无需训练的诊断方法——邻域类别泄漏（neighborhood class leakage），该方法能够预测异常分数方向的不稳定性。研究在Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10和Imagenette数据集上进行了验证，涵盖了像素空间和VAE潜在空间两种表示。

Result: 实验证明，当异常类与正常分布在表示空间重叠时，异常分数会趋向于随机水平甚至发生反转，且优选的分数方向可能依赖于未知的异常类别。所提出的邻域类别泄漏诊断方法能够有效预测不同数据集和表示空间中的分数方向不稳定性。

Conclusion: 类分割异常检测基准应被视为依赖几何结构的压力测试，而非异常检测能力的无条件证据。研究建议重新审视该评估协议的有效性边界。

Abstract: Within-dataset class-split evaluation is widely used as a proxy for fully unconditional out-of-distribution anomaly detection. We show that this protocol can become ill-posed when the held-out anomaly class overlaps the normal mixture in representation space. In this regime, anomaly scores may collapse toward chance or even invert, and the preferred score direction can depend on the unknown anomaly class. We introduce a simple training-free diagnostic, neighborhood class leakage, and show that it predicts score-direction instability across Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Imagenette, in both pixel and VAE latent spaces. Our results suggest that class-split AD benchmarks should be treated as geometry-dependent stress tests rather than unconditional evidence of anomaly-detection ability.

</details>


### [95] [Graph Mamba Survival Analysis Based on Topology-Aware ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02602)
*Yuanfang Chen,Peiqiang Yan,Yuntao Shou,Qian Zhao,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TopoMamSurv框架，通过拓扑感知排序和双向Mamba模块解决WSIs生存分析中Transformer计算复杂度高和图结构建模不足的问题，在5个TCGA数据集上验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算病理学中，WSIs生存分析对患者预后评估至关重要，但面临重大技术挑战。虽然Transformer通过自注意力机制捕获长距离依赖关系，但其O(N²)时间复杂度在大规模WSIs图结构中造成严重计算瓶颈。Mamba模型以线性复杂度突破了这一瓶颈，但由于其对输入数据顺序的高度敏感性，传统图Mamba中的节点排序方法（如基于节点度或子图大小的方法）无法充分考虑图数据的拓扑连通性，限制了Mamba序列建模的性能。此外，其单向架构无法利用图像的双向空间结构。

Method: 提出了基于拓扑感知排序的新型图Mamba生存分析框架(TopoMamSurv)：1)设计拓扑感知排序(TAO)策略，使节点排序更好地适应Mamba的序列敏感性；2)构建双向Mamba模块并集成图卷积网络(GCN)，实现图像的双向空间上下文建模；3)形成"局部聚合-全局捕获"的层次化特征学习架构，通过TAO、双向语义建模和层次化特征融合的系统性设计。

Result: 可视化实验证实，通过TAO策略提取的节点确实表现出更高的相似性。该框架在五个TCGA数据集上验证了其全面的性能优势，有效解决了WSIs分析中长距离依赖建模、计算效率和空间结构利用之间的矛盾。

Conclusion: TopoMamSurv框架通过拓扑感知排序、双向语义建模和层次化特征融合的系统性设计，成功协调了WSIs分析中长距离依赖建模、计算效率和空间结构利用之间的矛盾，为计算病理学中的生存分析提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In computational pathology, Whole Slide Images (WSIs) survival analysis is crucial for patient prognosis assessment, but it faces multiple technical challenges. Although the Transformer captures long-range dependencies through its self-attention mechanism, its $O(N^2)$ time complexity causes a severe computational bottleneck in large-scale WSIs graph structures. The Mamba model breaks through the Transformer's computational bottleneck with linear complexity. But, owing to Mamba's high sensitivity to the order of input data, traditional node sorting methods in Graph Mamba, such as those based on node degree or subgraph size, fail to adequately account for the topological connectivity of graph data. This inadequacy consequently restricts the performance of Mamba's sequential modeling. Moreover, its unidirectional architecture cannot leverage the bidirectional spatial structure of images. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a novel Graph Mamba survival analysis framework based on topology-aware ordering (TopoMamSurv) to adapt to the sequential sensitivity of Mamba. Our visualization experiments further confirmed that the nodes extracted through the topology-aware ordering (TAO) strategy indeed exhibit higher similarity. Furthermore, we designed a bidirectional Mamba module and integrated a Graph Convolutional Network (GCN) to achieve bidirectional spatial context modeling of images, forming a hierarchical feature learning architecture for "local aggregation - global capture." This framework effectively reconciles the contradiction between long-range dependency modeling, computational efficiency, and spatial structure utilization in WSIs analysis through its systematic design of TAO, bidirectional semantic modeling, and hierarchical feature fusion. This framework has been validated for its comprehensive performance advantage on five TCGA datasets.

</details>


### [96] [Libra: Efficient Resource Management for Agentic RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03077)
*Kaiwen Chen,Xin Tan,Jingzong Li,Hong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了Libra系统，针对智能体强化学习中长尾分布、训练与推演不对称性、轨迹长度漂移三大挑战，通过全局资源规划器和因果驱动的多级反馈队列调度器，在48块A800 GPU上实现了最高3.0倍的吞吐量提升和2.5倍的收敛加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为大语言模型后训练的标准范式，但在智能体RL场景中，推演阶段会调用工具生成长尾分布且非平稳的工作负载。这带来了三个核心挑战：少数长轨迹主导推演时间、推演与训练在计算模式和内存需求上存在强不对称性、策略演进导致轨迹长度分布漂移使静态资源划分逐渐失效。

Method: 提出Libra系统，包含两个核心机制：1）周期性全局资源规划器，联合优化推演和训练集群间的GPU分配，通过弹性混合池实现轻量级非阻塞的工作器重分配；2）因果驱动多级反馈队列（C-MLFQ）调度器，基于工具返回结果的因果信号将请求路由到异构推演桶，而非依赖脆弱的长度预测。

Result: 在48块A800 GPU上的评估表明，Libra相比基线方法实现了最高3.0倍的吞吐量提升，且在奖励收敛速度上提升了最高2.5倍。

Conclusion: Libra通过全局资源规划和因果驱动的调度策略，有效解决了智能体强化学习中的资源管理挑战，显著提升了系统吞吐量和训练效率，为大语言模型的智能体RL训练提供了高效的系统解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard post-training paradigm for large language models (LLMs), extending beyond preference alignment to complex reasoning and multi-turn agentic behaviors. In agentic RL, the rollout stage generates trajectories while invoking tools, producing long-tailed and non-stationary workloads that challenge conventional resource-management assumptions. Three fundamental challenges arise. First, due to the long-tail distribution, a small fraction of trajectories dominates rollout makespan. Second, rollout and training exhibit strong asymmetry in compute patterns, memory demands, and sensitivity to sequence length. Third, as the RL policy evolves, the trajectory-length distribution drifts over time, rendering any static resource split progressively suboptimal.
  We present Libra, which introduces two core mechanisms. The first is a periodic global resource planner that jointly optimizes GPU allocation across rollout and training clusters. It leverages an elastic hybrid pool to enable lightweight, non-blocking worker reallocation between stages. The second is a causality-driven multi-level feedback queue (C-MLFQ) scheduler, which routes requests to heterogeneous rollout buckets based on causal signals derived from tool-return outcomes, rather than relying on fragile length predictions. Evaluated on 48 A800 GPUs, Libra achieves up to 3.0$\times$ higher throughput and converges up to 2.5$\times$ faster in reward compared to the baselines.

</details>


### [97] [Auditable Climate Risk Intelligence from Fragmented ESG Data: Deterministic Orchestration and Imbalance-Aware Learning for Scope 1-3 Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02604)
*Karan Sehgal,Khawar Naveed Bhatti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可审计的ESG验证框架，整合单一事实来源编排、时序异常检测、不平衡感知集成学习和可解释性治理，并发布了合成的ESG验证基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESG和气候风险数据在异构的范围一、二、三报告环境中仍然呈碎片化分布，而传统验证流程缺乏来源感知的可审计性、隐藏漂移检测和面向可重复性的治理能力。

Method: 该框架整合了单一事实来源编排、时序漂移分析、基于SMOTE的稀有事件优化、集成学习、来源感知编排和基于TreeSHAP的可解释性技术，用于治理审查和审计重构。

Result: 通过分类指标（召回率、F1、ROC AUC）、校准指标（ECE、Brier分数）和治理导向的审计追踪完整性指标进行评估，框架在统计分类器、异常检测方法、时序预测基线和阈值系统对比中表现出色。

Conclusion: 该框架将ESG报告重新构建为支持可重复性、可解释性和操作可审计性的确定性气候风险治理基础设施。

Abstract: ESG and climate risk data remain fragmented across heterogeneous Scope 1, Scope 2, and Scope 3 reporting environments, while conventional validation pipelines lack provenance aware auditability, hidden drift detection, and reproducibility oriented governance. This paper proposes a deterministic climate risk intelligence framework integrating single source of truth orchestration, temporal anomaly detection, imbalance aware ensemble learning, and explainability oriented governance for auditable ESG validation. To support open reproducibility, we construct and release a synthetic ESG validation benchmark calibrated against publicly reported characteristics of the GHG Protocol, PCAF, and ISSB standards. The methodology incorporates temporal drift analysis, SMOTE based rare event optimization, ensemble learning, provenance aware orchestration, and TreeSHAP based interpretability for governance inspection and audit reconstruction. We evaluate the framework against statistical classifiers, anomaly detection methods, temporal forecasting baselines, and a threshold based system using classification metrics (recall, F1, ROC AUC), calibration metrics (ECE, Brier score), and a governance oriented audit trace completeness metric measuring the fraction of flagged anomalies for which a deterministic source to escalation provenance chain can be reconstructed. Results are reported as mean and standard deviation across stratified five fold cross validation with paired significance testing. The framework reframes ESG reporting toward deterministic climate risk governance infrastructure supporting reproducibility, explainability, and operational auditability.

</details>


### [98] [MOSAIC: Efficient Mixture-of-Agent Scheduling via Adaptive Aggregation and Inference Concurrency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03014)
*Saptarshi Mitra,Yifan Zhang,Rachid Karami,Phyo Pyae Moe Aung,Nazmul Takbir,Sreetama Sarkar,Souvik Kundu,Sitao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MOSAIC调度框架，通过整数线性规划优化专家放置与提示分配，并利用置信度自适应聚合策略，在4-GPU系统上实现了最高2.3倍的端到端加速且精度损失不超过0.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixture-of-Agents（MoA）系统通过将查询路由至多个专家LLM并聚合输出来提升推理准确性，但在有限GPU资源上执行时存在瓶颈：基于技能的路由导致专家需求倾斜，指令调优LLM与长推理模型的结合导致生成长度极端变化，传统调度策略因负载不均而造成严重GPU空闲与吞吐量崩溃。

Method: 提出MOSAIC调度框架，包含两大核心机制：（1）基于整数线性规划（ILP）的调度器，联合优化专家放置与每worker提示分配，将推理专家跨worker复制并固定轻量级专家；（2）置信度感知自适应聚合，利用专家间一致性跳过重型最终聚合器LLM来处理共识查询。

Result: 在4-GPU系统上，相比基线调度器，MOSAIC实现了最高2.5倍专家阶段加速、4.23倍聚合器阶段加速以及1.7~2.3倍端到端加速，同时精度损失控制在0.1个百分点以内。

Conclusion: MOSAIC有效解决了MoA工作负载在有限GPU资源下的调度瓶颈，通过联合优化与自适应聚合显著提升了系统吞吐量，为资源受限场景下的多专家LLM推理提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) systems improve reasoning accuracy by routing each query to multiple expert LLMs and aggregating their outputs. Efficiently executing this workload on limited GPU resources has bottlenecks. Skill-based routing creates skewed expert demand, and combining instruction-tuned LLMs with long-reasoning models results in extreme variability in generation lengths. Consequently, traditional scheduling strategies suffer from significant GPU idling and throughput collapse due to load imbalances. We present MOSAIC, a scheduling framework to accelerate MoA workloads. First, we formulate an Integer Linear Program (ILP) based scheduler that jointly optimizes expert placement and per-worker prompt assignment from offline-profiled costs, replicating reasoning experts across workers while pinning lightweight ones. Second, MOSAIC uses confidence-aware adaptive aggregation, leveraging inter-expert agreement to bypass the heavy final aggregator LLM for consensus queries. In our 4-GPU system, MOSAIC achieves up to 2.5x expert-stage, 4.23x aggregator-stage and 1.7~2.3x end-to-end speedups over the baseline scheduler, while matching accuracy within 0.1pp.

</details>


### [99] [Cross-Modal Contrastive Learning of ECG and Angiography Representations for Severe Stenosis Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02605)
*Nikola Cenikj,Özgün Turgut,Alexander Müller,Alexander Steger,Jan Kehrer,Marcus Brugger,Daniel Rueckert,Philip Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出StenCE预训练框架，首次实现从常规ECG信号中有效检测冠状动脉狭窄，在重度狭窄分类任务中达到高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冠状动脉狭窄诊断的金标准冠脉造影具有侵入性且资源消耗大，导致部分无症状患者漏诊。ECG检查快速、廉价、无创且普及率高，但尚未发现可靠的狭窄特异性信号，无法用于狭窄风险分层。

Method: 引入StenCE预训练框架，可直接从ECG信号中提取特征对患者进行分层。该框架在不同的狭窄严重程度阈值下进行评估，并应用于多种ECG疾病分类任务，验证其在不同ECG编码器上的泛化能力。

Result: 模型在各种ECG编码器上均表现出一致的性能提升，超越了先前工作。成功检测到ECG中用于狭窄诊断的信号，并首次在重度狭窄分类任务中实现了高性能表现。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用常规ECG进行冠状动脉狭窄筛查的可行性，为无症状患者的早期诊断提供了新的非侵入性解决方案，具有重要的临床应用价值。

Abstract: Coronary artery stenosis is a common cardiovascular disease, with severe, untreated cases posing significant risks of heart attack. Although coronary (X-ray) angiograms remain the standard for stenosis diagnosis, they are invasive, time- and resource-intensive, and therefore only performed on patients with a high probability of disease based on symptoms and prior clinical tests. However, a subset of patients, especially those without symptoms, may remain undiagnosed. Detecting indications of stenosis from ECGs, which are fast, cheap, non-invasive, and thus routinely acquired even in asymptomatic patients, would support early diagnosis. However, as no reliable stenosis-specific signal has been identified in ECGs, they can not currently be used for stenosis risk stratification. To address this, we introduce StenCE, a pretraining framework, allowing stratification of patients based on features derived directly from ECGs. Evaluations across varying stenosis severity thresholds and additional ECG disease classification tasks demonstrate consistent performance improvements across different ECG encoders, outperforming previous work. The obtained models successfully detect signals for stenosis diagnosis in ECGs and are the first to achieve high performance in severe stenosis classification. The source code is available at https://github.com/NikolaCenic/ecg-stenosis-cls.

</details>


### [100] [ReLoRA: Knowledge-Reusing Adaptation for Fast Rollout of Evolving LLM Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02606)
*Yang Xu,Zihuai Xu,Hongli Xu,Yunming Liao,Zhiwei Yao,Xitong Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReLoRA框架，用于解决大语言模型频繁更新导致原有LoRA适配器失效的问题，实现了快速恢复服务质量的适配器重适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型作为持续演进服务部署的场景中，基模型频繁更新会导致已部署的任务特定LoRA适配器失效。现有解决方案存在两难困境：从头重新训练每个适配器计算成本过高且延迟服务发布；而简单地将原适配器应用于更新后的基模型则因适配器与主干网络不兼容导致服务质量下降。

Method: ReLoRA包含两个关键优化步骤：1）自适应LoRA初始化，利用贝叶斯优化构建兼容性感知的初始化点，通过融合先前部署的任务适配器信息和基模型演化信息；2）带调度正则化的微调，首先通过强正则化快速引导适配器进入高质量区域，随后放松正则化进行任务特定细化。

Result: 实验表明，与基线方法相比，ReLoRA将就绪时间（time-to-readiness）减少高达8.9倍，准确率提升高达4.6%。

Conclusion: ReLoRA框架成功实现了在减少重适应开销的同时快速恢复服务质量，为管理大量下游模型服务的提供商提供了一种高效的适配器更新解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as continuously evolving services, where frequent base-model updates may invalidate previously deployed task-specific Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapters. For service providers managing numerous downstream model services, retraining each LoRA adapter from scratch for every updated base model is computationally prohibitive and delays service rollout. Meanwhile, the simpler alternative, i.e., naively applying the original LoRA adapter to the updated base model, often leads to degraded service quality due to adapter-backbone incompatibility. To address this problem, we propose ReLoRA, a knowledge-reusing re-adaptation framework that efficiently restores service-ready LoRA adapters for evolving LLM services while preserving or improving task performance. Specifically, ReLoRA comprises two key optimization steps: 1) Adaptive LoRA initialization leverages Bayesian optimization to construct a compatibility-aware starting point by fusing information from both the previously deployed task adapter and the base model's evolution; 2) Fine-tuning with scheduled regularization first rapidly steers the adapter to a high-quality region via strong regularization, followed by relaxed regularization for task-specific refinement. This design enables rapid service-quality recovery with reduced re-adaptation overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReLoRA reduces time-to-readiness by up to 8.9$\times$ and improves accuracy by up to 4.6\% compared to baselines.

</details>


### [101] [Geometry-Aware Tabular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02607)
*David Turtora Zagardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出几何感知表格扩散模型(GATD)，通过显式的成对角度和长度几何特征来捕获列间关系，在减少3.5倍参数量的同时实现了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格数据合成的扩散模型依赖隐式机制捕获列间关系，缺乏对列间依赖关系的显式建模。

Method: 提出几何感知表格扩散(GATD)，计算列值差异的成对角度和长度作为几何特征，将其作为扩散去噪器的输入和辅助监督目标，引入显式的关系监督。

Result: MLP实例在10个数据集上获得8/10 Shape、7/10 Trend和9/10下游效用指标的最佳性能，Shape和Trend误差分别降低27%和20%；参数量平均减少3.5倍(分类任务最高减少25倍)；方法可迁移至GNN和Transformer架构。

Conclusion: 消融实验证明监督信号(而非额外输入或容量)驱动性能提升，显式关系监督是表格扩散模型的可迁移归纳偏置。

Abstract: Tabular synthesis is critical for privacy-preserving sharing and augmentation, yet diffusion models rely on implicit mechanisms to capture inter-column relationships. We introduce Geometry-Aware Tabular Diffusion (GATD), which augments tabular diffusion denoisers with pairwise angles and lengths computed from column value differences and used as inputs and auxiliary targets. Our MLP instantiation achieves state-of-the-art benchmark performance while using 3.5x fewer parameters on average (up to 25x for classification tasks): on ten datasets, it wins 8/10 Shape, 7/10 Trend, and 9/10 downstream utility (F1/RMSE), reducing Shape and Trend error by 27% and 20%. Default loss weights transfer to GNN and Transformer denoisers, improving Shape on 27/30 and Trend on 25/30 architecture-dataset cells. A matched ablation shows supervision (not extra inputs or capacity) drives the gain. This shows explicit relational supervision is a portable inductive bias for tabular diffusion.

</details>


### [102] [Validation-Gated Multi-Agent Governance for Online Adaptation of Thermal-Hydraulic Surrogate Models under Operating-Regime Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03321)
*Doyeong Lim,Seungyoon Lee,In Cheol Bang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种受监护的持续自适应框架，通过多智能体协作和确定性门控机制，实现了热工水力代理模型的可审计演化，将预测误差降低了19.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能代理模型在热工水力预测中存在条件锁定问题——离线选择并冻结的模型一旦部署到预训练范围之外，性能会显著下降。现有方法缺乏在部署过程中安全、可控地适应新工况的机制。

Method: 开发了受监护的持续自适应框架，包含五个角色分离的智能体（监控、诊断、自适应、安全审计和编排器），通过诊断误差特征、优先选择候选模型族并审查晋升决策。采用确定性冠军-挑战者门控和后台影子学习机制保留模型替换的最终权威。从七个代理模型族中通过分组三折交叉验证筛选，选择时间傅里叶神经算子作为初始冠军模型。

Result: 在60秒历史到10秒轨迹预测任务上：静态部署的通道平均MAE为7.06，警告超标率为56.8%；基于规则的自适应MAE降至6.54；完全多智能体审查模式(MA-Full)达到最低平均误差5.72和35.8%超标率，相比静态部署改进19.0%。成对bootstrap置信区间排除了零假设，验证了从神经算子到Transformer和图神经网络的晋升是有效的。

Conclusion: 带有日志记录和门控控制的自适应机制能够支持可审计的代理模型演化，同时确定性门控机制保留了部署权威。该框架在确保安全性的前提下，有效解决了代理模型的条件锁定问题，为实验热工水力回路数据的在线预测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Artificial-intelligence surrogates can support second-by-second thermal-hydraulic forecasting, but models selected and frozen offline may become condition-locked once deployed outside their pretraining envelope. This study develops a guarded continual-adaptation framework for experimental thermal-hydraulic loop data in which role-separated agents - Monitor, Diagnosis, Adaptation, Safety-Auditor, and Orchestrator - diagnose error signatures, prioritize candidate model families, and review promotions, while deterministic champion-challenger gates and background shadow learning retain final authority over model replacement. Seven surrogate families were screened by blocked three-fold cross-validation, and a temporal Fourier neural operator was selected as the initial champion for 60-s-history-to-10-s-trajectory forecasting on two held-out transients, with three seeds per adaptive mode. Static deployment gave a channel-averaged MAE of 7.06 and a 56.8% warning-exceedance ratio; rule-based adaptation reduced MAE to 6.54, whereas shadow refresh alone remained close to Static. The MA-Full mode, in which the role-separated multi-agent council reviews every evaluated stream step, achieved the lowest mean error, 5.72, and 35.8% exceedance, corresponding to a 19.0% improvement over Static. Paired bootstrap intervals against Static excluded zero, although intervals among adaptive modes overlapped and the six paired units limit broad statistical claims. Validated promotions from the neural operator to Transformer and graph neural network indicate that logged, gate-controlled adaptation can support auditable surrogate evolution while deterministic gates retain deployment authority.

</details>


### [103] [Pruning Deep Neural Networks via the Marchenko--Pastur Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02608)
*Leonid Berlyand,Theo Bourdais,Houman Owhad,Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Marchenko-Pastur随机矩阵理论的深度神经网络剪枝方法，可在极低微调预算下保持模型精度，并在ImageNet数据集上实现了显著的稀疏加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度神经网络剪枝方法通常需要较长的剪枝后重优化流程，这限制了其实际应用。本研究旨在探索在极短的校准和微调预算下实现准确率保持的剪枝方法，避免长时间的后剪枝重优化流程。

Method: 采用Marchenko-Pastur (MP) 随机矩阵方法进行剪枝。理论部分提供确定性数据路径证书：若被移除组件R的传播logit效应较小，剪枝将降低弹性网络目标并保留样本。包含三种模型：(1)零预算情况下的完美剪枝；(2)修剪-恢复扩展模型；(3)附加L2正则化模型。在iid-Gaussian充分条件下，拟合的MP边缘σ+提供高概率的层级预算信号。

Result: 在ImageNet-1k数据集上：(1) ViT-B/16 2:4+ToMe在仅3个蒸馏周期后达到83.41% top-1准确率（比稠密模型下降1.70 pp），MAC减少59.81%，A40原生2:4后端加速1.388×，A100端点加速2.705×；(2) 结构化稀疏：ViT-B/16 6:12达83.74%，ViT-L/16 8:16达85.33%（下降0.51 pp），ConvNeXtV2-Base 12:16达86.35%（下降0.37 pp）；(3) CNNs：ResNet50 8:16达75.87%（下降0.26 pp），ResNet152d达81.33%（下降1.53 pp）并实现约50% MAC减少和1.62× A40加速。

Conclusion: MP随机矩阵方法为深度神经网络剪枝提供了理论基础和实际可行的解决方案，能够在极低的微调预算下实现准确的剪枝效果，并通过确定性证书保证剪枝质量，在各种架构上均展现出优秀的性能保持和加速效果。

Abstract: We study a Marchenko--Pastur (MP) random-matrix approach to pruning deep neural networks with very small post-pruning fine-tuning budgets. The main practical contribution is accuracy retention under short calibration and fine-tuning schedules, rather than a long post-pruning reoptimization pipeline. The theory gives deterministic data-path certificates: if the removed component $R$ has small propagated logit effect $L_s \| R ψ_1(s) \|_\infty$, pruning decreases an elastic-net objective and preserves samples whose dense margin exceeds twice the perturbation. The zero-budget case gives perfect pruning; a prune--restore extension models weight restoration inside a fixed sparse-execution pattern; and an additive $L_2$-regularized model shows admissible random-like components vanish at the training limit, with persistent spikes stabilizing as the MP bulk collapses. Under iid-Gaussian sufficient conditions, the fitted MP edge $σ_+$ gives a high-probability layerwise budget signal.
  On ImageNet-1k, after only three distillation epochs, ViT-B/16 $2{:}4{+}$ToMe reaches $83.41\%$ top-1 ($-1.70$ pp from dense) at $59.81\%$ sparse-execution MAC reduction, with $1.388\times$ best-observed A40 native-$2{:}4$ backend speedup for the same checkpoint and ToMe graph; a separate no-ToMe A100 endpoint gives $2.705\times$. At structured sparsity, ViT-B/16 $6{:}12$ reaches $83.74\%$, ViT-L/16 $8{:}16$ dense+permutation reaches $85.33\%$ ($-0.51$ pp), and ConvNeXtV2-Base $12{:}16$ reaches $86.35\%$ ($-0.37$ pp). For CNNs, ResNet50 $8{:}16$ dense+permutation reaches $75.87\%$ ($-0.26$ pp), and ResNet152d CAST-conv+permutation reaches $81.33\%$ ($-1.53$ pp) at ${\sim}50\%$ MAC accounting with a $1.62\times$ A40 im2col$+2{:}4$ sparse-GEMM audit.

</details>


### [104] [Building Better Activation Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02609)
*Jan Bauer,Celeste De Schamphelaere,Adam Karvonen,Niclas Luick,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文改进了Activation Oracles的训练机制并开源了首个综合评估套件AObench，尽管能力提升有限，但显著改善了输出质量并解决了评估难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的Activation Oracles在解释残差流激活时存在幻觉和模糊性等重要问题，且文本反转混淆使其难以评估，亟需改进训练方法和评估框架。

Method: 从四个方面改进AO训练机制：基于策略的rollouts训练、改进对话数据集、输入更多层以及改进注入公式；同时开发了首个综合评估套件AObench。

Result: 能力提升较为有限，但输出质量（quality of life）提升显著；AObench为AO质量评估提供了首个综合评估平台。

Conclusion: 本工作为改进AO及其他可扩展端到端可解释性范式中的模型奠定了基础，推动了可解释性研究的发展。

Abstract: Activation Oracles (AOs) are promising methods for interpreting residual stream activations. However, current AOs face important issues, such as hallucinations and vagueness. Additionally, text-inversion confounds make them hard to evaluate. To this end, we improve the Activation Oracle (AO) training regime in four ways: training on on-policy rollouts, improving the conversational dataset, feeding more layers and an improvement to the injection formula. The capability improvements are marginal, but quality of life improvements are quite substantial. In addition, we open source the first comprehensive evaluation suite for AO quality, which we call AObench. Overall, we hope that our work sets a foundation that helps improve AOs and other models in the paradigm of scalable, end-to-end interpretability.

</details>


### [105] [Hallucination Is Linearly Decodable from Mid-Layer Hidden States in Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02628)
*Aizierjiang Aiersilan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现开源LLM的隐藏状态中编码了线性可分的真实性信号，在单层中网络上使用线性探针可获得0.904-1.000 AUROC的高检测性能，显著优于采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究开源大语言模型是否在其隐藏状态中编码了线性可分的真实性信号，以及该信号在网络深度上的分布情况，以探索更高效准确的事实性检测方法。

Method: 在三个7B-8B指令微调模型上，从四个幻觉基准数据集提取各层隐藏状态，比较线性探针、MLP探针、INSIDE EigenScore、自一致性和注意力熵四种检测方法。

Result: 线性探针在单层中网络上达到0.904-1.000 AUROC，显著优于采样方法的0.541 AUROC；真实性信号近似线性，峰值探针层在模型家族间呈现一致分布模式；首块注意力熵在知识支撑场景下提供额外信号。

Conclusion: 开源LLM的隐藏状态中存在强线性真实性信号，峰值位于中网络层，线性探针是高效准确的检测方法，采样方法的低区分性源于结构性不匹配而非方法本身局限性。

Abstract: We investigate whether open-source LLMs encode a linearly separable truthfulness signal in their hidden states, and at which network depth this signal is strongest. Across three $7$B--$8$B instruction-tuned models (Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, Qwen2.5-7B) loaded in $4$-bit NF4 quantization, we extract per-layer hidden states on four hallucination benchmarks (TruthfulQA, HaluEval-QA, FEVER, and a controlled synthetic set) and compare four detection approaches: linear and MLP probes, INSIDE EigenScore, self-consistency, and attention entropy. A linear probe on a single mid-network layer achieves $0.904$--$1.000$ AUROC on held-out splits, while sampling-based detectors do not exceed $0.541$ AUROC under the same protocol. The truthfulness signal is approximately linear: MLP probes rarely surpass linear probes by more than $0.01$ AUROC. Peak probing layers fall in a consistent band across model families on natural-language benchmarks -- blocks~$13$--$18$ of~$32$ for Llama and Mistral, and blocks~$19$--$25$ of~$28$ for Qwen. First-block attention entropy provides a complementary signal in knowledge-grounded settings ($0.866$--$0.941$ AUROC on HaluEval-QA) at no additional inference cost. The low discriminability of sampling methods under this protocol reflects a structural mismatch between paired-label evaluation and the information these methods access, rather than an inherent limitation of those methods. Code and data are released for full reproducibility on a single $8$\,GB GPU.

</details>


### [106] [Regime-Arrival Uncertainty in Generalization Bounds under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02657)
*Prince Poudel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对马尔可夫切换分布偏移的框架，通过分解制度错配和制度敏感性来量化制度组成不匹配带来的额外风险，为制度感知模型提供了泛化边界理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准泛化边界假设训练和部署分布相同或静态，未考虑平静期与危机状态比例不同的制度切换环境。在现实应用中，分布偏移常呈现马尔可夫切换特性，需要量化制度组成不匹配带来的额外风险。

Method: 提出一个框架，在马尔可夫切换分布偏移下，通过精确分解分离制度错配和制度敏感性；利用谱间隙校正的有效样本量将边界扩展到β混合数据；在合成数据和25年全球股票指数数据上展示极小化极大下界。

Result: 提出的惩罚项是事后实现的泛化差距，而仅训练估计器未显示显著相关性；危机的特征几何可以被检测，但其时序到达无法预测；在合成数据和真实金融数据上验证了边界的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架不是预测机器，在制度变化罕见情况下预测未来制度组成仍是开放问题。研究揭示了制度感知模型中可检测危机特征但难以预测其时序到达的局限性。

Abstract: The standard generalization bounds assume that the training and deployment distributions are the same, or are static, and don't consider regime switching environments where the ratio of calm vs crisis states is different. This paper proposes a framework that generalizes regime-aware models by quantifying the extra risk due to regime composition mismatch, when distribution shifts are Markov-switching. We obtain an exact decomposition, separating regime mismatch from regime sensitivity; we extend the bound to beta-mixing data using the effective sample size corrected for the spectral gap; and we show a minimax lower bound for synthetic data and on 25 years of global equity indices. The proposed penalty is an ex post realized generalization gap, whereas the training-only estimator does not show significant correlation: the feature geometry of crises can be detected, but not the temporal arrival. Thus, the framework is not a forecast machine. Forecasting the composition of the future regime is an open question in the rare cases of regime change.

</details>


### [107] [CL-DMDF:Dynamic Multimodal Data Fusion Model Based on Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02659)
*Dong Li,Lingling Zhang,Binghao Han,Linlin Ding,Yue Kou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对比学习的动态多模态数据融合模型（CL-DMDF），通过跨特征和模态维度的注意力机制、实体中心对比学习模块和自适应融合模块，有效解决了现实场景中模态缺失或不确定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态数据融合方法通常针对特定任务设计并假设模态完全可观察，但现实应用中常因各种因素导致模态输入不确定或缺失。部分传统模型过度关注缺失模态内的局部交互，忽视了多模态表示中嵌入的全局互补线索。

Method: 提出CL-DMDF模型，包含三个核心组件：（1）跨特征和模态维度的新型注意力机制，计算可靠的注意力分数；（2）实体中心对比学习模块，从实体特征构建基于中心的正样本以增强判别性学习；（3）自适应融合模块，提升动态融合策略的效率和准确性。

Result: 在三个数据集上进行的广泛实验表明，CL-DMDF在多种多模态融合任务中均表现出有效性。

Conclusion: CL-DMDF模型成功解决了现实场景中模态缺失或不确定的挑战，通过跨维度注意力机制和对比学习策略，有效捕捉了多模态表示中的全局互补信息，为多模态数据融合提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal data fusion involves integrating and analyzing information from multiple modalities to uncover latent correlations and complementary patterns, thereby enhancing data processing and decision-making. While existing methods for structured multimodal inputs are typically designed around specific tasks and assume fully observed modalities, real-world applications often suffer from uncertain or missing modality inputs due to various factors. Some traditional models overly emphasize local interactions within missing modalities, neglecting the global complementary cues embedded in multimodal representations. To overcome these limitations, we propose a Dynamic Multimodal Data Fusion model based on Contrastive Learning (CL-DMDF). CL-DMDF introduces a novel attention mechanism that operates across both feature and modality dimensions to compute reliable attention scores, effectively reflecting importance at each level. The CL-DMDF further incorporates an entity-centroid contrastive learning module that constructs centroid-based positive samples from entity features to enhance discriminative learning. Additionally, an adaptive fusion module is employed to improve the efficiency and accuracy of dynamic fusion strategies. Extensive experiments conducted on three datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the CL-DMDF across diverse multimodal fusion tasks.

</details>


### [108] [Improvise, Adapt, Overcome: An On-The-Fly Multifidelity Algorithm for Efficient Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02662)
*Vivin Vinod,Peter Zaspel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应多保真机器学习框架，通过动态查询各保真度的训练样本，自主确定训练数据集组成，相比单保真度方法降低30倍数据生成成本，比标准MFML提升5倍效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习加速了量子化学发展，但高保真训练数据的生成成本过高成为瓶颈。标准多保真机器学习(MFML)方法依赖预定义的缩放因子来确定稀疏数据比例，往往产生冗余的多保真数据，导致效率损失。

Method: 引入自适应即时多保真机器学习框架，通过动态查询每个保真度的训练样本，使模型在提升到更高成本参考计算之前，先在较低保真度上饱和模型精度，从而自主确定训练数据集组成。

Result: 在耦合簇能量和激发能等化学性质上验证了adaptive-MFML方法。数值实验表明，相比单保真方法降低数据生成成本高达30倍，比标准MFML提升高达5倍。

Conclusion: 通过减少数据冗余，建立了量子化学中可持续成本感知机器学习的高精度低成本途径。

Abstract: Machine learning has accelerated quantum chemistry but is hindered by the prohibitive cost of generating high fidelity training data. Multifidelity machine learning (MFML) mitigates this overhead by systematically combining abundant low fidelity data with sparse high fidelity data. In spite of its success, standard MFML schemes rely on pre-defined scaling factors to determine sparse data ratio across fidelities, often generating redundant multifidelity data resulting in a loss of efficiency. Here, we introduce an adaptive on-the-fly multifidelity framework for machine learning that autonomously determines training dataset composition. By dynamically querying training samples at each fidelity, the algorithm saturates model accuracy at lower fidelities before moving up to more expensive reference calculations. We benchmark the novel adaptive-MFML across diverse chemical properties including the computational chemistry gold standard coupled cluster energies, and the more chemically challenging excitation energies. In our numerical experiments we show that our adaptive algorithm reduces data generation costs by up to a factor of 30 compared to single fidelity methods and improves upon standard MFML by up to a factor of 5. The mitigation of data redundancy establishes a high-accuracy low-cost pathway for sustainable cost-aware machine learning in quantum chemistry.

</details>


### [109] [AdaWeather: Adaptively Mixing Probabilistic Weather Forecasts with Logarithmic Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02663)
*Saptarishi Dhanuka,Sarvesh Iyer,Manmeet Singh,Mihir More,Rushil Gupta,Dhruman Gupta,Parthasarathi Mukhopadhyay,Sandeep Juneja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AdaWeather框架，通过结合机器学习和专家混合方法自适应地融合多个概率天气预报，相比传统方法具有更优的理论遗憾界和预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习概率天气预报模型与数值天气预报性能相当，但没有任何单一模型能在时空维度上持续占优，模型性能高度依赖上下文，这促使研究者寻求能够鲁棒地结合多种预测的自适应方法。

Method: 引入AdaWeather自适应框架，将机器学习与专家混合方法相结合来融合多个概率天气预报；理论上将传统专家建议方法相对于事后最佳单一专家的遗憾分析，扩展至相对于事后最佳静态专家混合具有对数遗憾。

Result: 理论上证明了该方法相对于事后最佳静态专家混合具有对数遗憾；实证上针对温度预测任务验证了方法优于现有方法。

Conclusion: AdaWeather通过自适应融合多种概率预测，在提升预测性能和鲁棒性方面展现出优势，为组合天气预报提供了新的理论保证和实用框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning have produced probabilistic weather forecasting models comparable to state-of-the-art numerical weather predictors. But no model consistently dominates spatio-temporally, and relative performance is highly context-dependent. This motivates adaptive methods for combining multiple forecasts to obtain improvements and robustness. While combined forecasts have been proposed in the literature, these are achieved either through supervised learning or through prediction with expert advice methods. We introduce AdaWeather, an adaptive framework that combines many probabilistic forecasts using both machine learning as well as mixture of experts to arrive at a unified improved probabilistic forecast. While traditional expert methods develop the regret bounds with respect to the best single expert in hindsight, we extend the algorithm and analysis to show our method has logarithmic regret compared to the best static mixture of experts in hindsight. Empirically, we focus on forecasting temperature, and observe improvements over existing methods.

</details>


### [110] [Anomalies in Multivariate Time Series Benchmarks Are Mostly Univariate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02670)
*Marc Pinet,Julien Cumin,Samuel Berlemont,Dominique Vaufreydaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现当前多变量时间序列异常检测基准数据集中的异常主要表现为单变量偏差，而非跨通道结构变化，因此无法有效评估跨通道建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来的多变量时间序列异常检测(MT-SAD)模型普遍采用跨通道建模，基于异常结构可能分布在多个通道的隐含假设。然而，这一假设缺乏系统性验证，研究旨在评估该假设在现有基准数据集上的有效性。

Method: 提出了一种分段诊断框架，对每个标注的异常进行分类：是否存在至少一个通道偏离其正常历史、跨通道相关结构是否改变，或两者兼具。同时引入补充指标量化异常片段中单变量偏差的时间步占比。在8个公开基准数据集上进行评估，并构建合成数据（相移正弦通道加共享噪声）验证框架的跨通道结构捕获能力。

Result: 在合理阈值范围内，跨通道破裂总是伴随单变量偏差出现，不会单独发生。在8个基准中的6个上，至少50%的标注异常片段在79%-100%的时间步上表现为单变量偏差，其中3个数据集达到100%。合成数据实验中，通道依赖(CD)模型成功利用跨通道信号，而通道独立(CI)模型失效。但在真实基准上，最新SOTA检测器的CI/CD对比显示CD建模未带来可测量的性能提升。

Conclusion: 当前MT-SAD基准数据集不适合验证跨通道建模能力，因为其异常主要是单变量性质的。研究呼吁开发结构更加多样化的评估数据集，以真正检验跨通道建模的有效性。

Abstract: Many recent multivariate time series anomaly detection (MT-SAD) models incorporate cross-channel modeling, under the implicit assumption that the structure of anomalies may be spread across multiple channels. We evaluate this assumption on eight widely used public benchmarks by introducing a per-segment diagnostic framework that flags, for each labeled anomaly, whether at least one channel deviates individually from its normal history, whether the cross-channel correlation structure changes, or both. The framework shows that no crosschannel rupture occurs without an accompanying univariate deviation across a range of reasonable thresholds. A complementary metric also reveals that on six of the eight benchmarks, at least half of the labeled anomaly segments deviate univariately on 79% to 100% of their timesteps, reaching 100% on three of these datasets. To verify that our framework captures cross-channel structure when present, we construct synthetic data of phase-shifted sinusoidal channels with shared noise. Each anomalous segment is altered through one of two channelwise corruptions that preserve the per-channel marginal distribution while breaking cross-channel structure, and our framework correctly characterizes these segments as cross-channel-only. On these data, channel-dependent (CD) models successfully exploit the cross-channel signal whereas channel-independent (CI) ones fail. The CI/CD comparison of a recent SOTA detector on real benchmarks further confirms that CD modeling brings no measurable gain. We conclude that current MTSAD benchmarks are unsuitable for validating cross-channel modeling capabilities, and we call for the development of more structurally diverse evaluation sets. The code for this study is publicly available.

</details>


### [111] [Aligning Data-Driven Predictors with Allocation: A Decision-Focused Approach to Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02671)
*Itai Zilberstein,Ioannis Anagnostides,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 传统生存分析模型虽在统计指标上表现优异，但用于器官分配决策时效果可能极差。本文提出基于NDCG的决策聚焦学习方法，在心脏移植数据上显著提升分配效益，每年可增加数万生命年。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习预测模型通常针对标准统计指标（如C-index）进行优化，但实际应用场景是算法决策任务，导致预测目标与决策目标错位。在器官分配等高风险领域，即使高精度的生存预测模型用于分配决策时也可能产生极差结果，无法保证比随机选择更好的效用。

Method: 提出决策聚焦学习方法，引入信息检索领域的主干指标NDCG（归一化折损累积增益）作为优化目标。理论证明NDCG能转化为分配性能保证。提出bootstrap方法优化现有生存模型的NDCG，并解决右删失数据在排序评估中的挑战。

Result: 在美国历史心脏移植数据上，方法将基线模型的NDCG提升50-100%，转化为每年数万额外生命年的增益。证明了NDCG在生存分析中具有理论效用保证。

Conclusion: 成功弥合了生存分析与策略优化的差距，将预测模型训练与下游决策任务对齐。框架有望推广到更多基于预测的决策场景，为机器学习在关键决策中的应用提供新范式。

Abstract: Machine learning predictors have become essential tools for guiding automated decision making. However, a major misalignment persists: predictive models are typically optimized in terms of standard statistical metrics in isolation from the algorithmic tasks they inform. We highlight this incongruity in the high-stakes domain of organ allocation by demonstrating that any algorithm relying on (even highly accurate) survival predictors optimized for standard metrics -- such as the Concordance index (C-index) -- can yield arbitrarily poor outcomes when used for allocation, failing to guarantee utility better than a uniform random selection. To bridge the gap between survival analysis and policy optimization, we introduce a decision-focused learning approach based on optimizing normalized discounted cumulative gain (NDCG), a mainstay metric in information retrieval. We establish the utility of NDCG in survival analysis by proving that it translates to guarantees on the performance of allocation. Empirically, we propose a bootstrapping approach to optimize the NDCG of existing survival models. Unlike prior work, we also address the challenge of right censorship when evaluating ranking. On historical heart transplant data from the US, our method dramatically boosts the NDCG of baseline models by 50-100%, which translates to tens of thousands of additional life years gained annually when deployed for transplant allocation. We anticipate that our framework will find broader applications in decision making with predictions.

</details>


### [112] [Before Fusion, Ask What to Keep: Contextual Calibration of Multimodal Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02679)
*Jiyuan Liu,Liangwei Nathan Zheng,Wei Emma Zhang,Xinpei Wang,Weitong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种紧凑的校准模块，在多模态融合前对各个模态表示进行自适应调整，通过跨模态支持与冲突信号抑制误导性成分、保留微弱但有用的证据，在五个基准测试上取得了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态系统中不同模态的贡献不均衡，某些模态对特定输入可能是有益的，但对其他输入可能产生干扰，且模态内部的局部特征响应可能与其他来源的证据相冲突。直接融合可能导致不可靠的模态信息干扰最终预测。

Method: 设计了一个紧凑的校准模块，在模态融合前对原始模态特征进行调整。该模块在摘要层面对各模态与其他模态进行比较，提取跨源支持和冲突线索，并将其转换为实例维度和特征维度的调制信号，对原始模态特征进行校准。模块设计为即插即用组件，可直接附加到不同的融合骨干网络中。

Result: 在情感理解、动作识别、音视频事件检测和音视频情感分类等五个基准测试上，该预融合校准策略在序列式和卷积式融合设置下均提升了性能。在模态移除、合成破坏、训练动态和特征级可视化等分析中，该方法减少了不可靠模态的干扰并产生更稳定的多模态优化。

Conclusion: 在多模态融合前对模态表示进行校准可以有效减少不可靠模态的干扰，使模型能够抑制误导性成分、保留微弱但有用的证据，并强调在当前多模态上下文中得到更好支持的响应，从而提升多模态系统的整体性能。

Abstract: Multimodal systems often benefit from combining information across language, sound, and visual streams, but this benefit is not guaranteed. A modality that is useful for one input may become distracting for another, and local feature responses within the same modality can disagree with evidence from other sources. This work investigates how to adjust multimodal representations before they are merged by a downstream predictor. We develop a compact calibration module that compares each modality with the others at the summary level, extracts cues of cross-source support and conflict, and converts these cues into instance-wise and dimension-wise modulation signals. The calibration is applied to the original modality features rather than to already fused representations, enabling the model to suppress misleading components, preserve weak but useful evidence, and emphasize responses that are better supported by the current multimodal context. The module is designed as a plug-in component and can be attached to different fusion backbones without changing their prediction heads. Across five benchmarks covering sentiment understanding, action recognition, audio-visual event detection, and audio-visual emotion classification, the proposed pre-combination calibration strategy improves performance under both sequence-based and convolutional fusion settings. Additional analyses under modality removal, synthetic corruption, training dynamics, and feature-level visualization show that calibrating signals before fusion can reduce interference from unreliable modalities and produce more stable multimodal optimization.

</details>


### [113] [Locality Does Not Imply Reachability: Boundary Repair in Block-Sparse Causal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02680)
*Zhibo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了固定块因果注意力中存在序列局部性与注意力图可达性不匹配的边界伪影问题，提出了相位条件覆盖分析框架和最小化的边界桥接注意力修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏因果注意力通常基于序列局部性假设：邻近token应易于访问，而远距离token可被丢弃以降低成本。然而，研究发现序列局部性与注意力图可达性之间存在不匹配：在固定块因果注意力中，两个相邻token在每一层深度上都可能在注意力图中断开连接。这种边界伪影需要被形式化和解决。

Method: 1) 通过结构依赖集形式化边界伪影，证明当所有注意力层使用相同固定块因果掩码且其余操作为逐位置操作时，目标表示只能依赖于其自身块前缀中的token；2) 推导相位条件覆盖函数，证明可达性依赖于源-目标距离和目标在块内的偏移量；3) 提出边界桥接注意力（Boundary Bridge Attention）作为构造性修复，保留固定块路径并在块边界附近添加零额外参数的辅助因果边。

Result: 1) 构建的K路边界复制分布中，架构级边界分离导致top-1准确率上界为1/K，期望交叉熵下界为log K；2) 覆盖定律能够预测稀疏模式何时失效、修复何时有效以及滑动窗口注意力与边界修复为何不可互换；3) 受控1024-token实验显示，增益集中在覆盖对齐的诊断任务上；4) 固定检查点的8K-token Qwen2.5-7B探针显示相同的覆盖不可比模式。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了一个理论指导的诊断框架，用于分析块稀疏因果注意力中的局部性-可达性不匹配问题，包含相位条件覆盖分析和最小构造性修复方案。研究为理解和改进稀疏注意力机制提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: Sparse causal attention is usually described by sequence locality: nearby tokens should remain easy to access, while distant tokens may be dropped to reduce cost. This paper studies a mismatch between sequence locality and attention-graph reachability. In fixed block causal attention, two adjacent tokens can be disconnected in the attention graph at every depth. We formalize this boundary artifact through structural dependency sets: if every attention layer uses the same fixed block causal mask and all remaining operations are positionwise, a target representation can depend only on tokens in its own block prefix. This yields an architecture-level boundary-copy separation for a constructed K-way boundary-copy distribution, with top-1 accuracy upper bound 1/K and expected cross-entropy lower bound log K. We then derive phase-conditioned coverage functions showing that reachability depends on both source-target distance and the target's offset within its block. These coverage laws predict when a sparse pattern should fail, when a repair can help, and why sliding-window attention and boundary repair are not interchangeable. Boundary Bridge Attention is treated as a constructive witness: it preserves the fixed block path and adds zero-additional-parameter auxiliary causal edges near block boundaries using shared projections. Controlled 1024-token experiments show that gains concentrate in coverage-aligned diagnostics. As secondary external-validity evidence, a fixed-checkpoint 8K-token Qwen2.5-7B probe shows the same coverage-incomparability pattern. The contribution is a theory-guided diagnostic framework for locality-reachability mismatch in block-sparse causal attention, together with phase-conditioned coverage analysis and a minimal constructive repair.

</details>


### [114] [Filter, Then Reweight: Rethinking Optimization Granularity in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02684)
*Yuying Li,Leqi Zheng,Yongzi Yu,Wenrui Zhou,Xuchang Zhong,Xing Hu,Jing Jin,Huangjie Yuan,Tao Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FiRe-OPD方法，通过在轨迹层面过滤低质量样本和在词元层面进行软重加权，实现了对大语言模型在策略蒸馏过程的双重粒度优化，在多个实验设置上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在策略蒸馏（OPD）研究正从全轨迹KL监督转向选择性训练范式，主要关注轨迹选择、信息性词元识别和可靠监督信号的选择。然而现有方法在优化粒度上仍有不足，缺乏对轨迹和词元两个层面的联合调整，且硬性词元选择会导致信息损失和优化不稳定。

Method: 提出FiRe-OPD（Filter, then Reweight）方法，包含两个阶段：首先在轨迹层面过滤掉低质量的rollout样本，然后在保留的轨迹内应用软重加权机制来强调信息性更强的词元。相比于硬性词元选择，软加权机制能有效缓解信息损失并增强优化稳定性，实现更细粒度的OPD优化。

Result: 在强到弱、单教师、多教师三种设置下验证了方法有效性。相比最近的词元级OPD方法，在强到弱设置下AIME 2024提升6.25分，在多教师设置下Miner任务提升18.81分，证明了方法的优越性。

Conclusion: FiRe-OPD通过轨迹过滤和词元软重加权两个阶段的协同作用，实现了更细粒度的优化，有效提升了在策略蒸馏的性能，为大语言模型蒸馏提供了新的优化思路。

Abstract: On-Policy distillation (OPD) in large language models is shifting from full-trace KL supervision toward more selective training paradigms. Recent OPD methods increasingly focus on selecting which trajectories to learn from, which tokens are most informative, and which supervision signals are most reliable. Motivated by this trend, we rethink optimization granularity of OPD and propose \fireicon\ FiRe-OPD (Filter, then Reweight), which jointly adjusts supervision signals at both trajectory and token levels. In details, FiRe-OPD first filters trajectories to remove low-quality rollout samples, and then applies soft reweighting within the retained trajectories to emphasize informative tokens. Compared with hard token selection, FiRe-OPD leverages a soft-weighting mechanism to effectively mitigate information loss and enhance optimization stability, thereby achieving finer-grained OPD optimization. We validate the effectiveness of FiRe-OPD across strong-to-weak, single-teacher, and multi-teacher settings, and demonstrate its superiority over recent token-level OPD methods ( (e.g., +6.25 on AIME 2024 in strong-to-weak, +18.81 on Miner in multi-teacher). Our code is available at https://github.com/YuYingLi0/FiRe-OPD.

</details>


### [115] [$Ψ$-Bench: Evaluating Persona-Sensitive Influencing in Persuasive Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02754)
*Peixuan Han,Hongyi Du,Jiayu Liu,Yihang Sun,Yutong Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ψ-Bench基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在现实对话中主动影响用户的能力，发现现有模型虽然能生成合理论点，但在说服力方面仍有较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前个性化语言代理研究主要将代理定位为用户偏好的被动响应者，缺乏主动与用户互动并提供指导的能力。为了系统评估这种主动个性化能力，需要建立专门的评估基准。

Method: 设计了包含三个现实说服场景的Ψ-Bench基准测试，通过对话历史生成明确的用户画像赋予模拟客户个性化特征，并在该基准上评估了10个前沿大语言模型的表现。

Result: 大多数模型能够产生连贯合理的论点，但即使是最先进的模型在说服力方面仍存在显著改进空间；提供客户画像信息可使平均性能提升18.24%，证明了用户特定信息对有效说服的重要性。

Conclusion: 个性化影响是评估和开发更主动个性化语言代理的一个具有挑战性但实用的研究方向，用户特定信息对于实现有效说服至关重要。

Abstract: Personalization is a crucial capability of modern language agents. However, current research primarily positions personalized agents as passive responders to user preferences, limiting their ability to interact with users and provide suggestions or guidance proactively. To systematically evaluate such proactive personalization in realistic interactions, we propose $Ψ$-Bench, a benchmark for assessing LLMs' ability to influence realistic users through conversation. We design three real-world interaction scenarios that involve persuasion in $Ψ$-Bench, and endow simulated clients with personal characteristics through explicit user profiles derived from dialogue histories. We evaluate 10 frontier LLMs on $Ψ$-Bench and find that while most models can produce coherent and reasonable arguments, even state-of-the-art models still leave considerable room for improvement in persuasion. We also find that providing access to client profiles yields an average performance gain of 18.24\%, highlighting the importance of user-specific information for effective persuasion. Overall, our work highlights persona-sensitive influencing as a challenging yet practical direction for evaluating and developing more proactive personalized LLM agents. Codes are available at: https://github.com/Hanpx20/Psi-Bench.

</details>


### [116] [Binary Road Surface Classification Using Machine Learning on Production Vehicle Signals During Cruising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02762)
*Vishal Hariharan,Salar Basiri,Kanwar Bharat Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对车辆巡航时传统基于动力学的摩擦估计方法失效的问题，提出基于机器学习的数据驱动框架，通过车辆动态信号统计特征实现路面状况（干/湿 vs 雪/冰）的二元分类，在巡航工况下成功识别路面状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时获取道路滑溜程度或峰值抓地力对车辆预警和干预控制系统至关重要。传统基于动力学的递归估计器通过计算滑移斜率来估计摩擦，但在车辆巡航且滑移量极小的情况下，受限于现有量产级传感器（如轮速传感器）无法准确测量或估计微小滑移，导致这些方法失效。

Method: 采用基于特征的框架和端到端数据驱动框架，利用滑动窗口方法将短时缓冲窗口内的轮速、轮扭矩、纵向加速度、转向角和横摆角速度等车辆动态信号批量输入机器学习模块，建立车辆动力学行为统计特征与路面状况之间的关联，进行路面状态的二元分类（抓地/干/湿 vs 滑溜/雪/冰）。

Result: 在公共道路数据上的验证结果表明，数据驱动方法即使在车辆巡航工况下也能正确识别路面状况，证明了该方法在轮胎和车辆动力学领域中构建准确的数据驱动摩擦相关状态估计器的潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动方法能够突破传统基于动力学方法的局限，在低滑移工况下实现路面状况的有效识别，为开发准确的摩擦相关状态估计器提供了新的技术路径，在轮胎和车辆动力学领域具有重要的应用前景。

Abstract: Knowledge of real-time road slipperiness, or even better, a refined estimate of peak grip potential, is a critical input for vehicle warning and intervention control systems. Typically, friction is estimated through dynamics-based recursive estimators by calculating the slip slope; however, its efficacy is heavily constrained by the vehicle dynamic scenario. When the vehicle is cruising and there is little to no slip, these methods become ineffective due to the inability of present-day production-grade sensors, such as wheel speed sensors, and methods to either measure or accurately estimate micro slip, which is crucial for distinguishing different surfaces. To address this challenge, the correlation between vehicle signals and road surface condition during cruising needs to be uncovered using machine learning. In this paper, a feature-based framework and an end-to-end data-driven framework are used to correlate the statistics of vehicle dynamics behavior with the condition of the road surface and perform binary classification into grip, dry or damp, and slip, snow or ice, conditions. A sliding-window approach is adopted to batch a short buffered window of wheel speeds, wheel torques, longitudinal acceleration, steering angle, and yaw rate, which are fed into a machine learning module for predicting the road state. Validation results on public-road data show scenarios where the data-driven method identifies the road surface correctly even during cruising, showing promise for accurate data-driven friction-related state estimators in the field of tire and vehicle dynamics.

</details>


### [117] [Representational Capacity: Geometric Limits on Feature Representation in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02765)
*Alexander Guha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于嵌入矩阵评估Transformer模型表征容量的框架，发现模型可分为两类，并推导出改进的容量公式，预测误差降低两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型维度是Transformer语言模型中的基础超参数，但其在特征表示几何限制中的作用尚未被充分探索。线性表示和叠加假设提出模型将特征编码为潜在空间中的近正交方向，但缺乏有效的方法来估计模型能支持多少这样的方向。

Method: 提出将嵌入矩阵作为潜在空间近正交性约束的可测量代理：通过成对余弦相似度分布中有意义的token关系与偶然相似性之间的边界，估计模型对完美正交性的接受偏差ε。基于此，推导出改进的容量公式，其中近正交方向的数量依赖于向量与维度的比率（k/d）而非原始计数。

Result: 在数十个开源模型上的应用揭示了两类模型：具有高ε值、嵌入缺乏近正交结构的模型，以及具有低ε值、保持近正交结构的模型。标准Johnson-Lindenstrauss引理严重低估了训练表示的打包效率，改进公式将预测误差降低了两个数量级且无需额外参数。表征容量对ε呈指数敏感，较大模型倾向于更严格的正交性约束而非最大化原始容量。

Conclusion: 定义了表征容量作为模型潜在空间中可用于特征和嵌入的可区分方向数量的上界，为理解模型维度如何限制特征表示能力提供了理论框架，揭示了模型在稳定性与容量之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Model dimension ($d_{model}$) is a fundamental hyperparameter in transformer language models, yet its role in setting the geometric limits of feature representation remains under-explored. Grounded in the Linear Representation and Superposition Hypotheses - which propose that models encode features as near-orthogonal directions in latent space - we develop a framework for estimating how many such directions a model can support. We first establish the embedding matrix as a measurable proxy for near-orthogonality constraints across the latent space: the boundary between meaningful token relationships and incidental similarity in the pairwise cosine similarity distribution gives a concrete estimate of the model's accepted deviation $\varepsilon$ from perfect orthogonality. Applying this metric across dozens of open-source models reveals two classes: models with high $\varepsilon$ whose embeddings lack near-orthogonal structure, and models with low $\varepsilon$ that maintain it. We then show that the standard Johnson-Lindenstrauss lemma greatly underestimates the packing efficiency of trained representations, and derive an adjusted capacity formula in which the number of near-orthogonal directions depends on the ratio of vectors to dimensions ($k/d$) rather than the raw count - a single modification that cuts prediction error by two orders of magnitude with no extra parameters. Combining these results, we define representational capacity as an upper bound on the number of distinguishable directions available for features and embeddings in a model's latent space. Capacity is exponentially sensitive to $\varepsilon$, and larger models favor tighter orthogonality constraints over maximizing raw capacity - a pattern compatible with several explanations (a stability-capacity trade-off, a ceiling on usable concepts, or confounds with model scale) that we leave to future work.

</details>


### [118] [QUIVER: Quantum-Informed Views for Enhanced Representations in Large ML Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02785)
*Aritra Bal,Michael Binder,Markus Klute,Benedikt Maier,Michael Spannowsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了QUIVER框架，通过量子Fisher视角增强经典机器学习模型，在QM9和JetClass数据集上证明了量子几何特征能够在容错量子硬件出现前为标准机器学习任务带来实际价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型机器学习模型能够从多模态输入中获益，因为不同模态提供了对同一示例的互补视角。然而，现有经典特征增强方法往往存在冗余性。作者受到量子信息理论启发，旨在利用变分量子电路训练过程中捕获的高阶相关性，构建一个真正互补而非冗余的新模态，以丰富经典数据驱动的特征表示。

Method: 提出QUIVER（量子信息视角增强表示）框架，核心是构建量子Fisher视角：通过变分量子电路（VQC）执行相同任务，提取量子Fisher信息矩阵作为几何驱动的、基独立的特征表示。该矩阵编码了学习量子状态流形的内在几何特性。与经典特征增强不同，这种由量子信息理论驱动的特征图难以经典模拟，能够揭示经典数据或模型容量难以学习到的统计结构。该框架通过针对性修改基础架构，可将量子Fisher视角融合到广泛的模型架构中。

Result: 在两个来自完全不同领域的基准数据集上验证了QUIVER的有效性：分子性质预测的QM9数据集，以及大型强子对撞机（LHC）中喷注风味预测的JetClass数据集。实验表明，QUIVER能够提升标准性能指标。更重要的是，证明了从模拟变分电路中提取的量子几何特征可以为标准机器学习任务提供可衡量的价值。

Conclusion: QUIVER展示了量子几何特征作为经典特征的互补模态而非冗余模态的潜力。该方法具有领域无关性，可应用于广泛的模型架构。研究成果表明，即使容错量子硬件尚未成熟，利用量子几何信息已能为传统机器学习任务带来实际增益，为量子-经典混合机器学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large machine learning models benefit substantially from multimodal inputs that provide a complementary view of the same example. We introduce QUIVER (QUantum-Informed Views for Enhanced Representations, a paradigm that enriches classical data-driven features with a quantum Fisher view: a geometrically motivated, basis-independent summary of higher-order correlations captured by a variational quantum circuit (VQC) trained to perform the same task. Unlike classical feature augmentation, the quantum Fisher information matrix encodes the intrinsic geometry of the learned quantum state manifold. While this feature map, motivated by quantum information theory, is ordinarily non-trivial to model classically, it can surface statistical structure that additional classical data or model capacity finds difficult to learn. This makes the quantum Fisher view a genuinely complementary modality rather than a redundant one. We demonstrate that QUIVER improves standard performance metrics on two benchmark datasets from very different fields: QM9 for predicting molecule properties, and JetClass for predicting jet flavor at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC). The core contribution, however, is domain-agnostic: the quantum Fisher view can be fused into a broad class of model architectures via targeted modifications to the base architecture, to incorporate information about the quantum geometry of the problem. These results demonstrate that quantum-geometric features, extracted from simulated variational circuits, can deliver measurable value for standard machine learning tasks, well before the advent of fault-tolerant quantum hardware.

</details>


### [119] [Qift: Shift-Friendly No-Zero W2 Post-Training Quantization for Rotated W2A4/KV4 LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02823)
*Chi-Wei Huang,Chia-Chi Tsai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在激进量化设置下标准W2量化失败的问题，提出了Qift——一种基于Hadamard旋转的无零点固定2比特量化方案，显著改善了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的2比特权重量化（W2）在激进的W2A4/KV4设置下经常失败。研究者发现这不仅是比特宽度问题，更是重构层级的问题。通过分析发现，预训练权重已经接近零中心分布，而Hadamard旋转主要使其标准化形状高斯化。

Method: 提出Qift方法：一种固定的无零点W2层级集合，专为旋转后的W2A4/KV4推理设计。主要层级集合为{±0.5, ±1.5}（等价于半尺度重参数化下的{±1, ±3}），并提供幂次方变体{±1, ±4}。该方法无需训练、无需学习码本、无需分组网格、无需零点，仅保留标准的逐通道缩放。通过尺度不变比率分析确定了有效的内外质心比率范围为0.25到0.33。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B的所有224个线性模块上验证表明：1）无零点层级集合一致改善了纯W2A4困惑度、L层混合W2/W4困惑度、下游准确性和GPTQ残差行为；2）在L=16混合精度下，大幅缩小了与W3A4的差距，同时保持一半的transformer层处于2比特精度。

Conclusion: Qift为W2A4量化提供了一个简单、源感知且部署友好的解决方案，作为复杂学习型W2码本的替代方案。该工作揭示了量化层级几何结构的重要性，证明了理解源分布特性对于设计有效量化方案的必要性。

Abstract: Two-bit weight quantization is attractive for memory-efficient LLM inference, but the standard W2 level set {-2,-1,0,+1} often collapses under aggressive W2A4/KV4 settings. We study the scalar level-set geometry of two-bit weights in a Hadamard-rotated quantization pipeline. Conventional asymmetric W2 substantially improves over the standard level set, indicating that W2A4 failure is not only a bit-width problem but also a reconstruction-level problem. Across all 224 linear modules in each of LLaMA-2-7B and LLaMA-3.1-8B, pretrained weights are already nearly zero-centered, while Hadamard rotation primarily Gaussianizes their standardized shape: excess kurtosis and Q-Q error drop by orders of magnitude. Based on this approximate zero-centered Gaussian-like source model, we propose Qift, a fixed no-zero W2 level set for rotated W2A4/KV4 inference. The main level set is {+/-0.5, +/-1.5}, equivalently {+/-1, +/-3} under a half-scale reparameterization; a power-of-two variant uses {+/-1, +/-4} for sign-and-shift decoded weight application. Qift redesigns the fixed two-bit code-to-level mapping and is training-free, learned-codebook-free, group-grid-free, and zero-point-free, retaining the standard per-channel scale. A scale-invariant ratio analysis identifies an effective inner/outer centroid ratio range of 0.25 to 0.33, explaining why mirror no-zero (MNZ), Lloyd, NF2, and PoT-MNZ perform well while {+/-1, +/-2} does not. On both models, the no-zero level sets consistently improve pure W2A4 perplexity, L-layer mixed W2/W4 perplexity, downstream accuracy, and GPTQ residual behavior over the standard W2 level set. At L=16 mixed precision, they substantially narrow the gap to W3A4 while keeping half of the transformer layers at two-bit precision, giving a simple, source-aware, and deployment-friendly alternative to more complex learned W2 codebooks.

</details>


### [120] [Mitigating Spurious Correlations with Memorization-Guided Dataset De-Biasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02830)
*Arda Fazla,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段样本评分方法，通过解耦核心特征与虚假特征的学习动态来缓解虚假相关性，在仅使用10%训练数据的情况下，标准ERM模型即可达到优于现有去偏差技术的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据集通常包含与目标标签无因果关系的虚假相关性，当此类相关性在训练样本中占主导地位时，模型倾向于依赖它们，导致对不呈现相同虚假模式的少数样本进行错误分类。现有的样本选择方法通常需要未知的组标签信息，且广泛使用的样本评分函数依赖于虚假特征，无法准确捕捉核心因果相关特征的重要性或难度。

Method: 开发一种两阶段样本评分函数，解耦核心特征和虚假特征的学习动态，并分别评估它们的难度。基于该评分指标，提出一种新算法，在有或无虚假相关性的情况下，识别并优先选择信息量大的样本。

Result: 在选定的样本上训练的标准ERM模型，在仅使用原始训练数据10%的情况下，性能优于最先进的去偏差技术。广泛的实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段样本评分方法能够有效缓解虚假相关性问题，无需组标签信息即可识别并优先选择信息量大的样本，显著提升了模型的泛化能力和分类准确性。

Abstract: Real-world datasets often contain spurious correlations that are not causally related to the target label. When such correlations dominate the majority of training samples, models tend to rely on them, leading to misclassification of minority samples that do not exhibit the same spurious patterns. While a potential approach is to select subsets of data to better represent the minority samples, this may require access to group labels, which are typically unknown. Furthermore, as we demonstrate, widely used sample scoring functions in the invariant subset or coreset selection literature largely depend on spurious features and therefore fail to accurately capture the importance or difficulty of core, causally relevant features. Accordingly, we propose to mitigate spurious correlations by developing a two-stage sample scoring function that disentangles the learning dynamics of core and spurious features and evaluates their difficulty separately. Based on our proposed metric, we introduce a new algorithm to find and prioritize informative samples both with and without spurious correlations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that a standard ERM model trained on our selected samples achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art debiasing techniques, while requiring as little as 10\% of the original training data.

</details>


### [121] [Learning Coherent Representations: A Topological Approach to Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02841)
*Sigurd Gaukstad,Melvin Vaupel,Valdemar Kargård Olsen,Erik Hermansen,Benjamin Dunn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"coherence"的几何属性，用于约束神经网络学习到的特征表示具有空间连续性和可解释性，并设计了可微的目标函数Coh来优化这一属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络学习到的特征往往缺乏可解释性，单个神经元可能对不相关且分散的输入产生响应。受大脑中网格细胞和头方向细胞等神经编码机制启发，作者希望学习到具有几何连续性的特征表示。

Method: 定义了coherence属性，要求矩阵中每个样本关注几何上聚类的特征、每个特征被几何上连续的样本所需要。证明了coherent矩阵能保证样本空间和特征空间具有兼容的拓扑结构。基于Fréchet方差设计了可微分的目标函数Coh，在训练过程中强制实施coherence约束。

Result: 在合成数据、旋转MNIST数据集的自编码器实验以及BERT的语言数据token嵌入实验中验证了Coh的有效性。实验表明coherence约束能够产生几何上连续且可解释的特征表示，例如当数据位于圆形流形上时，coherent特征会将圆形分割为连续的弧段。

Conclusion: 与稀疏性约束不同，coherence不限制特征激活的样本数量，而是约束激活样本的几何连续性。这种方法不仅产生了可解释的单个特征，还构建了可解释的特征空间，为神经网络的可解释性研究提供了新的理论框架和实践方法。

Abstract: Deep neural networks learn representations where individual features often lack interpretable meaning; a single neuron may activate for scattered, unrelated inputs. We introduce coherence, a geometric property inspired by neural coding in the brain, where neurons like grid cells and head direction cells respond to contiguous regions of state space. A non-negative matrix is coherent if each row (sample) attends to geometrically clustered columns (features) and vice versa, and in addition every sample is well described by some feature and every feature is needed by some sample. We prove that coherent matrices induce a bounded interleaving between the Vietoris-Rips filtrations of samples and features, guaranteeing that both spaces share compatible topological structure. This geometric constraint facilitates interpretability. For example, if data lies on a circle, coherent features must tile that circle into contiguous arcs. We introduce Coh, a differentiable objective function based on Fréchet variance that enforces coherence during training. Unlike sparsity, which bounds how many samples a feature activates on, coherence bounds which samples, requiring geometric connectivity rather than only rarity. This yields not just interpretable features but an interpretable feature space. We validate Coh in an auto-encoder using synthetic and rotated MNIST datasets and in a token embedding of BERT using language data.

</details>


### [122] [Spectral-Progressive Thought Flow for Lightweight Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02842)
*Yixian Shen,Zhiheng Yang,Qi Bi,Changshuo Wang,Shuai Wang,Jia-Hong Huang,George Floros,Prayag Tiwari,Anuj Pathania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecFlow框架，通过在固定大小的离散余弦空间中表示视觉思维，解决了多模态空间推理中计算和内存开销大的问题，在保持性能的同时将计算和KV缓存成本降低高达2.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态空间推理方法依赖于长链条的中间文本和视觉思维，累积的视觉token和密集的跨模态注意力会导致巨大的计算和内存开销，限制了推理的效率和可扩展性。

Method: 提出Spectral-Progressive Thought Flow (SpecFlow)框架：(1)利用离散余弦变换的能量紧凑特性，将中间视觉思维表示在固定大小的离散余弦空间中，保留全局布局和关系结构，仅在需要高精度时引入高频细节；(2)采用无分类器引导，使自回归文本思维引导视觉工作空间的流式更新，无需扩展上下文；(3)维护有界的视觉工作空间，其更新仅依赖当前视觉状态和累积文本轨迹。

Result: 实验结果表明，SpecFlow在推理性能上达到竞争性或更优水平，同时将计算成本和KV缓存成本降低高达2.1倍，实现了与推理深度无关的稳定延迟和内存使用。

Conclusion: SpecFlow成功实现了轻量级多模态空间推理，通过频谱表示和有界视觉工作空间的设计，有效解决了长链推理中的计算和内存瓶颈问题，为长时域推理提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal spatial reasoning often relies on long chains of intermediate textual and visual thoughts, where accumulating visual tokens and dense cross-modal attention incur substantial computation and memory overhead. To address this challenge, we propose Spectral-Progressive Thought Flow (SpecFlow), a novel lightweight multimodal spatial reasoning framework that represents intermediate visual thoughts in a fixed-size discrete cosine space. By exploiting strong energy compaction, SpecFlow preserves global layout and relational structure while introducing high-frequency details only when increased spatial precision is required. To align visual state evolution with linguistic intent, classifier-free guidance enables autoregressive textual thoughts to steer flow-based updates of the visual workspace/state without expanding the context. As a result, SpecFlow maintains a bounded visual workspace whose updates depend only on the current visual state and accumulated textual trace, enabling long-horizon inference with stable latency and memory usage independent of reasoning depth. Empirical results show that SpecFlow achieves competitive or superior reasoning performance while reducing computation and KV cache costs by up to 2.1 times.

</details>


### [123] [A Systematic Evaluation of Current Architectures in Wind Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02849)
*Vinicius Bortolini,Gilson Adamczuk Oliveira,Erick Oliveira Rodrigues,Matheus Henrique Dal Molin Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述探讨了区间风速预测的混合方法，发现结合模态分解技术（如VMD、EEMD）的混合模型能显著提高预测精度和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区间风速预测对于将风能高效集成到电力系统中至关重要，因为它能够解释风资源的固有不确定性。目前缺乏对该领域混合方法的系统性综述研究。
