Method: 采用潜在狄利克雷分配（LDA）进行主题建模以指导论文选择；重点分析了结合深度学习、模态分解和统计方法的混合预测方法；区间构建主要采用双模型策略，独立预测上下界。

Result: 集成VMD和EEMD等分解技术的混合模型通过在不牺牲覆盖率的情况下缩小预测区间，提高了预测精度和可靠性；大多数研究采用双模型策略，使用LSTM或ELM等模型分别预测上下界；区间质量通过平衡覆盖率和区间宽度的指标进行评估。

Conclusion: 区间预测对风能运营具有重要价值，但面临评估指标缺乏标准化、计算复杂度高和实际验证有限等挑战。研究为提高模型鲁棒性和决策能力提供了见解。

Abstract: Interval wind speed forecasting is essential for the efficient integration of wind energy into power systems, as it accounts for the inherent uncertainty of wind resources. This study presents a systematic literature review focused on hybrid approaches to interval forecasting of wind generation, exploring the combination of deep learning, modal decomposition, and statistical methods. To guide the paper selection, Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) was applied for topic modeling, enabling the identification of patterns and research trends. The findings emphasize that integrating hybrid models with decomposition techniques-such as Variational Mode Decomposition (VMD) and Ensemble Empirical Mode Decomposition (EEMD)-enhances forecast accuracy and reliability by narrowing prediction intervals without compromising coverage. Regarding interval construction, most studies adopt a dual-model strategy, independently forecasting the lower and upper bounds. Input data are commonly decomposed using techniques like EMD, EEMD, or VMD, which extract frequency-based components. These components serve as inputs to models such as LSTM or ELM, trained separately for each bound. This approach allows for targeted modeling of uncertainty, improving flexibility and precision, Interval quality is typically evaluated through metrics that balance coverage and interval width. The review also highlights challenges, including the lack of standardized evaluation metrics, computational complexity, and limited real-world validation. Overall, the study reinforces the value of interval forecasting for wind energy operations and offers insights for advancing model robustness and decision-making.

</details>


### [124] [RESCAST-100K: A Comprehensive Dataset for Cross-Domain Residential Load and Indoor Temperature Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02852)
*Jainam Dhruva,Yousaf Raza,A. B. Siddique,Simone Silvestri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RESCAST-100K，一个大规模住宅能源预测基准数据集，包含约10万户美国住宅的模拟数据和5个真实数据集，支持跨领域泛化、迁移学习和零样本预测的系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅能源负荷和室内温度的准确短期预测对家庭能源管理系统、电网级需求响应和社区能效工作至关重要。领域自适应和迁移学习在解决住宅环境中常见的数据异构性和稀缺性问题上显示出潜力，但现有基准数据集存在目标覆盖范围窄、缺乏结构化跨领域评估支持等局限性，阻碍了研究进展。

Method: 构建了RESCAST-100K基准数据集，包含约10万户基于ResStock通过EnergyPlus模拟的美国住宅数据，提供15分钟分辨率时间序列，涵盖总负荷、HVAC负荷和室内温度三个耦合目标，配以天气数据、HVAC设定点和40多个静态建筑协变量。数据集提供配置驱动接口，沿地理、气候区、墙体结构和供暖设备等可解释轴实例化源域和目标域，支持可控域转移下的系统评估。同时整合5个真实住宅数据集，支持从仿真到真实的评估。对循环神经网络、注意力机制和MLP-mixer架构进行了零样本性能基准测试。

Result: 在跨领域、缺失输入条件和预测任务中，交叉注意力和MLP-mixer模型在域转移条件下持续优于循环神经网络和传统Transformer基线模型。基准测试结果表明不同架构在住宅能源预测的跨领域泛化能力上存在显著差异。

Conclusion: RESCAST-100K为机器学习和建筑分析社区提供了一个综合性基准平台，旨在推动家庭、社区和电网尺度的跨领域住宅预测研究发展，填补了现有数据集在结构化跨领域评估方面的空白。

Abstract: Accurate short-term forecasting of residential energy load and indoor temperature is essential for home energy management systems, grid-level demand response, and community energy efficiency efforts. Domain adaptation and transfer learning have shown promise for improving forecasting accuracy under data heterogeneity and scarcity commonly seen in residential settings. However, progress is limited by the lack of comprehensive residential datasets: existing benchmarks are narrow in target coverage and rarely support structured cross-domain evaluation. We introduce RESCAST-100K, a large-scale residential forecasting benchmark for studying cross-domain generalization. It provides a configuration-driven interface that instantiates source and target domains along interpretable axes, including geography, climate zone, wall construction, and heating equipment, enabling systematic evaluation of transfer learning, domain adaptation, and zero-shot generalization under controlled domain shifts. The benchmark covers approximately 100,000 EnergyPlus-simulated U.S. homes derived from ResStock, with 15-minute time series for three coupled targets per home: total load, HVAC load, and indoor temperature. These are paired with weather channels, HVAC setpoints, and over 40 static building covariates. RESCAST-100K also integrates five real-world residential datasets under a unified schema, supporting sim-to-real evaluation on the same tasks. We benchmark recurrent, attention-based, and MLP-mixer architectures for zero-shot performance across domains, missing-input conditions, and forecasting tasks. Cross-attention and MLP-mixer models consistently outperform recurrent and classical transformer baselines under domain shift. RESCAST-100K is intended to aid the machine learning and building analytics communities advance cross-domain residential forecasting at home, community, and grid scale.

</details>


### [125] [GRZO: Group-Relative Zeroth-Order Optimization for Large Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02857)
*Liyan Tan,Yequan Zhao,Yifan Yang,Ruijie Zhang,Xinling Yu,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRZO优化器，通过组相对归一化显著提升了零阶优化的性能，在降低内存占用的同时大幅提高了大型语言模型微调的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化是反向传播的内存高效替代方案，适用于大型语言模型微调，但其部署受到梯度估计高方差的限制，导致优化效果不佳。

Method: 提出GRZO（Group-Relative Zeroth-Order）优化器，每个小批量样本生成一个伪独立扰动，通过组相对归一化聚合每个样本的损失，在不增加前向计算成本且保持推理级内存的同时，将有效梯度方向数量从1提升到批大小。

Result: 理论证明GRZO方向无偏且方差随批大小比例收缩，获得比MeZO更紧的非凸收敛界。实验上，在Llama3-8B上平均准确率比MeZO提升+3.0，峰值GPU内存降低23%；作为MeZO核心的即插即用替代，将稀疏、低秩和量化零阶变体平均提升+6.0。

Conclusion: GRZO是一种高效且通用的零阶优化方法，有效解决了梯度估计方差大的问题，在多种模型和任务上显著提升了性能并降低了内存成本。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization is a memory-efficient alternative to backpropagation for fine-tuning large language models, but its deployment is limited by the high variance of gradient estimation. We propose GRZO, a Group-Relative Zeroth-Order optimizer that draws one pseudo-independent perturbation per mini-batch example and aggregates the per-example losses through group-relative normalization, raising the effective gradient-direction count from one to the batch size at no additional forward cost while preserving inference-level memory. We prove that GRZO is directionally unbiased with variance shrinking proportionally to the batch size, yielding a tighter nonconvex convergence bound than MeZO. Across RoBERTa-large, Llama3-8B, and OPT-13B over multiple tasks, GRZO improves average accuracy on Llama3-8B by $+3.0$ over MeZO at $23\%$ lower peak GPU memory; as a drop-in replacement for the MeZO core, it lifts sparse, low-rank, and quantized ZO variants by $+6.0$ on average.

</details>


### [126] [Forgetting is Not Erasure: Recovering Latent Knowledge via Transport Keys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02860)
*Archie Chaudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 挑战灾难性遗忘的传统观点，发现大部分遗忘源于内部阶段间的接口偏移而非特征永久丢失，通过轻量级传输键可以有效恢复性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为灾难性遗忘是由于模型在顺序训练中丢失了支持早期任务的特征表示。作者质疑这一观点的强形式，旨在探究遗忘是否真的是计算能力的永久性擦除，还是存在其他机制。

Method: 提出一种拼接评估协议，将更新后网络的早期计算与前驱网络的晚期计算相结合，并引入任务特定的紧凑传输键作为中介。传输键被设计为紧凑的接口对齐算子，可从小规模配对锚点激活中估计得出。

Result: 在Split CIFAR-100数据集上，ResNet风格网络在使用传输键后能够恢复大部分原始任务A的性能；在紧凑型Vision Transformer上也观察到类似的恢复模式。这表明顺序训练后，早期任务的相关计算并未被永久擦除。

Conclusion: 研究发现持续学习中大量表观遗忘可归因于内部阶段间的接口偏移，而非任务相关计算的永久性擦除。这表明持续学习不仅需要防止权重变化的方法，更需要更好的机制来索引和重新访问潜在计算。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often framed as a representational problem: after sequential training, a model appears to lose the features that supported performance on earlier tasks. We challenge the stronger form of this view. Across controlled continual-learning settings, we find that a significant portion of apparent forgetting can be attributed to interface drift between internal stages rather than permanent erasure of task-relevant computation. We study this phenomenon through a stitched evaluation protocol that combines early computation from a post-update network with late computation from its predecessor, optionally mediated by a compact, task-specific transport key. We describe transport keys at a systems level as compact interface-alignment operators estimated from a small set of paired anchor activations and evaluated through model stitching. On split CIFAR-100 with a ResNet-style network, transport keys recover most of the original Task A performance after sequential training on Task B. On a compact vision transformer, we observe a similar recovery pattern. These results suggest that continual learning may require better mechanisms for indexing and re-accessing latent computations, not only methods that prevent weight change.

</details>


### [127] [RRISE: Robust Radius Inference via a Surrogate Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02876)
*Jong-Ik Park,Shreyas Chaudhari,Carlee Joe-Wong,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RRISE框架通过学习代理模型将随机平滑认证压缩为单次前向传播，在保持认证精度的同时大幅提升效率，为重复部署场景提供了实用的认证鲁棒性解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机平滑（RS）通过平滑分类器提供架构无关的ℓ₂分类鲁棒性认证，但其依赖每个输入的蒙特卡洛（MC）采样，计算成本高昂，限制了其在实时系统中的应用。作者认为这种成本是结构性而非本质性的，可以通过在部署流中共享信息来显著降低。

Method: 提出RRISE框架：1）训练代理模型以拟合预计算的MC类别计数目标，使用软标签交叉熵损失；2）通过一次性保形校准步骤将代理预测转换为可证明保守的认证半径；3）实现部署可验证性：当校准半径为正时，代理预测可证明匹配平滑分类器的预测。

Result: 在图像分类基准测试中，RRISE在固定预算MC认证精度0.84个百分点范围内匹配性能，同时将每个查询的10⁴次噪声基模型评估替换为单次代理前向传播。在CIFAR-100和Tiny ImageNet上，RRISE实现了1.23到1.91倍的更高认证精度，而此前唯一的离线代理方法在这些数据集上失效。

Conclusion: RRISE成功将随机平滑认证的计算成本从结构性障碍转化为可控成本，在约10⁵次部署查询后回收MC训练成本，为重复部署设置中的认证鲁棒性建立了高效的实践路径。

Abstract: Randomized smoothing (RS) uses a smoothed classifier to provide architecture-agnostic certificates of $\ell_2$ classification robustness, but its dependence on per-input Monte Carlo (MC) sampling undermines its use in real-time systems. We argue that this cost is structural rather than fundamental, such that it can be significantly reduced by sharing information across the deployment stream. We introduce RRISE, an RS framework that compresses certification into a single forward pass through a learned surrogate. RRISE trains the surrogate against precomputed MC class-count targets via a soft-label cross-entropy loss and converts surrogate predictions into provably conservative certified radii through a one-time conformal calibration step. The resulting certificate is deployment-verifiable: whenever the calibrated radius is positive, the surrogate's prediction provably matches the smoothed classifier's and the smoothed classifier is constant on a ball of that radius around the input. Across image classification benchmarks, RRISE matches fixed-budget MC certified accuracy within $0.84$ percentage points while replacing up to $10^4$ noisy base-model evaluations per query with a single surrogate forward pass, recouping MC training cost after $\approx 10^5$ deployment queries. On CIFAR-100 and Tiny ImageNet, where the only prior offline-surrogate method collapses, RRISE achieves $1.23$ to $1.91\times$ higher certified accuracy, establishing efficient randomized smoothing as a practical path to certified robustness in repeated-deployment settings.

</details>


### [128] [Are we really tilting? The mechanics of reward guidance in flow and diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02884)
*Sanjit Dandapanthula,Nicholas M. Boffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了奖励引导扩散模型中“奖励黑客”现象的根源在于Doob h函数的有限粒子插件估计，提出了无需额外计算的闭式奖励阻尼方案来修正偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励引导算法在实践中容易遭遇“奖励黑客”问题，即模型过度优化奖励而偏离学习分布，但其根本原因尚不清楚，因此需要深入理解并解决该问题。

Method: 在高斯与高斯混合目标及二次奖励的简单设定下，以闭式分析Doob h函数的有限粒子插件估计，识别两种失效模式，并提出闭式奖励阻尼调度与best-of-n采样策略。

Result: 理论分析表明插件估计导致模内奖励黑客与模选择失败，所提奖励阻尼方案可有效修正模内偏差，best-of-n采样可补偿模选择失败。实验在高斯混合、2D棋盘及FLUX.1文本到图像生成中验证了理论洞见的实用性。

Conclusion: 奖励黑客源于插件估计的近似误差，而非奖励函数复杂性或训练隐式偏差；简单闭式修正即可显著改善生成质量与保真度。

Abstract: Reward guidance algorithms steer a learned generative process toward the reward-tilted measure at inference time. While empirically powerful, these methods are prone to reward hacking: the guided model over-optimizes the reward at the cost of fidelity to the learned distribution. Prior work has attributed this to the complexity of neural reward functions or implicit biases in diffusion training, but its fundamental origins remain poorly understood. We show that reward hacking arises from an approximation made in most practical implementations of reward-guided diffusion -- finite-particle plug-in estimation of the Doob h-function -- even in the simplest non-trivial settings of Gaussian and Gaussian mixture targets with quadratic rewards. In closed form, we isolate two distinct failure modes of the plug-in estimator: it leads to reward hacking within each mode and it cannot select high-reward modes. We propose a closed-form reward damping schedule that corrects the within-mode bias with no additional compute, and clarify the role of best-of-n sampling in compensating for the mode selection failure. Experiments on Gaussian mixture targets, a 2D checkerboard, and FLUX.1 text-to-image generation confirm that our theoretical insights carry over to practical settings.

</details>


### [129] [Scalable Uncertainty Quantification for Extreme Weather Forecasting via Empirical Neural Tangent Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02886)
*Jose Marie Antonio Miñoza,Rex Gregor Laylo,Sebastian C. Ibañez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NTK-UQ方法，为深度学习天气模型提供推理时不确定性量化，在90%覆盖率下比分裂共形预测区间锐度提升31-37%，并能随极端事件严重程度自适应调整区间宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习天气模型虽然预测精度媲美数值天气预报且速度快，但仅输出确定性预报而缺乏不确定性估计，这在极端天气事件的高风险决策中是关键缺陷。

Method: 提出基于神经正切核的不确定性量化方法（NTK-UQ），利用最后一层经验特征构建。理论分析揭示：（1）方差崩塌机制解释UQ失败条件——当特征空间有效秩接近截断秩时，GP校正项消耗几乎全部先验方差；（2）分解性能依赖极端天气的非高斯重尾结构，独立成分分析（ICA）利用高阶统计量分离重尾特征，优于仅捕获二阶方差的SVD。通过数据驱动的特征特征谱浓度比率选择规则自动选择ICA或SVD分解方法。该方法无需重训练，推理时仅需单次矩阵-向量乘积。

Result: 在四种评估架构上正确选择了最优分解方法。相比分裂共形预测（自然的后验基线），NTK-UQ在90%覆盖率下实现31-37%更锐度的预测区间，并独特地产生随极端事件严重程度扩展的自适应区间——这是共形预测在结构上无法实现的。光谱集中型架构（谱算子）需激进截断（k≤10），而基于注意力的模型可容忍全秩计算。

Conclusion: NTK-UQ框架成功填补了深度学习天气模型不确定性量化的空白，通过架构依赖性的理论分析和数据驱动的分解选择策略，实现了无需重训练的高效推理时不确定性估计，为极端天气事件的风险决策提供了关键支持。

Abstract: Deep learning weather models now match numerical weather prediction accuracy while running orders of magnitude faster, but produce deterministic forecasts without uncertainty estimates, a critical gap for high-stakes decisions during extreme weather events. This paper proposes Neural Tangent Kernel-based uncertainty quantification (NTK-UQ) using last-layer empirical features. Theoretical analysis predicts that UQ quality is architecture-dependent through two mechanisms. First, a variance collapse mechanism explains when UQ fails: when the eigenvalue truncation rank approaches the effective rank of the feature space, the GP correction term consumes nearly all prior variance, destroying discrimination between tropical cyclones and routine conditions; architectures with concentrated spectra (spectral operators) require aggressive truncation ($k \leq 10$), while attention-based models tolerate full-rank computation. Second, decomposition performance depends on the non-Gaussian, heavy-tailed structure of extreme weather: Independent Component Analysis exploits higher-order statistics (kurtosis, negentropy) to isolate heavy-tailed extreme-event features, achieving higher discrimination than singular value decomposition, which captures only second-order variance. A data-driven selection rule chooses ICA or SVD from the feature eigenspectrum concentration ratio, correctly prescribing the superior decomposition for all four evaluated architectures. Compared to split conformal prediction (the natural post-hoc baseline), NTK-UQ achieves 31--37\% sharper prediction intervals at 90\% coverage, and uniquely produces \emph{adaptive} intervals that scale with extreme event severity, which conformal prediction cannot achieve by construction. The framework requires no retraining; inference-time uncertainty requires only a single matrix-vector product per sample.

</details>


### [130] [A Nonmonotone Gradient-Based Algorithm for Symmetric Nonnegative Matrix Factorization and Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02887)
*Ryan Swart,Johannes Brust*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SNMPBB，首个应用于对称非负矩阵分解(Symmetric NMF)的非单调投影Barzilai-Borwein方法，显著提升了梯度算法的收敛速度和效果，并在图聚类和大规模问题中表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称非负矩阵分解在图聚类和机器学习中有广泛应用，但投影梯度方法在此问题上收敛缓慢，因此需要开发更高效的算法来提升性能。

Method: 提出了SNMPBB方法，将非单调投影Barzilai-Borwein方法首次适配到对称NMF；进一步扩展为Graph-SNMPBB（结合图拉普拉斯正则化）和LAI-SNMPBB（针对大规模低秩近似问题）；证明了所有变体的全局收敛性以及随机近似下Barzilai-Borwein曲率信息的保持。

Result: 在合成数据上，SNMPBB比SymANLS快6倍且残差相近，高秩时优势更明显；在6个真实聚类基准上，Graph-SNMPBB准确率匹配或超越SymANLS；在34个SuiteSparse矩阵上，LAI-SNMPBB在运行时间和残差质量上均优于LAI-SymPGNCG。

Conclusion: SNMPBB及其扩展版本证明了梯度算法在对称NMF中比之前认知的更有效，为图聚类和大规模矩阵分解问题提供了高效、收敛可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Symmetric nonnegative matrix factorization (Symmetric NMF) approximates a matrix as $WW^T$ with nonnegative rectangular factor $W$. It has broad applications in graph clustering and machine learning. In contrast to the NMF, projected gradient methods for the symmetric problem had been associated with slow convergence. To address this, we introduce SNMPBB, the first adaptation of nonmonotone projected Barzilai-Borwein methods to Symmetric NMF, demonstrating that gradient algorithms are significantly more effective than previously understood. We further extend SNMPBB to graph clustering using the graph Laplacian regularization (Graph-SNMPBB) and to large problems with low-rank approximations (LAI-SNMPBB). For all variants we prove global convergence to first-order stationary points and also that Barzilai-Borwein curvature information is preserved with randomized approximations. On synthetic data, SNMPBB achieves 6 times speedup over the alternative SymANLS for similar residuals, with advantages growing at higher ranks. Across six real-world clustering benchmarks, Graph-SNMPBB matches or exceeds SymANLS accuracy. Lastly, LAI-SNMPBB outperforms state-of-the-art LAI-SymPGNCG on 34 SuiteSparse matrices in both runtime and residual quality.

</details>


### [131] [Multi-Modal Machine Learning for Breast Cancer Recurrence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02892)
*Jiahao Shao,Xudong Wang,Anam Nawaz Khan,Christopher Brett,Xueping Li,Bing Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过整合治疗记录、病理报告和临床笔记等多模态临床数据，显著提升了乳腺癌复发预测的准确性，优于单一数据源方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌复发是幸存者长期死亡的主要原因，需要及时准确的风险评估来指导后续护理和治疗规划。传统预测模型通常仅限于结构化或非结构化数据，难以捕捉完整的临床背景。

Method: 整合多模态临床数据（治疗记录、病理报告、临床笔记），结合基于规则的正则表达式提取机制和严格的基于优先级的冲突协调策略，从自由文本病理叙述中恢复明确的肿瘤特征以增强结构化记录，并在多种机器学习模型上评估单一来源和多模态输入的性能。

Result: 多模态整合方法在预测准确性上始终优于单一模态方法，证明了多模态数据整合在乳腺癌复发预测中的附加价值。

Conclusion: 多模态临床数据整合能够有效提升乳腺癌复发预测性能，相比传统单一数据源方法具有显著优势，为临床决策提供更全面的支持。

Abstract: Breast cancer recurrence, a leading cause of long-term mortality among survivors, requires timely and accurate risk assessment to guide follow-up care and treatment planning. Traditional predictive models, often limited to either structured or unstructured data alone, struggle to capture the full clinical context. This study examines the impact of integrating multi-modal clinical data, including treatment records, pathology reports, and clinician notes, on recurrence prediction. By integrating a rule-based regular expression extraction mechanism with a rigorous precedence-based conflict reconciliation strategy, our approach effectively recovers definitive tumor characteristics from free-text pathology narratives to augment structured records. We also benchmark performance against commonly used feature sets from prior breast cancer studies to assess the added value of multi-modal integration. Single-source and multi-modal inputs are evaluated across a range of machine learning models. Results show that multi-modal integration consistently improves predictive accuracy compared to single-modal methods.

</details>


### [132] [Fast Unlearning at Scale via Margin Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02920)
*Federico Di Gennaro,Alexander Shevchenko,Fanny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MASC方法，通过在线停止规则和消除下游评估需求，显著降低了语言模型遗忘技术的计算成本，同时保持了竞争性的遗忘-保留权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型遗忘方法存在两个计算效率瓶颈：1）训练过程超出所需的遗忘-保留权衡点，造成不必要的计算开销；2）需要保存多个检查点并通过下游验证数据进行反复评估来选择最终模型，增加了存储和计算负担。这些限制严重影响了方法的可扩展性。

Method: 提出了MArgin Self-Correction (MASC)方法，核心机制包括：1）主动缩小原始下一个token与最可能替代词之间的logit差距；2）引入在线停止规则，当遗忘序列中足够大比例的token位置上该差距平均值足够小时，输出最终模型；3）完全无需下游评估数据和检查点选择过程。

Result: 在TOFU、MUSE News和MUSE Books三个基准数据集上，MASC以现有基线方法的一小部分计算成本实现了竞争性的遗忘-保留权衡。额外发现：随着模型参数规模增加，MASC和SimNPO方法的权衡表现均有所改善——遗忘指标保持相当水平的同时保留效用提升。

Conclusion: MASC成功解决了现有遗忘方法的可扩展性问题，通过在线停止机制避免了过度训练和检查点选择的开销，为大规模语言模型的高效遗忘提供了实用解决方案，且展现出良好的规模扩展性。

Abstract: Language-model unlearning updates a trained model to behave as if it had not seen selected training examples, while preserving utility and avoiding costly retraining. Existing approaches typically fine-tune the pretrained model with a fixed training budget and select the final model afterwards by evaluating several saved checkpoints on downstream validation data. Two sources of unnecessary computation limit scalability: training beyond the desired forget-retain trade-off, and checkpoint selection that requires extra storage and repeated evaluations. To address these limitations, we introduce MArgin Self-Correction (MASC), an efficient unlearning method with an online stopping rule that does not require downstream evaluation. Given a text sequence to be forgotten, MASC actively reduces the logit gap between the original next token and the most likely alternatives. It outputs a final model once this gap is small on average over a sufficiently large proportion of token positions across all forget sequences. On TOFU, MUSE News, and MUSE Books, MASC achieves a competitive forget-retain trade-off at a fraction of the computational cost of existing baselines. We further observe that as we increase model size (a.k.a. number of parameters), the trade-offs improve for both MASC and SimNPO -- the forget metrics remain comparable while retain utility increases.

</details>


### [133] [Hierarchical RBF-KAN and RBF-SKAN Architectures for Multidimensional Function Approximation and Random Field Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02936)
*Mingtao Xia,Qijing Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于径向基函数的分层Kolmogorov-Arnold神经网络架构（hierarchical RBF-KAN和RBF-SKAN），用于确定性函数逼近和随机场学习，理论上证明了其通用逼近能力并展示了缓解维数灾难的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高维确定性函数逼近和随机场模型学习问题，传统神经网络方法在处理高维输入时面临维数灾难的挑战，需要开发新的网络架构来有效降低逼近问题的有效维度。

Method: 提出了分层径向基函数Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（hierarchical RBF-KAN）用于多维确定性函数逼近，以及分层径向基函数随机Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（hierarchical RBF-SKAN）用于随机场学习，两者均采用径向基函数作为激活函数。

Result: 理论方面建立了两种架构的通用逼近结果，推导了分层RBF-KAN的定量逼近估计，证明了该框架通过降低逼近问题的有效维度，具有缓解高维函数学习中维数灾难的潜力；证明了分层RBF-SKAN可在Wasserstein-2度量下逼近随机场模型。实证方面验证了所提方法能有效学习多变量函数和随机场模型。

Conclusion: 提出的基于径向基函数的分层KAN架构在理论和实证层面均表现出良好的性能，能够有效处理高维函数逼近和随机场学习问题，为缓解维数灾难提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In this manuscript, we propose and analyze hierarchical Kolmogorov--Arnold neural network architectures employing radial basis functions as activation functions for approximating deterministic functions and random field models. Specifically, we develop a hierarchical radial-basis-function Kolmogorov--Arnold network (hierarchical RBF-KAN) for multidimensional deterministic function approximation and a hierarchical radial-basis-function stochastic Kolmogorov--Arnold network (hierarchical RBF-SKAN) for random field learning. From a theoretical perspective, we establish universal approximation results for both architectures. In particular, we derive quantitative approximation estimates for the hierarchical RBF-KAN, showing that the proposed framework has the potential to partially alleviate the curse of dimensionality in learning high-dimensional functions by reducing the effective dimensionality of the approximation problem. Furthermore, we show that the hierarchical RBF-SKAN can approximate random field models under the Wasserstein-2 metric. Empirically, we show that our proposed radial-basis-function-based neural network structure could effectively learn multivariate functions and random field models.

</details>


### [134] [ERP-XTTN: Interpretable Prototype-Guided Cross-Attention for Cross-Subject ERP Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02939)
*Charlotte Genevier Wyman,Leanne Hirshfield*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ERP-XTTN的跨注意力架构，通过原型路由实现对ERP信号的高精度、可解释且无需校准的跨被试验证，在多种ERP成分上展现出具有竞争力的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试的无校准脑机接口分类器在保持可解释性方面仍面临挑战，现有方法难以兼顾分类准确性与模型可解释性。本文旨在探索基于原型的跨注意力机制能否在部署兼容条件下提供竞争性强且可解释的ERP分类方案。

Method: 提出ERP-XTTN架构，利用仅查询-键的跨注意力将EEG信号块路由到固定的差分波原型；原型由训练折叠差分波极值自动生成；分类完全依赖注意力路由，注意力忠实性源于结构设计而非事后解释。采用因果滤波和LOSO（留一被试法）评估，对比EEGNet和xDAWN+RG。

Result: 在八个ERP成分上评估，最佳基线与ERP-XTTN的平均AUROC差距在3通道时为0.018、全导联时为0.034；差距主要源于相对EEGNet的时间灵活性损失和相对xDAWN+RG的空间利用损失。透明路由揭示了跨被试信号结构，假阳性更像真阳性，表明分类错误具有神经生理学可解释性。

Conclusion: ERP-XTTN在因果、无校准条件下跨多种ERP实现了良好泛化，最小导联配置下仅有较小的可解释性代价，为BCI分类器的设计提供了兼具精度与透明度的新思路。这也是首个基于ERP CORE的epoch级LOSO基准测试。

Abstract: Interpretable brain-computer interface classifiers that generalize across subjects without calibration remain an open challenge. We test whether prototype-based cross-attention can provide competitive, interpretable event-related potential (ERP) classification under deployment-compatible conditions. We propose ERP-XTTN, a cross-attention architecture that routes input EEG patches to fixed difference-wave prototypes via query-key-only cross-attention with no value projection, so classification depends entirely on attention routing and attention faithfulness is structural rather than post-hoc. Prototypes are derived automatically from extrema in the training-fold difference wave. We evaluate across three public sources (BNCI Horizon 2020, HRI Cursor, and ERP CORE) spanning eight ERP components (ERN, LRP, ErrP, N170, P300, N2pc, MMN, N400), using leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) evaluation with causal filtering at two channel counts (3-channel and full montage), against EEGNet and xDAWN with Riemannian geometry (xDAWN+RG). The mean gap between the best baseline and ERP-XTTN was .018 AUROC at 3 channels and .034 at full montage, arising from two largely distinct sources: a temporal-flexibility cost relative to EEGNet and a spatial-exploitation cost relative to xDAWN+RG, the latter driven by signal-to-noise ratio at full montage. Beyond accuracy, the transparent routing reveals cross-subject signal structure that black-box models cannot: false positives resembled true positives more than true negatives did, indicating that classification errors are neurophysiologically explicable. ERP-XTTN generalizes across diverse ERPs under causal, calibration-free conditions with a small interpretability cost at minimal montages. To our knowledge, this is the first epoch-level LOSO benchmark on ERP CORE.

</details>


### [135] [Outsmarting the Chameleon: Counterfactual Decoupling for Tactical OOD Shifts in Live Streaming Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02946)
*Yiran Qiao,Jing Chen,Jiaqi Xu,Yang Liu,Qiwei Zhong,Xiang Ao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LPCD框架，从潜在因果视角解决直播流中对抗性战术OOD偏移问题，通过建模潜在意图和叙述变体并强制潜在反事实一致性，实现稳健的风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直播流作为社交互动和数字商业的主要媒介，面临着恶意行为者通过不断重新设计叙述包装来规避检测的复杂风险。这种战术性OOD偏移暴露了现有OOD泛化范式的关键局限性：在意图-战术紧密耦合演化且原始级别反事实定义不清的情况下，现有方法的假设难以满足。

Method: 提出潜在预测反事实解耦(LPCD)即插即用框架：1) 在潜在层面对意图和叙述变化进行建模，实现对抗性战术重新包装下的反事实推理；2) 强制潜在反事实一致性，将风险预测锚定在因果稳定的恶意意图上；3) 推理时应用轻量级无参数校准，缓解战术引起的分布偏移。

Result: 在大规模工业数据集和在线生产流量上的大量实验表明，LPCD始终优于最先进的基线方法，验证了其在真实世界直播流中调节演化对抗性风险的有效性。

Conclusion: LPCD框架成功解决了直播流风险评估中的战术性OOD偏移挑战，通过潜在因果建模和反事实一致性约束，实现了对不断演化的对抗性风险的稳健检测，为实际工业应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Live streaming has emerged as a primary medium for social interaction and digital commerce, yet it is increasingly plagued by sophisticated risks. A fundamental challenge in this domain is \emph{tactical out-of-distribution (OOD) shift}: while malicious actors maintain stable underlying objectives, they continuously redesign narrative packaging to evade detection. Such adversarial shifts expose critical limitations of existing OOD generalization paradigms, whose assumptions are difficult to satisfy in the presence of tightly coupled intent-tactic evolution and ill-defined raw-level counterfactuals.
  In this paper, we tackle this issue from a \emph{latent causal} perspective and propose \underline{L}atent-\underline{P}redictive \underline{C}ounterfactual \underline{D}ecoupling~(LPCD), a plug-in framework for robust live streaming risk assessment. LPCD enables counterfactual reasoning under adversarial tactical re-packaging by modeling intent and narrative variation at the latent level, and enforces \emph{latent counterfactual consistency} to anchor risk prediction on causally stable malicious intent. At inference time, LPCD applies a lightweight, parameter-free calibration to further mitigate tactic-induced distribution shifts. Extensive experiments on large-scale industrial datasets and online production traffic demonstrate that LPCD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, validating its effectiveness in moderating evolving adversarial risks in real-world live streaming. The project page is available at https://qiaoyran.github.io/LiveStreamingRiskAssessment/.

</details>


### [136] [BYORn: Bootstrap Your Own Responses to Defend Large Vision-Language Models Against Backdoor Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02947)
*Ivan Sabolić,Marin Oršić,Josip Šarić,Sven Lončarić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BYORn框架，通过识别并动态替换语义不合理的 poisoned 目标响应，实现了对自回归视觉语言模型后门攻击的有效防御，同时保持了干净任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的监督微调范式在开放式生成设置中容易受到后门攻击，且现有防御方法无效；观察到被投毒的目标响应通常在给定图像-文本输入和预训练模型的情况下语义上不合理。

Method: 提出BYORn（Backdoor-robust fine-tuning framework），该方法识别语义不合理的响应并动态地用模型生成的替代响应替换它们，从而打破触发器与目标输出之间的相关性；其目标梯度对应于干净数据分布上总体风险上界的经验估计梯度。

Result: BYORn在保持干净任务性能的同时，持续提高了对后门攻击的鲁棒性，建立了泛化能力与攻击成功率之间的新的权衡前沿；即使面对专门设计的自适应攻击，BYORn仍然有效。

Conclusion: BYORn成功地在自回归视觉语言模型的微调过程中实现了对后门攻击的有效防御，在不牺牲模型正常性能的前提下显著提升了安全性，为该领域提供了新的防御范式。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning is the predominant approach for adapting autoregressive vision-language models to downstream tasks. Recent work has shown that this paradigm is highly vulnerable to backdoor attacks, and that existing defenses are ineffective in open-ended generation settings. In response, we propose BYORn, a backdoor-robust fine-tuning framework motivated by the observation that poisoned target responses are often semantically implausible given the corresponding image-text inputs and a pretrained model. BYORn identifies such misaligned responses and dynamically replaces them with alternative responses generated by the model, thereby breaking the correlation between triggers and target outputs. The resulting objective gradient corresponds to the gradient of the empirical estimate of the population risk upper bound over the clean data distribution. Empirically, BYORn consistently improves robustness to backdoor attacks while preserving clean-task performance, establishing a new trade-off frontier between generalization and attack success rate. Finally, we demonstrate that BYORn remains effective against adaptive attacks specifically designed to circumvent the proposed defense.

</details>


### [137] [From Non-Convex to Strongly Convex: Curvature-Adaptive FTPL for Online Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02948)
*Moses Charikar,Chirag Pabbaraju,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的FTPL算法，通过动态调整扰动尺度，在非凸在线优化中实现了曲率自适应，能够在未知曲率累积情况的前提下，从最坏情况下的O(√T)遗憾自动提升到曲率有利时的O(log T)遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线非凸优化工作虽然证明了FTPL可以达到最优的O(√T)遗憾，但无法利用目标函数的曲率信息来加速收敛。本文的动机是设计一种能够在非凸设置中实现曲率自适应的方法，使其在曲率累积充分时能获得更快的收敛速率，而无需预先知道曲率信息。

Method: 作者提出了一种改进的FTPL算法，核心是将标准FTPL的固定扰动尺度替换为时变尺度。该尺度仅基于历史信息通过简单的"跟随领导者"调节规则来确定，并证明这种调节规则在常数范围内可以与事后最优选择相竞争。算法能够利用累积曲率信息而无需预先知道其具体形式。

Result: 所提方法对任意非凸Lipschitz损失达到O(√T)遗憾，且随着累积曲率的增长而改善：在oracle调用足够精确且累积曲率线性增长（包括经典的强凸情形）时，可以达到O(log T)遗憾。同时，作者针对一维凸损失情形给出了匹配的下界，证明最坏情况非凸遗憾与曲率驱动的快速收敛之间存在本质的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将曲率自适应从凸在线优化扩展到了非凸在线优化领域，证明了FTPL在适当的动态扰动尺度下可以实现最优的曲率自适应性能。上下界结果的匹配表明，这种性能提升在理论上是最优的，揭示了非凸在线优化中曲率利用的本质界限。

Abstract: Curvature adaptivity is a classical theme in online optimization: for convex Lipschitz losses, adaptive methods interpolate between the optimal $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret for general convex losses and $O(\log T)$ regret under strong convexity. Recent work has shown that Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader (FTPL) achieves optimal $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret even for online non-convex Lipschitz losses, assuming access to an approximate offline-optimization oracle, but these guarantees do not exploit curvature. We show that FTPL can be made curvature-adaptive in the non-convex setting, without knowing in advance how curvature will accumulate over time. Our algorithm replaces the fixed perturbation scale of standard FTPL with a time-varying scale chosen using only past information. We give a simple follow-the-leader tuning rule for this scale and show that it competes, up to constants, with the best choice in hindsight. The resulting method achieves $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret for arbitrary non-convex Lipschitz losses and improves as cumulative curvature grows; with sufficiently accurate oracle calls, it achieves $O(\log T)$ regret when cumulative curvature grows linearly, which includes the classical strongly convex regime. We complement these upper bounds with matching lower bounds for prescribed cumulative-curvature sequences, already for one-dimensional convex losses, showing that the tradeoff between worst-case non-convex regret and curvature-driven fast rates is intrinsic.

</details>


### [138] [Gate AI: LLM Security Benchmark Evaluation Methodology and Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02959)
*Ryle Goehausen,Marcus Sousa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大语言模型提示注入和越狱检测器的评估框架，解决了现有评估中的两个系统性缺陷：每数据集阈值调优和未披露操作点。通过16个公共基准数据集（12,111个样本）上的5折交叉验证，采用统一的单一全局操作点，并配合一系列诊断测试来检验泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型提示注入和越狱检测器评估存在两个系统性缺陷：一是针对每个数据集进行阈值调优，二是未披露操作点（operating points），这些问题导致评估结果缺乏可比性和泛化性，无法真实反映检测器的实际性能。

Method: 提出一个评估框架：1）在16个公共基准数据集（12,111样本）上使用5折交叉验证；2）采用StratifiedKFold和StratifiedGroupKFold（基于父提示ID和MinHash+LSH近重复聚类）两种分层方式；3）在留出折上选择单一全局操作点（在FPR≤1%约束下最大化F1），统一应用于所有数据集；4）通过一系列诊断测试验证泛化性，包括留一数据集交叉验证、随机标签控制、对抗性验证、置换特征重要性、长度偏差相关性等；5）对于外部比较，重新调整检测器阈值以匹配竞争对手的假阳性率。

Result: 该方法确保了每个数据集的结果反映单一阈值而非针对每个基准的优化；通过并行StratifiedGroupKFold分析揭示了泄漏风险；大量诊断测试（大多数有定量通过阈值，其余有明确的失败模式）全面检验了检测器的泛化能力；外部对比在匹配操作点下进行，确保了公平性。

Conclusion: 该评估框架有效解决了现有评估中的系统性问题，通过单一全局操作点和全面的诊断测试，提供了更公平、更可靠、更能反映真实泛化性能的检测器评估方法，为检测器的比较和选择提供了可信的依据。

Abstract: Published evaluations of prompt-injection and jailbreak detectors for Large Language Models often suffer from two systematic weaknesses: per-dataset threshold tuning and undisclosed operating points. We describe an evaluation harness that addresses both. The detector under evaluation is scored across 16 public benchmarks (12,111 samples) using 5-fold cross-validation. StratifiedKFold (by row) is the headline pass; a parallel StratifiedGroupKFold pass over a composite key (parent-prompt id plus MinHash + LSH near-duplicate clusters at Jaccard $\gtrsim 0.8$) runs alongside it as a leakage-premium diagnostic. A single global operating point is selected on the held-out folds (max F1 subject to FPR $\leq 1\%$) and applied uniformly to every dataset, so per-dataset results reflect one threshold rather than per-benchmark optimisation. Generalisation is examined through a battery of diagnostics (leave-one-dataset-out cross-validation, a random-label control, adversarial validation, permutation feature importance, length-bias correlation, classifier-head agreement, cross-source near-duplicate detection, threshold transferability, train-vs-OOF agreement, and a paraphrase-invariance probe), most with a quantitative pass threshold and the remainder with a stated failure mode. For every external comparison, the detector's threshold is re-tuned to the competitor's published false-positive rate so head-to-head values are evaluated at matched operating points.

</details>


### [139] [KForge: LLM-Driven Cross-Platform Kernel Generation for AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02963)
*Taras Sereda,Burak Bartan,Ankita Nayak,Tom St. John,Natalie Serrino,Zain Asgar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了KForge框架，利用两个协作的LLM代理自动生成并优化跨平台的高性能计算内核，在NVIDIA和Intel硬件上分别实现了2.12%和5.13倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境的推理任务需要面向异构加速器，但为每个阶段编写高性能内核耗时、需要深厚的底层专业知识，且无法随内核复杂性增长而扩展。现有的LLM自动内核生成方法在底层代码生成和跨后端泛化方面仍面临挑战。

Method: 提出了KForge框架，采用迭代优化循环，由两个协作的LLM代理驱动：生成代理使用编译和正确性反馈生成并逐步优化内核；性能分析代理解读性能分析数据并发出推荐以指导下一轮合成。循环在功能传递（驱动候选内核达到正确性）和优化传递（缩小与手工优化基线的性能差距）之间交替。

Result: 在NVIDIA B200上，KForge在gpt-oss-20b推理速度基准测试中比TensorRT-LLM实现了2.12%的端到端吞吐量提升。在Intel Arc B580上，KForge生成的Triton内核在KernelBench Level 2的37个GEMM+尾操作工作负载上，相比PyTorch eager和torch.compile中的较快者实现了5.13倍的几何平均加速。

Conclusion: KForge成功解决了异构硬件后端高性能内核编写的系统挑战，通过LLM驱动的迭代优化框架，能够自动生成跨平台的高效内核，并在实际硬件上表现出优于现有手工优化基线的性能。

Abstract: Production inference increasingly targets a heterogeneous mix of accelerators. Agentic pipelines interleave reasoning, tool calls, and multi-agent coordination, each with distinct compute and memory profiles. For optimal efficiency, each stage should run on the accelerator best suited to it. This creates a systems challenge: each pipeline now requires high-performance kernels across a growing set of hardware backends and programming models. Writing these kernels by hand is time-consuming, demands deep low-level expertise, and does not scale as kernel complexity grows. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have been leveraged for automatic kernel generation, but challenges in low-level code generation and cross-backend generalization persist. We present KForge, a cross-platform framework built around an iterative refinement loop driven by two collaborating LLM-based agents: a generation agent that produces and progressively refines kernels using compilation and correctness feedback, and a performance-analysis agent that interprets profiling data, from programmatic APIs to GUI-based tools, and emits recommendations that steer the next round of synthesis. The loop alternates between functional passes, which drive a candidate to correctness, and optimization passes, which close the performance gap to hand-tuned baselines. We evaluate KForge on two backends with very different baseline reference availability. On NVIDIA B200, KForge achieves a 2.12$\%$ improvement in end-to-end throughput compared to TensorRT-LLM on the gpt-oss-20b inference speed benchmark. On Intel Arc B580, KForge generates Triton kernels achieving a 5.13$\times$ geometric mean speedup over the faster of PyTorch eager and torch.compile on 37 GEMM + tail-ops workloads from KernelBench Level 2, primarily via operator fusion and mixed-precision execution.

</details>


### [140] [Neural Networks Provably Learn Spectral Representations for Group Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02993)
*Jianliang He,Leda Wang,Fengzhuo Zhang,Siyu Chen,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过群组合任务，利用傅里叶域分析揭示了神经网络训练动力学的表示论机制，证明神经元会收敛到单个不可约表示，并发现了一种新的低秩压缩现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络训练过程中结构化内部结构如何涌现是深度学习研究的核心问题，现有理论缺乏对这一现象的深入理解。

Method: 通过群组合任务（预测有限群G中元素的组合g1☆g2），将投影梯度流提升到傅里叶域，证明训练动力学受表示论能量泛函上的黎曼梯度上升支配。

Result: 在随机初始化下，每个神经元几乎必然收敛到单个不可约表示，跨层傅里叶系数实现旋转秩一对齐；对阿贝尔群提供了完整的群体层面描述：随机初始化促进非平凡表示的均匀多样化并诱导Haar均匀相位，通过多数投票机制联合逼近指示函数；相位对齐和表示竞争以指数收敛速率出现。

Conclusion: 该框架为特征学习提供了表示论解释，刻画了矩阵值群表示的新型低秩压缩现象，深化了对神经网络训练中结构涌现的理解。

Abstract: Understanding how structured internal structure emerges during neural network training is central to the study of deep learning. We investigate this phenomenon through the group composition task, where a two-layer neural network is trained to predict $g_1 \star g_2$ for elements of a finite group $G$. By lifting the projected gradient flow to the Fourier domain, we demonstrate that the training dynamics are governed by a Riemannian gradient ascent on a representation-theoretic energy functional. We prove that, under random initialization, this flow drives each neuron to converge almost surely toward a single irreducible representation, while the cross-layer Fourier coefficients achieve a rotational rank-one alignment. This framework provides a representation-theoretic account of feature learning and characterizes a novel low-rank compression phenomenon for matrix-valued group representations. Moreover, for Abelian groups, we provide a complete population-level description: random initialization promotes uniform diversification across nontrivial representations and induces Haar-uniform phases, jointly approximating the indicator via a majority-vote mechanism. We further prove that both phase alignment and representation competition emerge with exponential convergence rates.

</details>


### [141] [CoughSense: Five-Class Respiratory Disease Classification via Whisper Encoder Fine-Tuning and Dual-Encoder Cross-Attention Fusion with Balanced Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02998)
*Nikhil Vincent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CoughSense系统，利用Whisper编码器和主动帧QKV注意力池化，将咳嗽录音分类为健康、COVID-19、哮喘、支气管炎和肺炎五类，在五折交叉验证中达到82.3%的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动咳嗽分析研究大多局限于二元COVID-19检测，而实际应用需要一个能在消费级智能手机上从单次咳嗽录音中区分多种呼吸系统疾病的实用工具。

Method: 整合四个公共数据集共18,301条录音，使用OpenAI Whisper编码器作为预训练骨干网络。核心贡献是主动帧QKV注意力池化，仅关注1500个编码器标记中的前200个，解决3秒咳嗽仅占Whisper 30秒输入窗口150个标记的静音稀释问题。其他技术包括WeightedRandomSampler、SpecAugment、平衡Mixup、监督对比辅助损失、FiLM症状调节和梯度反转域适应，以及融合Whisper与OPERA-CT的双编码器模型。

Result: CoughSense（Whisper-tiny，8.6M参数）在五折交叉验证中达到82.3%平衡准确率（macro-F1为0.817，AUC为0.941），比ImageNet预训练的EfficientNet-B2高11.1个百分点，比从头训练的ViT高29.6个百分点。五个类别的召回率均超过74%，其中四个超过80%。双编码器模型达到85.4%平衡准确率。主动帧池化是所有消融组件中贡献最大的单个组件，提升5.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 主动帧池化有效解决了使用Whisper作为骨干网络的短音频任务中的静音稀释问题，对类似的短音频分类任务具有广泛适用价值。系统成功实现了多类别呼吸系统疾病分类，为低成本呼吸系统筛查提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Automated cough analysis offers a path to low-cost respiratory screening, but most existing work stops at binary COVID-19 detection. A practical tool needs to tell apart several respiratory conditions from one cough recording on a consumer smartphone. We present CoughSense, a system that sorts cough recordings into five classes. These are healthy, COVID-19, asthma or respiratory condition, bronchitis, and pneumonia. We aggregated 18,301 recordings from four public datasets (Coswara, CoughVID, Virufy, and the West China Hospital Pediatric Cough Dataset) and used the OpenAI Whisper encoder as a pretrained backbone for cough disease classification. The main contribution is active-frame QKV attention pooling, which restricts attention to the first 200 of 1500 encoder tokens. This avoids the silence-dilution problem that arises because a 3-second cough fills only 150 tokens of Whisper's 30-second input window. Other training parts handle the 19 to 1 class imbalance and the four-dataset domain shift. These include WeightedRandomSampler, SpecAugment, Balanced Mixup with forced minority pairing, a supervised contrastive auxiliary loss, FiLM symptom conditioning, and gradient-reversal domain adaptation. A dual-encoder model fuses Whisper with the OPERA-CT respiratory foundation model through cross-attention. CoughSense (Whisper-tiny, 8.6M parameters) reached 82.3 percent balanced accuracy on five-fold cross-validation (macro-F1 of 0.817, AUC of 0.941). It beat an ImageNet-pretrained EfficientNet-B2 by 11.1 points and a ViT trained from scratch by 29.6 points. All five classes passed 74 percent recall and four of five passed 80 percent. The dual-encoder model reached 85.4 percent balanced accuracy. Active-frame pooling is the largest single contributor across all ablation components at 5.1 points, which should help any short-audio task using Whisper as a backbone.

</details>


### [142] [How Quantization Changes Interpretable Features: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03002)
*Evan Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 量化后大模型的可解释性特征会系统性退化，且这种退化无法被传统任务指标（如困惑度）检测到，表明行为表现相同并不意味着内部机制一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型部署流程通常只关注量化后模型在困惑度或下游任务准确率上的表现，却极少检验全精度模型中识别出的可解释性特征是否在权重舍入后仍然存活。鉴于安全审计和干预措施日益依赖这些特征，研究量化对特征忠实性的影响至关重要。

Method: 研究者使用冻结的稀疏自编码器（SAE）作为固定测量基准，对全精度模型和RTN量化模型在相同词元上的激活进行编码。通过皮尔逊相关性量化特征存活率，并在Pythia-70M和Gemma-2-2B模型上从INT8到INT4进行比特宽度扫描。

Result: 特征存活呈分级退化模式：在INT6下，Pythia-70M有62.4%的活跃特征存活，Gemma-2-2B为51.3%，多数非存活特征被模糊而非完全破坏。存活率可仅从全精度统计数据预测（交叉验证AUC 0.92-0.97），且量化与匹配困惑度的幅度剪枝会破坏高度重叠的特征集（Jaccard重叠0.79-0.86）。

Conclusion: 任务指标的等效性不足以证明可解释性发现在量化部署中仍然有效，需要开发特征级别的压缩审计方法来确保模型压缩后的安全性和可解释性。

Abstract: Quantization is a standard path to deploying large language models, and a quantized model is typically judged acceptable when its perplexity or downstream accuracy stays close to the full-precision original. Whether the model still computes in the same way, or whether the interpretable features identified in the full-precision model survive weight rounding, is rarely tested, even as safety audits and steering interventions increasingly rely on those features. We ask whether sparse autoencoder (SAE) features extracted from a dense full-precision model remain faithful once that model is quantized. Using a frozen SAE as a fixed measurement basis, we encode full-precision and round-to-nearest (RTN) quantized activations on identical tokens and quantify per-feature survival by Pearson correlation, sweeping bit-widths from INT8 to INT4 on Pythia-70M and Gemma-2-2B. We find that feature survival is graded: features degrade systematically rather than failing all at once, with 62.4 percent of active features surviving at INT6 on Pythia-70M and 51.3 percent surviving at INT6 on Gemma-2-2B, and with most non-survivors blurred rather than destroyed. Survival is predictable from full-precision statistics alone, with cross-validated AUCs of 0.92 to 0.97 and peak activation as the strongest marginal predictor. Critically, task metrics can miss this damage: on Gemma-2-2B, INT7 improves perplexity while degrading 18.7 percent of features. Finally, quantization and matched-perplexity magnitude pruning damage strongly overlapping feature sets, with Jaccard overlap of 0.79 to 0.86 and damage-score Spearman correlation of 0.98, suggesting a shared mode of compression-induced vulnerability. These results show that behavioral parity is insufficient evidence that interpretability findings transfer to quantized deployments, motivating feature-level audits of compression.

</details>


### [143] [Exact equivariance, kept through training, buys zero-shot generalisation across the symmetry group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03003)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了等变编码器-预测器构建的潜在世界模型具有数学上可证明的对称性：当动力学具有群G对称性时，单步预测误差在整个群上保持不变。实验验证了该对称性在训练后仍保持（残差~10^-6），等变模型在分布外泛化显著优于非等变基线（误差降低13-157倍），且闭环控制在群上保持浮点精度不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索等变神经网络构建的世界模型是否能继承训练损失的对称性，并验证这种对称性是否能带来更好的泛化性能和模型紧凑性。作者希望验证"举一反三"的数学性质：在受限数据切片上拟合动力学能否泛化到整个轨道。

Method: 方法包括：1) 构建由等变编码器E和等变预测器f组成的潜在世界模型；2) 利用群论证明当动力学具有群G对称性（通过正交表示ρ(g)作用）时，单步预测relMSE在整个群上保持不变；3) 使用Muon/AdamW优化器、EMA和VICReg损失进行训练；4) 将等变性论证扩展到闭环控制，证明在匹配的等变规划器下控制轨迹也满足对称性。

Result: 实验结果表明：[A] 对称性在优化后仍保持（组合编码-预测残差~10^-6）；[B] 单步误差在整个群上平坦（五位有效数字），等变模型比非等变基线在分布外误差降低13.8倍（2D）、17.2倍（3D）、157倍（SE(3)全群），模型参数减少4.5-7.4倍；[C] 闭环控制在群上保持浮点精度不变性（2D/SO(2)上精确，3D/SE(3)上统计平坦）；等变模型的H步 rollout误差保持平坦，而非等变模型的残差随H累积。

Conclusion: 结论：等变世界模型能够数学上严格保证对称性，带来显著的分布外泛化提升和模型紧凑性。相比数据增强、暴力扩展和软等变性方法，严格等变性能够实现浮点精度的不变性。该性质在闭环控制中同样成立，且对长时间预测保持稳定性。这为构建高效、可泛化的世界模型提供了理论基础。

Abstract: A latent world model built from an equivariant encoder $E$ and an equivariant predictor $f$ inherits a provable symmetry of its training loss: when the world's dynamics genuinely carries a group $G$ acting on latents by an orthogonal representation $ρ(g)$, the one-step prediction relMSE is exactly invariant across the whole group, so fitting the dynamics on a restricted slice of orientations mathematically determines it on the entire orbit (jǔ yī fǎn sān). We verify this end-to-end at laptop scale (CPU/MPS, fully seeded). [A] The symmetry survives a real Muon/AdamW + EMA + VICReg run -- composed encode-then-predict residual $\sim 10^{-6}$ after optimisation, not just at initialisation, and under any optimiser. [B] One-step error is flat to five digits across the group, while a same-hypothesis-class non-equivariant baseline fits the slice but breaks out-of-distribution (VN $\times 1.00$ vs baseline $\times 13.8$ in 2D, $\times 17.2$ in 3D, $\times 157$ over the full $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ ladder), with the equivariant model $4.5$-$7.4\times$ smaller. [C] The same isometry argument lifts to closed loop: under a matching equivariant planner the control trajectory at orientation $g$ is exactly $ρ(g)$ applied to the seen one, so closed-loop error is invariant across the group -- float-floor-exact in 2D/$\mathrm{SO}(2)$ on real PushT and statistically flat in 3D/$\mathrm{SE}(3)$ (disjoint 95% CIs). We stress-test the prior against Sutton's Bitter Lesson: augmentation, brute-force scale, and soft-equivariance each close at most the across-group task metric, never the float-floor exactness. Because equivariance is closed under composition, the $H$-fold rollout stays flat ($\times 1.00$, $\le 2\times 10^{-7}$) at every horizon, while the baseline's residual compounds with $H$. Out of scope: task-success sweeps, planner-free invariance, and scaling.

</details>


### [144] [ConTraIRL: Factorized Contrastive Abstractions for Transferable IRL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03017)
*Yikang Gui,Bikramjit Banerjee,Prashant Doshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ConTraIRL框架，通过学习解耦的动力学和目标潜在表示，实现了逆向强化学习中的组合式奖励迁移，在未见过的动力学-目标组合上实现了有效的少样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在逆向强化学习（IRL）中，当策略需要泛化到未见过的环境动力学和任务目标组合时，奖励迁移往往不可靠。

Method: 采用双编码器架构将观测映射到分离的动力学和目标潜在空间，使用双对比目标进行训练：时间对齐鼓励动力学编码器学习目标不变的结构，而目标编码器捕获动力学不变的特征。这种分解支持在重组的动力学-目标设置下进行奖励推断。

Result: 在连续控制基准测试上的实验表明，该方法能够有效地对未见过的动力学-目标配对进行少样本迁移，在样本效率和奖励恢复方面优于迁移IRL基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过学习解耦的动力学和目标潜在表示，ConTraIRL框架成功实现了组合式奖励迁移，为解决IRL中策略泛化到新环境组合的挑战提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Reward transfer in Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) is unreliable when policies must generalize to unseen combinations of environment dynamics and task goals. We propose Factorized Contrastive Abstractions for Transferable IRL (ConTraIRL), a framework that enables compositional reward transfer by learning decoupled latent representations of these two factors. ConTraIRL uses a dual-encoder architecture that maps observations into separate dynamics and goal latent spaces, trained with a dual contrastive objective. Temporal alignment encourages the dynamics encoder to learn goal-invariant structure, while the goal encoder captures dynamics-invariant features. This factorization supports reward inference under recombined dynamics-goal settings. Experiments on continuous control benchmarks demonstrate effective few-shot transfer to unseen dynamics-goal pairings, improving sample efficiency and reward recovery over transfer IRL baselines.

</details>


### [145] [Will Accurate Fields Mislead Photonic Design? FromGlobal Accuracy to Port Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03038)
*Yitian Zhang,Yonghong chen,Youming Chen,Yiyang Li,Xing Zhe,Renhe Lu,Shaolin Liao,Yuzhe Ma,Zhong Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出传播对齐神经算子PaNO及其变体PaNO-R2，解决神经场代理模型在光子器件设计中全局场误差与局部端口读out排名不一致的问题，在3×3 MMI基准测试上将端口功率误差降低72.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经场代理模型可以加速光子设计流程，但在传播主导的MMI分束器和耦合器中，全局场误差较小的模型仍可能错误排名候选器件。这是因为端口功率、分束比、相位和耦合由累积模态干涉和输出窗口聚合决定，而非仅由平均场相似度决定。现有的场误差指标与设计决策之间存在失配。

Method: 提出Field/Mediator/Readout视角，将密集复场误差与传播轮廓误差、输出窗口误差分离。在此基础上设计传播对齐神经算子PaNO，保持全场预测接口，同时围绕局部边界结构、横向模态内容、轴向传播和跨模态交互组织潜在状态。进一步提出PaNO-R2变体，针对端口区域附近的残差场分量引入输出感知反馈。

Result: 在15波长可调谐3×3 MMI基准测试（4608个留出字段）上：PaNO将NeurOLight的端口功率误差从0.2018降至0.0739，尽管cMAE略高；PaNO-R2取得最佳cMAE、传播轮廓误差、输出轮廓误差和端口功率误差，相比NeurOLight分别降低端口功率误差和输出轮廓误差72.7%和72.5%。

Conclusion: 全局场精度不足以保证设计相关的读出保真度。通过传播对齐机制组织潜在状态，可以有效弥合场误差与设计决策之间的差距，为光子器件设计提供更可靠的代理模型。

Abstract: Neural field surrogates can accelerate photonic design loops, but a surrogate that looks accurate in global field error can still mis-rank candidate devices when the final decision depends on localized output-port readouts. This risk is acute in propagation-dominated MMI splitters and couplers, where port power, splitting, phase, and coupling are determined by accumulated modal interference and output-window aggregation rather than by average field similarity alone. We study this field-to-design mismatch through a Field/Mediator/Readout view that separates dense complex-field error from propagation-profile and output-window errors before port aggregation. To align the surrogate with this chain, we propose PaNO, a propagation-aligned neural operator that keeps the full-field prediction interface while organizing latent states around local boundary structure, transverse modal content, axial propagation, and cross-mode interaction. We also evaluate PaNO-R2, an output-aware feedback variant for residual field components near the port region. On a 15-wavelength tunable $3{\times}3$ MMI benchmark with 4608 held-out fields, PaNO lowers NeurOLight's port-power error from 0.2018 to 0.0739 despite slightly higher cMAE, showing that global field accuracy alone is not sufficient for design-relevant readout fidelity. PaNO-R2 attains the best cMAE, propagation-profile error, output-profile error, and port-power error, reducing NeurOLight's port-power and output-profile errors by 72.7\% and 72.5\%.

</details>


### [146] [What Do Students Learn? A Feature-Level Analysis of Dark Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03052)
*Seungu Kang,Songkuk Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Confusion Distillation (CD)，一种无需教师模型的自蒸馏方法，通过利用模型自身的混淆矩阵结构信息作为动态软目标，在CIFAR-100上优于现有自蒸馏方法1.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏研究对学生模型如何获取特征表示的机制探索不足，且传统KD依赖教师模型。本文旨在揭示KD的有效机制，并基于此开发无需教师的高效自蒸馏方法。

Method: 使用Interaction Tensor框架分析学生模型的特征学习机制，发现有效KD会剪枝低频样本特定特征；基于数据集级混淆矩阵包含类似教师"暗知识"的结构信息这一发现，提出Confusion Distillation (CD)，将模型自身的混淆模式作为动态软目标进行自蒸馏。

Result: CD在ResNet-34和ResNet-50上的CIFAR-100实验中表现优异，相比CS-KD和PS-KD等现有自蒸馏方法提升1.2%，同时提供了比标准KD更具计算效率的替代方案。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏的有效性源于其正则化作用，促使模型依赖紧凑的高度可复用特征集；混淆矩阵可作为有效的知识载体，为无教师模型的高效自蒸馏开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) is a powerful tool for model compression, yet the precise mechanisms by which student models acquire feature representations remain underexplored. In this work, we analyze student feature learning using the Interaction Tensor framework. Our analysis reveals that effective KD acts as a regularizer that prunes low-frequency, sample-specific features, encouraging the student to rely on a compact set of highly reusable features. Crucially, we observe that the dataset-level confusion matrix contains structural information analogous to the teacher's "Dark Knowledge." Leveraging this insight, we propose Confusion Distillation (CD), a teacher-free self-distillation method that utilizes the model's own evolving confusion patterns as dynamic soft targets. CD achieves competitive performance on ResNet-34 and ResNet-50 for CIFAR-100, outperforming existing self-distillation methods like CS-KD and PS-KD by 1.2% while offering a computationally efficient alternative to standard KD.

</details>


### [147] [Rethinking Molecular Text Representations for LLMs: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03057)
*Arun Raja,Garrett M. Morris,Kian Ming A. Chai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了16个大语言模型在9种分子表示和8个化学任务上的表现，发现性能强烈依赖于表示方式，CML表现最佳，而广泛使用的SMILES并非最优选择，论证了任务感知的表示路由必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地用于分子任务，但目前尚不清楚应该使用哪种分子表示方法，缺乏对不同表示方式在LLM上性能差异的系统性研究。

Method: 构建系统基准测试，评估16个LLM（包括推理/非推理变体、化学专用LLM和闭源前沿模型）在9种分子表示和8个化学任务上的表现，并通过LLM-as-a-judge、tokenization审计、线性探针和注意力分析进行机理研究。

Result: 性能强烈依赖于表示方式，无单一表示在所有任务中获胜；CML表现最佳，其次是MolJSON、InChI和规范SMILES；结构化文本表示主导结构任务，IUPAC主导语义任务；化学专用模型在SMILES上表现好但在结构化文本表示上性能大幅下降；IUPAC产生最高比例的正确分子生成；不同表示在模型内部编码方式不同。

Conclusion: 研究结果反对表示不变性评估，强调了SMILES-only评估的局限性，并为基于LLM的化学研究提出了任务感知的表示路由方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for molecular tasks, but it remains unclear which molecular representation to use. We present a systematic benchmark evaluating LLM molecular competence across nine representations and eight chemical tasks. We benchmark 16 LLMs across five model families, including reasoning and non-reasoning variants, chemistry-specialized LLMs, and closed frontier models. Performance is strongly representation-dependent and no single representation wins across tasks, though CML is the best, followed by MolJSON, InChI, and then canonical SMILES. Explicit structured text representations (CML and MolJSON) dominate structural tasks; IUPAC dominates semantic tasks, winning molecule retrieval for all 16 LLMs; and SMILES variants are rarely optimal despite their prevalence in pretraining. Chemistry-specialized models perform well with SMILES at the cost of large degradations with structured text representations, suggesting SMILES-only evaluation rewards specialization that does not generalize. Using LLM-as-a-judge, we find that IUPAC produces the highest fraction of correct molecule generations. A mechanistic study via tokenization audits, linear probes and attention shows that representations are encoded differently inside the model; for example, structured representations require higher attention across the molecular span. Our results argue against representation-invariant evaluation and motivate task-aware representation routing for LLM-based chemistry.

</details>


### [148] [Learn When and Where to Connect: Adaptive Virtual Nodes for Dynamic Message Passing on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03068)
*Jaejun Lee,Joyce Jiyoung Whang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MAVN框架，通过自适应动态引入虚拟节点并学习节点与虚拟节点间的连接模式，解决了现有VN方法的连接限制问题，在9个数据集上相比骨干网络提升高达46.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于虚拟节点(VN)的MPNN方法存在三方面局限性：(1)限制所有节点连接相同数量的VN；(2)在应用MPNN前固定连接；(3)节点独立连接到VN而不考虑其他连接到同一VN的节点，这些限制阻碍了有效消息传递。

Method: 提出MAVN端到端可微分框架，包含三个核心机制：(1)按需动态引入VN以响应跨层演化的节点表示；(2)基于双层视角评分机制（节点对VN的偏好+VN对节点的偏好）自适应确定何时、向哪些节点引入VN；(3)从候选VN池中选择必要的VN并连接到非空节点子集。

Result: 在九个真实世界数据集上的实验表明：MAVN持续提升骨干MPNN性能，相比骨干网络提升高达46.5%，并超越现有基线方法；理论上证明对于任意节点-VN连接模式，存在MAVN参数集可以模拟该模式。

Conclusion: MAVN成功解决了现有VN方法的连接约束问题，实现了灵活自适应的节点-VN连接，显著提升了MPNN性能，为图神经网络中的消息传递优化提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: While Virtual Nodes (VNs) are often utilized in Message Passing Neural Networks (MPNNs) to facilitate effective message passing, existing VN-based methods have limitations, such as constraining all nodes to connect to the same number of VNs, fixing the connections before applying MPNNs, and connecting a node to a VN independently of the other nodes that connect to the same VN. We propose MAVN, an end-to-end differentiable MPNN framework that allows non-constrained connections between nodes and VNs and dynamically introduces VNs on demand in response to evolving node representations across layers. Specifically, MAVN learns to adaptively determine when (at which layer) and where (to which nodes) to introduce and connect VNs based on the relative importance of connections. From a pool of candidate VNs, MAVN selects the necessary VNs in each layer, where each selected VN is connected to a nonempty subset of nodes, guided by a dual-perspective scoring mechanism that jointly captures the nodes' preferences for VNs and the VNs' preferences for nodes. We theoretically prove that for any node-VN connectivity pattern, there exists a set of MAVN's parameters that can simulate the pattern. Experiments on nine real-world datasets demonstrate that MAVN consistently improves the performance of backbone MPNNs, achieving up to 46.5% improvement over the backbones and outperforms the baselines.

</details>


### [149] [Efficient Hyperparameter Optimization for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03073)
*Minping Chen,Bowen Xiao,Du Liang,Chuxuan Zeng,Zeyi Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JF-HPO方法，通过同时调整模型大小和训练预算作为保真度，实现了大语言模型强化学习的高效超参数优化，计算效率提升高达14.9倍，性能提升5.8%-111.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的强化学习对超参数配置高度敏感，超参数优化至关重要但计算成本昂贵。现有多保真度HPO方法由于模型规模巨大和训练周期资源密集，在LLM RL场景下效率仍然不足。

Method: 提出联合保真度超参数优化（JF-HPO）框架，同时调整模型大小和训练预算作为保真度。包含三个核心组件：（i）利用目标LLM的小型代理模型进行高效训练和评估；（ii）集成基于训练动态的精心设计早停策略；（iii）引入高效检查点机制消除冗余计算。

Result: 相比现有HPO方法，JF-HPO在相同时间预算下显著提升每个试验的计算效率（高达14.9倍），同时达到更优或具有竞争力的预测精度。与VeRL Recipe的超参数配置相比，JF-HPO实现了5.8%至111.6%的性能提升。

Conclusion: JF-HPO成功解决了大语言模型强化学习中超参数优化的计算效率瓶颈问题，通过联合保真度策略在保证性能的同时大幅降低计算成本，为LLM RL的实际应用提供了高效的超参数优化解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models (LLMs) is highly sensitive to hyperparameter configurations, making hyperparameter optimization (HPO) essential yet computationally expensive. Existing multi-fidelity HPO methods remain inefficient for LLM RL due to the massive model scale and resource-intensive training cycles. In this paper, we propose Joint Fidelity Hyperparameter Optimization (JF-HPO), which simultaneously adapts both model size and training budget as fidelity. JF-HPO is empowered by: (i) it leverages a small proxy model of the target LLM for efficient training and evaluation in each HPO trial; (ii) it integrates carefully designed early-stopping strategies based on training dynamics; (iii) it introduces an efficient checkpointing mechanism to eliminate redundant computations. Compared with existing HPO methods, JF-HPO significantly improves the computational efficiency of each trial (up to 14.9 times), while achieving better or competitive predictive accuracy under the same time budget. Notably, compared with utilizing hyperparameter configurations from the VeRL Recipe, JF-HPO delivers performance improvements ranging from 5.8% to 111.6%.

</details>


### [150] [RMPrior: Bridging Propagation Priors and Diffusion Refinement for Efficient Radio Map Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03074)
*Zixuan Guo,Xiucheng Wang,Nan Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种中点启动采样策略，通过将传播先验扰动到中间扩散时间步，在2倍加速的同时提高无线电地图重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型通过迭代去噪实现高保真无线电地图构建，但采样成本限制了其在需要频繁刷新的动态无线系统中的实用性；经典传播模型编码了有价值的场景级知识，而标准扩散推理通过从纯高斯噪声初始化完全丢弃了这些知识。

Method: 提出中点启动采样策略：将匹配的传播先验扰动到中间扩散时间步，预训练的扩散主干仅执行剩余的反向步骤，将计算集中于多径感知的精细化而非从噪声的完整重建。提供了初始化差距上界、截断改善重建保真度的充分条件以及激进截断下先验质量敏感性的理论分析。

Result: 在IRT4HighRes数据集上，当P_start=0.5时，方法实现2.01倍加速，同时NMSE、RMSE、SSIM和PSNR均优于全步基线；对三种不同保真度传播模型的先验质量消融实验证实重建质量随先验质量提升，且敏感性在更短反向轨迹下放大，与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将传播先验与扩散精细化相结合，显著提升采样效率与重建质量；实验结果还表明中点启动重建质量可作为评估不同传播模型场景级保真度的代理指标。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve high-fidelity radio map construction through iterative denoising, yet their sampling cost limits practicality in dynamic wireless systems where radio maps must be refreshed repeatedly. Meanwhile, classical propagation models encode valuable scene-level knowledge that standard diffusion inference discards entirely by initializing from pure Gaussian noise. This paper bridges propagation priors and diffusion refinement through a mid-start sampling strategy. A matched propagation prior is perturbed to an intermediate diffusion timestep, and the pretrained diffusion backbone executes only the remaining reverse steps, focusing computation on multipath-aware refinement rather than full reconstruction from noise. We provide theoretical analysis establishing an upper bound on the initialization gap, a sufficient condition under which truncation improves reconstruction fidelity, and a formal characterization of prior-quality sensitivity under aggressive truncation. Experiments on IRT4HighRes show that, at $P_{\text{start}}=0.5$, the proposed method achieves a $2.01\times$ speedup while simultaneously improving NMSE, RMSE, SSIM, and PSNR over the full-step baseline. A prior-quality ablation across three propagation models of different fidelity confirms that reconstruction quality tracks prior quality, with the sensitivity amplified under shorter reverse trajectories, consistent with the theoretical predictions. These results also suggest that mid-start reconstruction quality can serve as a proxy for ranking the scene-level fidelity of different propagation models.

</details>


### [151] [Multi-component Causal Tracing in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03085)
*Zirui Yan,Dennis Wei,Dmitriy A. Katz,Prasanna Sattigeri,Ali Tajer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的多组件因果追踪框架，通过高效算法识别对目标性能指标影响最大的模型组件子集，优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多局限于单一组件或单层的因果分析，缺乏统一框架来同时追踪多个组件的因果路径，且多组件组合搜索面临组合爆炸的计算挑战。

Method: 提出统一的多组件因果追踪框架，设计高效算法结合软干预和度量变换技术，将组合搜索问题转化为连续优化问题，在适当约束下高效求解生成组件选择的二值决策。

Result: 实验表明该方法能高效识别对目标指标（如准确性和公平性）具有高影响的模型组件子集（如注意力头和MLP神经元），性能优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了多组件同时因果追踪，为理解大语言模型的内部机制和优化模型性能提供了有效工具，代码已开源。

Abstract: Causal tracing systematically intervenes on a large language model's (LLM's) internal representations to uncover and quantify the causal pathways linking specific inputs or computations to specific metrics of interest, quantifying the LLM's behavior. Building on previous single-component or single-layer studies, this paper presents a unified framework for causally tracing multiple components simultaneously. This framework systematically identifies the subsets of components (e.g., attention heads and multi-layer perceptron neurons) most critical to a desired target performance metric (e.g., accuracy and fairness). This is achieved by incorporating flexible interventions applied to a wide range of desired metrics. To address the combinatorial complexity of the multi-component problem, an efficient algorithm is designed that leverages soft interventions and a carefully designed metric transformation, converting the combinatorial search problem into a continuous one that can be solved efficiently under proper constraints, thereby generating proper binary decisions for selecting components. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method efficiently identifies subsets of the model's components that have a high impact on the target metric, outperforming existing baseline approaches. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZiruiYan/multi-component-causal-tracing.

</details>


### [152] [Learning to Solve, Forgetting to Retain: Correct-Set Turnover in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03087)
*Chuanyu Qin,Chenxu Yang,Qingyi Si,Naibin Gu,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了RLVR训练中存在的"正确集翻转"现象（已解决问题变不可解），提出了修复窗口原理，并设计了零开销的保留感知复习机制来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现强化学习训练中存在隐藏成本：已掌握的问题在训练过程中会退化为不可解状态，而现有RLVR流程未将已掌握内容的保留作为显式优化目标。

Method: 提出\method{}机制，跟踪已掌握的提示，利用预展开批次替换策略定期重新引入这些提示进行复习，实现零额外展开开销的保留感知训练。

Result: 在涵盖图文、视频和纯文本的20个基准测试上，基于Qwen3-VL和Qwen2.5-Math模型验证，方法在GRPO、DAPO和回放基线上均取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将保留与获取并列为主要优化目标，并利用修复窗口原理及时干预，可以有效缓解训练过程中的知识退化问题，具有良好的跨模态和跨算法泛化性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the ability of large language model, yet headline accuracy gains often conceal a hidden cost: previously solved problems quietly become unsolvable as training proceeds. We frame this phenomenon as \emph{correct-set turnover}, representing the coupled dynamics of solution acquisition and regression over the mastered set. Under this view, retention becomes an explicit optimization target alongside acquisition. We analytically and empirically establish the \emph{repair-window principle}: the cost of restoring a regressed prompt grows sharply with review delay, defining a low-cost window that standard RLVR pipelines fail to exploit. To address this, we propose \textbf{\method{}}, a retention-aware review mechanism that tracks mastered prompts and periodically reintroduces them to \textbf{remind} the model of previous solutions. By utilizing pre-rollout batch replacement, \method{} incurs zero additional rollout overhead. Evaluated across 20 benchmarks spanning image-text, video, and text-only tasks with Qwen3-VL and Qwen2.5-Math, \method{} consistently improves performance over GRPO, DAPO, and replay baselines, demonstrating robust generalizability across modalities and algorithms.

</details>


### [153] [Constitutional On-Policy Safe Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03089)
*Ming Wen,Yuxuan Liu,Kun Yang,Yunhao Feng,Zhuoer Xu,Yuhao Sun,Shiwen Cui,Xiang Zheng,Xingjun Ma,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了COPSD方法，通过Cross-SFT冷启动校准教师模型，再进行宪法条件下的在线策略蒸馏，有效解决了安全对齐中OPSD方法的分布崩塌问题，在安全性与有用性之间取得了更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然在线策略自蒸馏(OPSD)是一种高效的后训练范式，但在安全对齐场景中仍存在严重的分布崩塌问题：宪法条件约束使教师模型分布向短小且过度保守的响应收缩，而反向KL散度进一步放大了这种收缩，导致表达能力下降。现有研究虽然发现OPSD在可验证推理任务中会崩塌，但安全对齐由高级宪法指导而非明确目标答案，需要重新审视密集蒸馏的有效性。

Method: 提出宪法在线策略安全蒸馏(COPSD)方法：1)首先通过Cross-SFT冷启动校准教师模型；2)然后执行宪法条件下的在线策略蒸馏。该方法基于对几何泄漏效应的理论分析：在非正交语义空间的安全边界下，安全压力会转移到表达能力维度。

Result: 在12个基准测试上的实验表明，COPSD相比基线方法实现了持续更强的安全性与有用性权衡，同时大幅降低了对一般推理能力的安全税。该方法有效缓解了教师分布的收缩问题，提升了模型的表达能力。

Conclusion: COPSD成功解决了安全对齐中OPSD的分布崩塌挑战，通过理论分析和实验验证证明了其有效性，为安全对齐提供了一种更优的蒸馏范式，在保持安全性的同时减少了对模型推理能力的负面影响。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as an efficient post-training paradigm by using a teacher conditioned on privileged information to provide dense token-level supervision. Prior work has shown that OPSD can collapse in verifiable reasoning tasks, but safety alignment differs in that it is guided by high-level constitutions rather than explicit target answers, making it a natural setting to revisit dense distillation. However, our pilot study show that safety OPSD still suffers from severe collapse: constitutional conditioning contracts the teacher distribution toward short and overly conservative responses, and Reverse KL further amplifies this contraction into reduced expressiveness. We formalize this effect as geometric leakage under safety boundaries in a non-orthogonal semantic space, where safety pressure transfers into the expressiveness dimension. Based on this analysis, we propose Constitutional On-Policy Safe Distillation (COPSD), which first calibrates the teacher through a Cross-SFT cold-start and then performs constitution-conditioned on-policy distillation. Experiments on 12 benchmarks show that COPSD achieves a consistently stronger safety--helpfulness trade-off than baselines while substantially reducing the safety tax on general reasoning ability.

</details>


### [154] [FGRPO: Federated GRPO with Adaptive Aggregation on Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03094)
*Pengyu Chen,Shaowei Li,Kai Wang,Yunsheng Yuan,Kai Han,Jun Luo,Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了联邦群组相对策略优化（FGRPO）框架，在保护数据隐私的前提下，实现了跨异构数据所有者的推理模型去中心化微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管群组相对策略优化（GRPO）通过消除评论家网络实现了卓越的可扩展性，但在中心化基础设施上部署需要从分布式所有者收集大量数据，这带来了严重的隐私风险。同时，异构任务间奖励尺度的差异会导致训练不稳定。

Method: 提出联邦GRPO（FGRPO）框架，采用基于相对性能增益的自适应聚合机制。该机制通过刻画每个客户端相对于其个性化历史基线的改进程度，动态优先处理有效的学习轨迹，从而有效缓解异构任务间奖励尺度差异导致的不稳定性。

Result: FGRPO能够在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据上实现稳健收敛，同时有效保护数据隐私，并能根据局部任务难度动态调整优先级。

Conclusion: FGRPO成功解决了GRPO在分布式场景下的隐私保护问题，并通过自适应聚合机制有效应对异构数据带来的挑战，为分布式推理模型微调提供了可行的隐私保护解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in language models have established reinforcement learning as the primary paradigm for eliciting self-correction and long-chain reasoning. While group relative policy optimization (GRPO) offers superior scalability by eliminating the critic network, deploying it on a central infrastructure entails collecting a large volume of data from distributed owners, which poses significant privacy risks. To address these concerns, we introduce federated GRPO (FGRPO), a framework designed to decentralize the fine-tuning of reasoning models across heterogeneous data owners. To effectively mitigate the instability caused by divergent reward scales across heterogeneous tasks, FGRPO incorporates an adaptive aggregation mechanism based on relative performance gain. By characterizing each client's improvement relative to its personalized historical baseline, the framework dynamically prioritizes effective learning trajectories regardless of local task difficulty. FGRPO ensures robust convergence on non-IID data while preserving data privacy.

</details>


### [155] [Learning to See via Epiretinal Implant Stimulation in silico with Model-Based Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03118)
*Jacob Lavoie,Marwan Besrour,William Lemaire,Jean Rouat,Réjean Fontaine,Eric Plourde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用深度强化学习智能体，通过组合各向同性和各向异性刺激形状来渲染视网膜图像的新方法，旨在提高人工视觉的图像可识别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜退行性疾病（如年龄相关性黄斑变性和色素性视网膜炎）会导致光感受器层退化。通过视网膜植入物电刺激幸存的视网膜神经节细胞来恢复视力时，会产生沿神经纤维束方向拉长的各向异性形状。现有方法试图通过避免刺激神经纤维束来获得各向同性的像素状形状，但本研究提出可以利用这些形状来渲染更易理解的图像。

Method: 研究开发了一个名为rlretina的强化学习环境，将视网膜刺激任务形式化为基于笔触的渲染任务。通过深度强化学习训练智能体，使其学习组合各向同性和各向异性形状来构建图像。采用基于心理物理学验证的神经纤维图模型，以基于模型的数据生成方式进行训练，模拟不同虚拟患者感知到的图像。研究还探索了基于误差和基于感知的奖励指标的适用性。

Result: 训练后的深度强化学习智能体能够成功学习组合各向同性和各向异性形状来形成图像。实验结果表明，与传统简单方法相比，智能体在不同虚拟患者条件下都能生成更具可识别性的图像，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 这项工作提出了一种处理视网膜表面刺激的新方法，利用各向异性光幻视为提高人工恢复视力的视敏度迈出了第一步，为改善视网膜植入物的视觉恢复效果提供了新思路。

Abstract: Objective: Diseases such as age-related macular degeneration and retinitis pigmentosa cause the degradation of the photoreceptor layer. One approach to restore vision is to electrically stimulate the surviving retinal ganglion cells with a microelectrode array such as epiretinal implants. Epiretinal implants are known to generate visible anisotropic shapes elongated along the axon fascicles of neighboring retinal ganglion cells. Recent work has demonstrated that to obtain isotropic pixel-like shapes, it is possible to map axon fascicles and avoid stimulating them by inactivating electrodes or lowering stimulation current levels. Avoiding axon fascicle stimulation aims to remove brushstroke-like shapes in favor of a more reduced set of pixel-like shapes. Approach: In this study, we propose the use of isotropic and anisotropic shapes to render intelligible images on the retina of a virtual patient in a reinforcement learning environment named rlretina. The environment formalizes the task as using brushstrokes in a stroke-based rendering task. Main Results: We train a deep reinforcement learning agent that learns to assemble isotropic and anisotropic shapes to form an image. We investigate which error-based or perception-based metrics is adequate to reward the agent. The agent is trained in a model-based data generation fashion using the psychophysically validated axon map model to render images as perceived by different virtual patients. We show that the agent can generate more intelligible images compared to the naive method in different virtual patients. Significance: This work shares a new way to address epiretinal stimulation that constitutes a first step towards improving visual acuity in artificially-restored vision using anisotropic phosphenes.

</details>


### [156] [TiWeaver: Unified Temporal Dynamics Modeling via Contextual Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03121)
*Zhe Li,Jindong Tian,Hao Miao,Zhi Lei,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TiWeaver框架，通过自适应分词和细粒度异步依赖建模解决多元时间序列中的不规则性问题，在12个数据集上SOTA，性能提升高达25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列数据因来源多样，常存在缺失值和非均匀采样频率等不规则性，导致通道间出现复杂且异步的时间依赖关系。现有采用固定分块方案的单模型难以适应这些多样的不规则性，从而阻碍了准确的预测。

Method: 提出TiWeaver统一框架，包含两个核心组件：1）图引导自适应分词器（G$^2$AT），通过联合考虑时间密度和表示一致性将时间序列划分为上下文高度一致的patch；2）细粒度异步依赖提取器（FADE），用于建模细粒度的异步通道间依赖关系，同时融合长期历史依赖。

Result: 在12个真实世界时间序列数据集上进行评估，TiWeaver实现了最先进的性能，相比现有方法性能提升高达25%，证明了其在不同领域和数据特征下的鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: TiWeaver成功解决了多元时间序列中的不规则性问题，能够自适应地处理时间动态和细粒度通道间依赖，为实际应用中的多元时间序列预测提供了一个统一且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting plays a critical role in real-world applications, including weather prediction, stock analysis, and health monitoring. Due to the diversity of data sources, time series exhibit diverse temporal dynamics, often accompanied by various irregularities such as missing values and non-uniform sampling frequencies. Such irregularities lead to complex and asynchronous temporal dependencies across channels. Thus, a single model with a fixed patching scheme often fails to adapt well to diverse multivariate time series, hindering accurate forecasting. In this paper, we propose TiWeaver, a unified framework designed to handle temporal dynamics and fine-grained inter-channel dependencies adaptively. Specifically, we introduce a Graph-Guided Adaptive Tokenizer (G$^2$AT) that divides time series into high contextually coherent patches by jointly considering temporal density and representation consistency. In addition, we propose a Fine-grained Asynchronous Dependency Extractor (FADE), which is designed to model fine-grained asynchronous inter-channel dependencies while incorporating long-term historical dependencies. We evaluate TiWeaver on 12 real-world time series datasets, where it achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing methods up to 25%. These results demonstrate its robustness and effectiveness across diverse domains and data characteristics.

</details>


### [157] [Rethinking Neural Width for Alternating Current Optimal Power Flow Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03125)
*Dhruvi Khandelwal,Anurag Basistha,Ayushi Jolotia,Parikshit Pareek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LG-ND算法，通过渐进式网络扩展确定ACOPF代理所需的最小网络宽度，在保持性能的同时显著降低网络复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习代理在交流最优潮流（ACOPF）应用中缺乏确定网络架构规模的系统性方法。针对安全关键型电网操作的形式化验证需求，研究如何确定神经网络近似ACOPF流形所需的最小宽度至关重要。

Method: 提出损失引导神经密集化（LG-ND）算法，通过构造性思想实验进行渐进式网络扩展。算法仅在当前深度神经网络拓扑无法进一步改进时才进行扩展，从而逐步发现所需的网络容量。

Result: 在多个IEEE系统上的实验结果表明，LG-ND算法每层神经元数量仅需文献基准方法的十分之一即可达到性能对等，实现了显著的架构极简性。

Conclusion: 这种架构极简性对安全关键型电网操作所需的形式化验证具有重要意义，为ACOPF深度学习代理的网络架构确定提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning proxies for Alternating Current Optimal Power Flow (ACOPF) lack systematic methods for determining architectural size. This paper conducts a constructive thought experiment to answer a fundamental inquiry: how wide must a neural network be to almost accurately approximate the ACOPF manifold? We introduce a Loss-Guided Neural Densification (LG-ND) algorithm that incrementally discovers necessary capacity by expanding only when the current deep neural network topology fails to improve further. Empirical results across various IEEE systems show that LG-ND achieves performance parity with literature baselines using up to ten times fewer neurons per layer. Such architectural minimalism is critical for the formal verification required in safety-critical grid operations.

</details>


### [158] [Synthetic Hallucinations, Real Gains: Hard Negatives from Frontier Models for FIM Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03130)
*Mahdi Erfanian,Nelson Daniel Troncoso,Aashna Garg,Amabel Gale,Xiaoyu Liu,Pareesa Ameneh Golnari,Shengyu Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需执行的幻觉缓解方法：利用前沿代码模型合成“看似合理但错误”的负样本进行对比学习，显著提升了代码模型的Fill-in-the-Middle准确性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型开源代码模型在IDE自动补全中仍会产生幻觉式FIM补全（调用不存在的方法、变量等），现有缓解方法要么需要语言特定的执行沙箱（不适用于中间输入状态），要么需要大量人工标注数据进行偏好优化。因此需要一种更高效、无需执行的替代方案。

Method: 提出执行免费的训练流程：1) 从GitHub抓取多语言FIM上下文（8种语言）；2) 使用三个前沿模型根据Delulu幻觉分类法合成四种类型的硬负样本；3) 构建配对的chosen/rejected数据集；4) 使用对比信号进行监督微调。在Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct上对100K精选数据进行微调。

Result: 7B模型在Delulu基准上exact match提升+18.8分，编辑相似度提升+0.22，覆盖所有语言和幻觉类型；同时在HumanEval-Infilling和SAFIM所有子集上均有改善。3B模型在Delulu上提升+12.8 EM。五维消融实验揭示了关键设计因素，SFT方法优于DPO/ORPO。

Conclusion: 该无需执行的合成负样本对比学习方法有效缓解了代码模型的FIM幻觉问题，在多个基准上取得显著提升。作者开源了完整流程代码，支持在任何许可语料库上端到端复现实验，为代码补全模型的可靠性提升提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Small open-source code models that power IDE autocomplete still emit hallucinated Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM) completions: syntactically natural calls to methods, parameters, variables, and imports that do not exist in the surrounding project. Existing mitigations either require per-language execution sandboxes that do not apply at mid-keystroke or preference-optimisation pipelines that need large human-labelled corpora.
