The proposed methodology is validated by demonstrating significant improvements in robustness when the algorithm is evaluated in perturbed Gymnasium MuJoCo environments, while considering the computational limitations of the post-hoc inference setting.

</details>


### [196] [When Should the Teacher Move? Temporal Coupling and Stability in Self On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03532)
*Haowei Guo,Baolong Bi,Ruicheng Zhang,Bingqian Sun,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了教师更新计划中的"隔离期"是自策略蒸馏稳定训练的关键结构特性，并发现了短视界评估无法检测的"状态无关崩溃"问题。提出了CGTR方法，通过门控机制在奖励改善和长度尾安全性的联合证据下触发教师更新，在四个任务上实现了零崩溃和最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自策略蒸馏中教师派生自学生自身参数历史，但教师的更新计划（控制师生时间耦合）尚未作为稳定性变量被系统研究。现有方法缺乏对教师冻结间隔与更新时机对训练稳定性影响的深入理解，且短视界评估可能掩盖长期训练中的失败模式。

Method: 1. 在Qwen3-8B上进行受控计划扫描，识别出"隔离期"（教师更新间完全冻结）是稳定学习的关键结构属性；
2. 引入时间KL结构、刷新冲击和长度尾风险的诊断框架；
3. 发现"状态无关崩溃"失败模式：时钟驱动刷新会在单一步骤中将暂时漂移的学生不可逆地复制到教师；
4. 提出Consolidation-Gated Teacher Refresh (CGTR)：保持隔离期的同时，基于奖励改善和长度尾安全性的联合证据门控每次刷新，确保教师更新响应真实的学生巩固而非时钟信号。

Result: 在四个任务（Chemistry、Biology、Physics、ToolUse）上，CGTR使用单一共享参数集且无需逐数据集调优，实现了零崩溃并在所有任务上取得最佳最终得分。方法能够根据每个任务的学习动态自动调节刷新频率。

Conclusion: 隔离期而非教师年龄是自策略蒸馏稳定学习的核心结构属性。时钟驱动的教师刷新存在状态无关崩溃风险，这在短视界评估中不可见且与EMA的慢性污染机制不同。基于学生巩固证据的门控刷新机制能够有效解决此问题，实现跨任务的鲁棒性能。

Abstract: Self on-policy distillation trains a student policy against a teacher derived from its own parameter history, yet the teacher's update schedule -- which governs the \emph{temporal coupling} between teacher and student -- has not been systematically studied as a stability variable. Through a controlled schedule sweep on Qwen3-8B, we establish that \emph{isolation periods}, defined as complete teacher freezing between updates, are the key structural property enabling stable learning, not teacher age. To characterize these underlying training dynamics, we introduce a diagnostic framework of temporal KL structure, refresh shock, and length-tail risk. This framework further uncovers \emph{state-oblivious collapse}: optimal short-horizon fixed schedules catastrophically fail under long-horizon training because a clock-driven refresh can copy a transiently drifting student into the teacher in a single, irreversible step. This failure mode is invisible under short-horizon evaluation and mechanistically distinct from EMA's chronic contamination. To address this, we propose \emph{Consolidation-Gated Teacher Refresh} (CGTR), which preserves isolation periods while gating each refresh on joint evidence of reward improvement and length-tail safety, ensuring every teacher movement responds to genuine student consolidation rather than a clock signal. With a single shared parameter set and no per-dataset retuning, CGTR achieves \textbf{zero collapse} and the best final score on all four tasks (Chemistry, Biology, Physics, ToolUse), self-regulating its refresh frequency to each task's learning dynamics.

</details>


### [197] [How Many Trees in a Random Forest? A Revisited Approach with Plateau Search and Optuna Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03549)
*Vadim Porvatov,Andrey Dukhovny,Andrey Lange*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成三元组的自适应平台搜索算法，用于自动确定随机森林中最小充分树数量，避免了传统超参数优化方法需要预定义搜索范围的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机森林超参数优化中，树的数量调优面临特殊困难：预测性能通常随集成规模单调提升，导致TPE和Hyperband等标准方法将估计值推向搜索范围右边界。早停策略虽可避免固定范围，但对评分噪声敏感且容易过早停止。

Method: 提出基于三元组的平台搜索算法，将树的数量从TPE直接搜索空间中移除，通过监测三个森林规模下袋外（OOB）分数的相对变化来追踪接近最小充分集成规模，并根据变化动态调整三元组位置。该方法基于容差参数实现自动化和用户可解释性。

Result: 理论分析证明了相对OOB分数准则与当前分数和极限分数差距的关系，并推导了相应OOB绝对相对差异的渐近方差估计。实验表明，选择的树数量与传统启发式方法差异显著：大多数经典基准数据集上更小，而在Arcene和Dorothea等高维生物信息学数据集上更大。

Conclusion: 该算法成功解决了随机森林树数量自动调优问题，无需预定义搜索范围即可找到最小充分集成规模，为实际应用提供了有效且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Hyperparameter optimization (HPO) for Random Forest faces a specific difficulty in tuning the number of trees: the predictive score typically improves monotonically with ensemble size, so standard methods such as Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) and Hyperband require a predefined search range and often drive the estimate toward its right boundary. Early-stopping strategies avoid fixing such a range, but can be sensitive to score noise and prone to premature stopping. To address this, we propose an integrated triplet-based plateau-search algorithm that removes the number of trees from the direct TPE search space and still exploits information accumulated across HPO trials. The method adaptively tracks a near-minimal sufficient ensemble size by monitoring relative changes in the out-of-bag (OOB) score across a triplet of forest sizes and shifting this triplet accordingly. This yields an automated and user-interpretable procedure based on a tolerance parameter. We also provide a theoretical analysis: we relate the proposed relative OOB-score criterion to the gap between the current and limiting scores, and derive an asymptotic variance estimate for the corresponding OOB-based absolute relative difference. Experiments show that the selected number of trees can differ substantially from the common heuristic: for most classical benchmark datasets it is smaller, whereas for some high-dimensional bioinformatics datasets, such as Arcene and Dorothea, it is larger. The source code and reproducible experiments are available at https://github.com/lange-am/rf_plateau_hpo.

</details>


### [198] [Analytical Evaluation of DCA Convergence Properties for Minimizing Prediction Functions of Gaussian RBF Support Vector Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03559)
*Yohei Kakimoto,Yuto Omae,Hirotaka Takahashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对RBF-SVR预测函数的非凸优化问题，提出了基于DC分解的DCA框架，发现单一标量$C_αρ$可预先刻画算法收敛性与初值依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高斯RBF核SVR预测函数的非凸优化问题，需要开发有效的优化算法并理解其收敛特性。由于RBF核具有解析结构，可借此构造显式DC分解，从而应用DCA求解并分析其理论性质。

Method: 利用RBF核的解析结构，构造目标函数的显式DC分解形式。推导出DC分量强凸性参数的下界$μ$和子问题梯度Lipschitz常数的上界$L$，两者均由训练后对偶系数和$C_α$、RBF核参数$γ$及DC分解参数$ρ$共同决定，且共享主导项$C_αρ$。在六个基准函数上进行数值实验验证。

Result: 证明了$μ$和$L$的闭式表达式，主导项均为$C_αρ$。数值实验表明$C_αρ$是刻画DCA收敛性质和初值依赖性的主要单一量，并分解为$C\to C_α$和$γ\to ρ$两条独立路径，其中SVR超参数$(C,γ)$主导$C_αρ$的变化。

Conclusion: RBF-SVR上DCA的收敛性质可通过单一标量$C_αρ$预先评估：训练前可从$(C,γ)$近似估计，训练后可闭式精确计算。该框架为RBF-SVR优化问题的DCA应用提供了完整的理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: For nonconvex optimization problems whose objective is the prediction function of a trained Support Vector Regression (SVR) model with the Gaussian radial basis function (RBF) kernel (RBF-SVR), we present a framework that applies the difference of convex functions (DC) algorithm (DCA) by exploiting the analytical structure of the RBF kernel to construct an explicit DC decomposition. Specifically, we derive in closed form both the lower bound $μ$ of the strong convexity parameter of the DC components and the upper bound $L$ of the gradient Lipschitz constant of the subproblem. Both $μ$ and $L$ are determined solely by the post-training dual-coefficient sum $C_α$ and the RBF kernel parameter $γ$, together with the DC decomposition parameter $ρ$, and they share a common leading term $C_αρ$. Through numerical experiments on six benchmark functions, we show that $C_αρ$ is the primary single quantity characterizing both the convergence properties and the initial-point dependence of DCA, and further demonstrate that it decomposes into two independent pathways, $C \to C_α$ and $γ\to ρ$, with its primary variation governed by the SVR hyperparameters $(C, γ)$. Together, these results allow the convergence properties of DCA on RBF-SVR to be assessed in advance through the single scalar quantity $C_αρ$: approximately from $(C, γ)$ before training, and exactly in closed form after training.

</details>


### [199] [Training a Predictive Coding Network on ImageNet using Equilibrium Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03584)
*Tugdual Kerjan,Rasmus Høier,Benjamin Scellier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次将平衡传播(EP)训练方法成功应用于ImageNet规模的预测编码网络(PCN)，在top-5分类任务上达到13.23%的测试错误率，接近反向传播基准的12.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡传播(EP)和预测编码网络(PCN)作为基于能量的模型，此前仅在小规模问题上得到验证，缺乏大规模应用的演示。本研究旨在开发EP训练方法应用于PCN，以扩展这两种方法的可扩展性。

Method: 开发了一种基于EP的PCN训练方法，结合了居中变体EP与针对PCN的新型平衡方案。使用该方法在完整的ImageNet数据集上训练了一个10层卷积PCN(VGG10)。

Result: 在ImageNet top-5分类任务上实现了13.23%的测试错误率，接近反向传播基准的12.2%。这是PCN和EP训练方法首次在ImageNet规模上的成功演示。

Conclusion: 该工作显著扩展了EP和PCN的可扩展性，表明EP框架在其他物理系统中扩展的主要挑战可能更多来自这些系统的计算特性，而非EP框架本身的固有局限性。

Abstract: Equilibrium Propagation (EP) is a physics-based training framework that has primarily been employed in energy-based models, including continuous Hopfield networks, nonlinear resistive networks and coupled phase oscillators. However, EP's practical applications have so far remained limited to relatively small-scale problems. Predictive coding networks (PCNs), another class of energy-based models rooted in computational neuroscience, are typically trained with a specialized algorithm and have likewise not yet been demonstrated at large scale. In this work, we develop an EP-based training method for PCNs which combines the centered variant of EP with a novel equilibration scheme for PCNs. Using this approach, we train a 10-layer convolutional PCN (VGG10) on full-size ImageNet, achieving 13.23\% test error rate on the top-5 classification task, close to the 12.2\% backpropagation baseline. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of both PCNs and EP-based training at ImageNet scale. These results significantly extend the scalability of both approaches and suggest that the primary challenges in scaling EP in other physical systems may come more from the computational properties of these systems than from inherent limitations of the EP framework.

</details>


### [200] [CauTion: Knowing When to Trust LLMs for Ensemble Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03602)
*Bo Peng,Kaiwen Wu,Sirui Chen,Zhiheng Wang,Yu Qiao,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CauTion框架，通过共识过滤和LLM可靠性估计，将大语言模型领域知识可靠地集成到统计因果发现算法集成中，在六组数据集上持续优于纯数据驱动和LLM增强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯统计因果发现方法存在等价类内统计不可区分性和对有限样本敏感等根本局限；现有LLM增强方法易受LLM错误影响且token成本高昂，单一算法结果易受算法特定偏差影响。

Method: 三阶段流程：(1)算法集成共识投票解决高达96%的一致边，实现近完美准确率；(2)无标注信任校准程序估计LLM与算法的相对可靠性，通过信任加权投票将LLM仲裁限制于算法证据不可靠的边；(3)循环修复保证最终因果图无环。

Result: 在六个数据集上持续优于纯数据和LLM增强基线，在更大规模图上提升更显著，对LLM错误具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CauTion框架通过共识过滤与信任校准仲裁机制，有效整合LLM领域知识与统计算法，解决了现有方法的脆弱性和成本问题，为因果发现提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data remains challenging due to the fundamental limitations of purely statistical methods, such as statistical distinguishability within equivalence classes and sensitivity to finite sample sizes. While large language models (LLMs) offer a promising source of domain knowledge to complement statistical inference, existing LLM-augmented methods are vulnerable to LLM errors and incur high token costs. Moreover, reliance on a single data-centric algorithm can make results sensitive to algorithm-specific biases. To address these limitations, we propose CauTion, a framework that reliably integrates LLM domain knowledge into an ensemble of statistical causal discovery algorithms through consensus filtering and LLM reliability estimation. CauTion proceeds in three stages. First, an algorithm ensemble utilizes a consensus voting to resolve up to 96% of edges on which algorithms agree, achieving near-perfect accuracy on the filtered consensus edges. Second, a trust-calibrated arbitration mechanism estimates the relative reliability of the LLM and the algorithms via an annotation-free trust calibration procedure, which is then utilized to govern a trust-weighted voting process that restricts LLM arbitration exclusively to edges with unreliable algorithmic evidence. Third, a cycle repair step is applied to guarantee the final causal graph is validly acyclic. Experiments on six datasets demonstrate that CauTion consistently outperforms both data-centric and LLM-augmented baselines, with larger gains on larger graphs and strong robustness to LLM errors. Code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauTion.

</details>


### [201] [Exploiting Verification-Generation Gap: Test-Time Reinforcement Learning with Confidence-Conditioned Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03608)
*Jiahui Li,Jianfeng Shan,Wenpei Chen,Shunyu Wu,Jian Lou,Wenjie Feng,Dan Li,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TTRL-CoCoV框架，通过置信度条件化验证机制解决了测试时强化学习中Pass@k优化不足的问题，在Pass@1和Pass@16上分别实现了+9.8%和+18.7%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时强化学习研究主要关注Pass@1性能，但在无标签设置下优化Pass@k（衡量生成覆盖率以维持持续探索）仍被忽视且极具挑战。直接应用适用于RLVR的Pass@k优势设计在无标签设置下效果不佳。

Method: 提出TTRL-CoCoV（测试时强化学习与置信度条件化验证）框架，基于"验证能力通常领先于生成能力"的关键洞察，采用置信度自适应机制：对高置信度样本，引导验证器并应用探索增强奖励以防止多样性坍塌；对低置信度样本，将伪标签选择委托给验证器以过滤错误伪标签；对中置信度样本，完全绕过验证。

Result: 在6个广泛认可的基准测试中，TTRL-CoCoV相比TTRL在Pass@1上平均提升+9.8%，在Pass@16上提升+18.7%；与全监督RL方法相比，在多个推理基准测试上Pass@1绝对提升高达+5.0%。

Conclusion: TTRL-CoCoV成功解决了无标签测试时强化学习中Pass@k优化的核心难题，通过置信度条件化验证机制有效扩展了Pass@k覆盖率并提升了Pass@1性能，证明了该方法在复杂推理任务中的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing the complex reasoning abilities of large language models in a completely label-free manner. Despite existing studies focusing on Pass@1 performance, optimizing Pass@k remains under-explored yet critical in label-free settings, which measures generation coverage for sustained exploration. Optimizing Pass@k in label-free setting is highly non-trivial, as directly applying the Pass@k advantage designs effective for RLVR yields unsatisfactory performance. Through in-depth empirical analysis, we discover the root causes hindering performance: pseudo-label estimations for low-confidence samples have a high probability of being incorrect, while candidate answers for high-confidence samples suffer from severe diversity collapse. To overcome these hurdles, we propose TTRL-CoCoV (Test-Time Reinforcement Learning with Confidence-Conditioned Verification), a novel confidence-adaptive framework that expands Pass@k coverage and improves Pass@1 performance. Based on our key insight that verification capability generally leads generation capability, TTRL-CoCoV employs a confidence-conditioned mechanism: for high-confidence samples, it bootstraps verifier and applies an exploration-enhancing reward to prevent diversity collapse; for low-confidence samples, it delegates pseudo-label selection to the verifier to filter incorrect pseudo-labels; and for medium-confidence samples, it bypasses verification entirely. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TTRL-CoCoV outperforms the best competing methods across 6 widely-recognized benchmarks, achieves average absolute gains of +9.8% in Pass@1 and +18.7% in Pass@16 over TTRL, and even achieves absolute Pass@1 improvements of up to +5.0% across multiple reasoning benchmarks when compared against fully supervised RL methods. Our code repository: https://github.com/shanjf666/CoCoV.

</details>


### [202] [Physics-Guided Policy Optimization with Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03620)
*Ke Wang,Yuning Wu,Haoran Liu,Chaoqun Jia,Devin Chen,Kai Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PGPO方法，通过互信息估计动态调节学习步长，解决了SDPO在LLM后训练中因固定步长导致的训练不稳定问题，在Science-QA数据集上最高提升4.5分且训练更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDPO方法中，模型基于特权信息的自我预测进行学习，但每次更新的可信度差异很大：某些批次的自我教师修正信息丰富，而其他批次可能产生误导。使用固定步长统一应用这些修正会导致训练不稳定，因此需要根据反馈质量动态调节步长。

Method: 受粘性流体动力学启发并在SDE层面形式化类比，提出PGPO方法。该方法引入信息调制的步长乘数，该乘数通过估计学生预测与反馈条件教师之间的互信息得到，从而实现对更新步长的自适应调节。该方法保持了vanilla SGD的一阶弱近似保证，且每次迭代开销可忽略。

Result: 在Science-QA数据集上的实验表明，PGPO在4个领域中的3个领域优于SDPO，增益最高达+4.5分；在SDPO于训练后期崩溃的设置中，PGPO仍能保持稳定训练。

Conclusion: PGPO通过物理启发的互信息调制步长策略，有效解决了SDPO的训练稳定性问题，在保持理论保证的同时显著提升了性能，为LLM后训练提供了一种更鲁棒的优化方法。

Abstract: Self-distilled policy optimization (SDPO) has become a popular paradigm for LLM post-training, where a model learns from its own predictions conditioned on privileged information. SDPO, however, is sensitive to how much each update step should be trusted: corrections from a self-teacher can be highly informative on some batches and misleading on others, and applying them uniformly with a fixed step size can destabilize training. Drawing inspiration from viscous-fluid dynamics and formalizing the analogy at the SDE level, we propose Physics-Guided Policy Optimization (PGPO), which introduces an information-modulated step-size multiplier derived from a mutual-information estimate between the student's predictions and the feedback-conditioned teacher. We show that this modulation preserves the order-1 weak-approximation guarantees of vanilla SGD, and incurs negligible overhead per iteration. We evaluate PGPO on the Science-QA dataset, where it outperforms SDPO on 3 of the 4 domains with gains of up to +4.5 points, while remaining stable in a setting where SDPO collapses late in training.

</details>


### [203] [AnchorMoE: Interpretable Time Series Classification via Anchor-Routed MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03631)
*Tao Xie,Zexi Tan,Haoyi Xiao,Mengke Li,Yiqun Zhang,Yang Lu,Cuie Yang,Yiu-ming Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AnchorMoE，一个基于混合专家(MoE)架构的可解释多变量时间序列分类框架，通过将预测精确分解为输入片段的加和，实现了内在透明性而非事后解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床诊断和工业故障检测等高风险领域，多变量时间序列分类需要透明决策；然而，现实时间序列中的判别信号通常稀疏、异质且被背景噪声严重遮蔽，隔离驱动模型预测的时间片段极具挑战性。

Method: 构建基于混合专家(MoE)的架构，对局部补丁的多视图表示进行编码并路由至专业化专家；引入几何正交性约束惩罚表示冗余，强制不同专家专注于异质预测模式；设计不确定性感知可靠性门动态校准各片段贡献，有效抑制背景噪声。

Result: 在真实世界和合成基准数据集上的大量实验表明，AnchorMoE在实现极具竞争力分类性能的同时，能够忠实地将决策锚定于原始时间序列。

Conclusion: AnchorMoE成功实现了分类精度与可解释性的平衡，通过构造性可解释设计和信号稀疏分布下的可靠性保障，为高风险领域的时间序列分类提供了安全部署基础。

Abstract: Multivariate time series classification (MTSC) is pivotal in high-stakes domains, such as clinical diagnosis and industrial fault detection, where safe deployment necessitates transparent decision-making. However, isolating the temporal segments that drive model predictions is challenging because discriminative signals in real-world time series are typically sparse, heterogeneous, and heavily obscured by background noise. This paper, therefore, proposes AnchorMoE, an interpretable-by-construction classification framework. Built upon a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture, AnchorMoE encodes multi-view representations of local patches and routes them to specialized experts, ensuring that the final prediction is formulated as an exact additive decomposition over the input segments, facilitating ante-hoc transparency rather than relying on post-hoc estimations. To maintain the reliability of this decomposition under sparse signal distributions, we introduce a geometric orthogonality constraint that penalizes representational redundancy, compelling distinct experts to specialize in heterogeneous predictive patterns. Furthermore, an uncertainty-aware reliability gate is designed to dynamically calibrate the contribution of each segment, effectively suppressing residual background noise. Extensive experiments on real-world and synthetic benchmarks demonstrate that AnchorMoE achieves highly competitive classification performance while faithfully grounding its decisions in the raw time series.

</details>


### [204] [Spatial Transcriptomics-Guided Alignment Enhances Molecular Profiling in Pathology Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03644)
*Fengtao Zhou,Yingxue Xu,Zhengyu Zhang,Yihui Wang,Zhengrui Guo,Ling Liang,Jiabo Ma,Cheng Jin,Ziyi Liu,Huajun Zhou,Hongyi Wang,Du Cai,Chenglong Zhao,Xi Wang,Can Yang,Yu Wang,Wenbin Li,Feng Gao,Zhe Wang,Zhenhui Li,Xiuming Zhang,Li Liang,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STAMP框架，利用空间转录组学引导病理基础模型对齐，实现从常规H&E图像推断分子表型，为精准肿瘤学提供低成本、高效的分子分型解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精准肿瘤学的分子分析受限于高昂成本、样本耗尽和长周转时间；当前病理基础模型主要依赖视觉中心的自监督学习或视觉-语言对齐，缺乏连接细微形态特征与潜在基因组改变所需的空间分辨分子监督。空间转录组学技术能够在完整组织切片内进行转录组定量，保留了组织学与分子谱之间的精确空间联系。

Method: 构建了涵盖多种解剖器官和测序平台的人类空间转录组数据集HumanST-1k，包含180万对H&E图像块和相应的转录组谱；提出STAMP框架，采用通路引导的对齐策略，将转录组数据聚合成具有生物学功能的通路，通过参数高效微调将分子信息整合入病理基础模型，丰富模型的表示空间。

Result: STAMP框架成功赋予病理基础模型内在分子感知能力，能够解析亚视觉分子特征；在多层次评估框架下验证了增强表示的临床实用性，证实了该方法在分子表型推断方面的有效性。

Conclusion: STAMP框架通过空间转录组学引导的对齐策略，有效桥接了组织形态与分子状态之间的鸿沟，为从常规H&E图像推断分子表型提供了新范式，在精准肿瘤学应用中具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Comprehensive molecular profiling is essential for modern precision oncology but remains hindered by prohibitive costs, specimen exhaustion, and protracted turnaround times. While pathology foundation models (PFMs) have demonstrated potential for inferring molecular phenotypes from routine hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) whole-slide images (WSIs), current architectures primarily rely on vision-centric self-supervised learning or vision-language alignment, lacking the spatially resolved molecular supervision required to connect subtle morphological features with underlying genomic alterations. Spatial transcriptomics (ST) emerges as a transformative technology that enables transcriptomic quantification within intact tissue sections, thereby preserving the precise spatial link between histology and molecular profiles. In this study, we present a Spatial Transcriptomics-guided Alignment framework for Molecular Profiling (STAMP), which endows PFMs with intrinsic molecular awareness. To support this paradigm, we curated HumanST-1k, a human ST dataset spanning diverse anatomical organs and sequencing platforms. This atlas yields 1.8 million pairs of H&E patches and corresponding transcriptomic profiles, providing a corpus that links histological structures with their molecular states. To mitigate the technical noise inherent to raw transcriptomics, STAMP applies a pathway-informed alignment strategy that aggregates transcriptomic data into biologically functional pathways, which are subsequently integrated into PFMs via parameter-efficient fine-tuning. This alignment enriches the representation space of PFMs and unlocks their capacity to resolve sub-visual molecular signatures. The clinical utility of these augmented representations was validated through a multi-tier evaluation framework.

</details>


### [205] [The Shape of Addition: Geometric Structures of Arithmetic in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03645)
*Liuyuan Wen,Xun Zhu,Lihao Huang,Wenbin Li,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过几何视角揭示大语言模型在算术运算中的脆弱性机制，提出IRST几何结构和噪声量化模型来解释算术错误，并开发了有效的错误检测与纠正方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在基础算术运算中表现出反常的脆弱性，这暗示了其内部计算与离散输出之间存在脱节。研究者希望理解这种算术错误的内在机制，探究为什么模型在处理简单的多操作数加法时会产生错误。

Method: 研究采用几何分析方法，在多操作数加法过程中分析残差流的几何结构。具体包括：1）识别并定义Iso-Raw-Sum Trajectory (IRST)几何结构，该结构由语义数字锚定表征并通过连续进位纤维进行调节；2）提出噪声量化模型，将算术错误解释为由内部神经噪声导致的几何滑动；3）利用几何一致性检查方法进行验证和纠正。

Result: 研究发现了表征算术运算的IRST几何结构，成功将算术错误机制化为几何滑动现象，即内部神经噪声推动连续潜在进位电位跨越量化阈值。同时揭示了探测器多功能性的原理，解释了轻量级探测器如何从单一激活向量中解耦共存的各种潜在信号（如真实值与幻觉）。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一个完整的几何框架来理解和处理大语言模型的算术错误问题，不仅从理论层面解释了错误的产生机制，还开发了实用的几何一致性检查方法，能够有效地在推理过程中检测和纠正量化失败，为大语言模型的可靠性提升提供了新的理论基础和技术手段。

Abstract: Large Language Models exhibit paradoxical fragility in fundamental arithmetic, implying a disconnect between internal computation and discrete output. By analyzing the residual stream geometry during multi-operand addition, we identify the Iso-Raw-Sum Trajectory (IRST), a geometric structure where representations are anchored by semantic digits and modulated by continuous carry fibers. We propose the Noisy Quantization Model to explain this geometry, framing arithmetic errors as Geometric Slippages caused by internal neural noise pushing a continuous, latent Carry Potential across quantization thresholds. This geometric framework further elucidates Probe Versatility, explaining how lightweight probes can disentangle coexisting latent signals (such as ground truth versus hallucination) from a single activation vector. Finally, we validate these insights through a geometric consistency check method that effectively detects and corrects these quantization failures during inference. Our code is available at https://github.com/RL-MIND/Shape-of-Addition.

</details>


### [206] [Speedrunning Tabular Foundation Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03681)
*Salih Bora Ozturk,Alexander Pfefferle,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为表格基础模型预训练引入了社区竞速基准，通过修改单文件训练脚本竞争达到下游ROC AUC目标的时间。目前最佳记录在0.92分钟内达成目标，相比74.32分钟的基线实现81倍加速，同时使用的合成数据集减少22倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练成本是表格基础模型研究的主要瓶颈，延缓了新架构、先验和优化思想的迭代周期。然而，社区缺乏一种简单的方式来比较和积累预训练加速成果。

Method: 引入nanoTabPFN社区竞速机制：贡献者修改单文件训练脚本，在一个NVIDIA L40S GPU上竞争达到TabArena子样本上固定下游ROC AUC目标的时间。竞速格式为社区提供了添加、验证和堆叠预训练改进的简单协议。

Result: 当前最佳记录在0.92分钟内达到目标ROC AUC，相比74.32分钟的基线实现81倍加速，同时使用的合成数据集减少22倍。排行榜对社区贡献开放。

Conclusion: 竞速格式为社区提供了简单协议来添加、验证和堆叠预训练改进，排行榜持续开放接受贡献。代码和记录已在GitHub公开。

Abstract: Pretraining cost is a major bottleneck for research on tabular foundation models, slowing the iteration cycle for new architectures, priors, and optimization ideas. Yet the community lacks a simple way to compare and accumulate pretraining speedups. We introduce a community speedrun for nanoTabPFN: contributors modify a single-file training script and compete to reach a fixed downstream ROC AUC target on subsampled TabArena using one NVIDIA L40S GPU. The current best record reaches the target in 0.92 minutes, an 81x speedup over the 74.32 minute baseline while using 22x fewer synthetic datasets. The speedrun format provides a simple protocol for the community to add, verify, and stack pretraining improvements, with the leaderboard open to contributions. Code and records are available at https://github.com/borawhocodess/modded-nanotabpfn.

</details>


### [207] [A Close Look At World Model Recovery In Supervised Fine-Tuned LLM Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03685)
*Patrick Emami,Nan Qiang,Peter Graf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过可解释性实验探究了经过监督微调（SFT）的大语言模型在经典规划任务中是否真正习得了世界模型，发现SFT能使模型线性编码动作有效性和部分状态谓词，且更广的状态空间覆盖有助于世界模型的恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管监督微调（SFT）能提升大语言模型在经典规划任务中的端到端性能，但目前尚不清楚这些模型是否真正学会了表示和推理其所解决的规划问题。由于经典规划问题的相对复杂性以及端到端规划生成对LLMs构成的挑战，这一问题难以探索。因此，本研究旨在揭示经过微调的LLMs是否以及在何种程度上恢复了底层的“世界模型”。

Method: 设计并实施了一系列可解释性实验，通过考察微调后LLMs的内部表示和生成能力，全面审视其世界模型的恢复情况。实验关注动作有效性的线性编码、状态谓词的表示，以及训练数据中状态空间覆盖（如随机游走数据）对世界模型恢复的影响。

Result: 研究发现：a) 在有效动作序列上进行监督微调能使LLMs线性编码动作有效性及部分状态谓词；b) 即使模型在输出概率上难以区分动作有效性，其内部表示仍可能将有效与无效动作区分开；c) 微调阶段更广的状态空间覆盖（如通过随机游走数据获得）能带来更准确的世界模型恢复。

Conclusion: 本工作提供了一套将可解释性技术应用于规划LLMs的方法论，并生成了关于知识如何在LLMs中表示的洞见，为理解LLMs在规划任务中的知识表征机制提供了新见解。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) improves end-to-end classical planning in large language models (LLMs), but do these models also learn to represent and reason about the planning problems they are solving? Due to the relative complexity of classical planning problems and the challenge that end-to-end plan generation poses for LLMs, it has been difficult to explore this question. In our work, we devise and perform a series of interpretability experiments that holistically interrogate world model recovery by examining both internal representations and generative capabilities of fine-tuned LLMs. We find that: a) Supervised fine-tuning on valid action sequences enables LLMs to linearly encode action validity and some state predicates. b) Models that struggle to use output probabilities for classifying action validity may still learn internal representations that separate valid from invalid actions. c) Broader state space coverage during fine-tuning, such as from random walk data, yields more accurate recovery of the underlying world model. In summary, this work contributes a recipe for applying interpretability techniques to planning LLMs and generates insights that shed light on open questions about how knowledge is represented in LLMs.

</details>


### [208] [Staying Alive: Uncensored Survival Analysis with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03689)
*Mariana Vargas Vieyra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种免训练的生存分析方法，利用表格基础模型(TFM)构建加速失效时间(AFT)模型，并基于Buckley-James估计器处理右删失数据，无需特定数据集的参数拟合即可达到与传统训练模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存分析在医疗和客户流失预测等领域应用广泛，但面临右删失（right-censoring）的关键挑战。虽然表格基础模型(TFM)在预测任务中表现出色，但将其应用于时间-事件数据预测时，右删失问题仍然是一个难点。现有的生存回归模型需要针对特定数据集进行训练，缺乏一种能够直接利用TFM处理删失数据的免训练方法。

Method: 提出了一种免训练的生存回归方法，利用TFM构建加速失效时间(AFT)模型。该方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 使用TFM构建AFT模型，仅需拟合单个标量参数而无需其他训练；(2) 基于Buckley-James估计器，引入非参数上下文估计器来处理右删失数据，通过迭代插补方式预测事件发生时间。

Result: 在标准生存分析基准测试上的实验表明，该方法与多种需要训练的参数化和半参数生存回归模型（包括Cox回归和参数化AFT模型）具有竞争力，能够有效处理右删失数据并实现准确的事件时间预测。

Conclusion: 本研究成功实现了将表格基础模型应用于生存分析任务，通过免训练的方式解决了右删失问题。所提出的方法无需数据集特定的参数拟合，仅通过单个标量参数即可达到与传统训练模型相当的性能，为生存分析领域提供了一种高效且实用的新方法。

Abstract: Survival Analysis (SA) is a statistical framework that models the time span until some event of interest occurs. Widely used in several domains, including healthcare and churn prediction, a central challenge in its applicability stems from the time of the event being partially observed or \emph{right-censoring}.
  Tabular Foundation Models (TFM) have attracted significant interest in recent years due to their ability to perform prediction tasks in a single forward pass, requiring no dataset-specific parameter fitting. Despite their success, their application to prediction tasks on time-to-event data remains difficult due to right censoring. In this work, we present a training-free method to survival regression by leveraging TFMs to both predict the time of the event and iteratively impute right-censored data.
  Our method uses a TFM to construct an Accelerated Failure Time (AFT) model requiring no training beyond fitting a single scalar parameter. Subsequently, by building on the Buckley-James estimator, we introduce a non-parametric in-context estimator for right-censored data. Our experiments on standard survival analysis benchmarks show that our method is competitive with several parametric and semi-parametric survival regression models that require training, including Cox regression and parametric AFT models.

</details>


### [209] [Multi$^2$: Hierarchical Multi-Agent Decision-Making with LLM-Based Agents in Interactive Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03698)
*Sangeun Park,Minhae Kwon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Multi²分层多智能体决策框架，通过将高层目标生成与低层动作执行分离，有效缓解了LLM智能体在长期交互中的目标漂移问题，并发布了三个分层基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体在长期决策中存在目标漂移(objective drift)问题，即目标和计划在长时间交互过程中逐渐偏离，导致长期决策能力脆弱。

Method: 提出Multi²分层多代理框架，将智能体行为分解为互补角色：高层智能体(System 1)通过监督微调(SFT)进行上下文感知的子目标生成；低层智能体(System 2)通过离线到在线强化学习(RL)在交互环境中执行原子动作。

Result: 在多种交互环境中，Multi²持续优于强基线智能体，在多轮交互中展现出更强的鲁棒性和协调性。同时发布了三个分层基准数据集，填补了LLM智能体分层决策训练和评估的空白。

Conclusion: 通过明确分离高层规划和低层执行，Multi²框架成功实现了稳定的长期控制，缓解了目标漂移问题，并实现了高效适应，为构建能够规划、行动和适应动态环境的智能体系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: A central goal of large language model (LLM) research is to build agentic systems that can plan, act, and adapt through sustained interaction with dynamic environments. While recent LLM-based agents exhibit impressive contextual reasoning, their long-horizon decision-making remains fragile, often suffering from objective drift, where goals and plans drift over extended interactions. We introduce Multi$^2$, a hierarchical multi-agent decision-making framework that explicitly decomposes agent behavior into complementary roles. A high-level agent (System 1) focuses on context-aware sub-goal generation using supervised fine-tuning (SFT), while a low-level agent (System 2) executes atomic actions through offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) in interactive environments. This separation enables stable long-horizon control, mitigates objective drift, and allows efficient adaptation. Across diverse interactive environments, Multi$^2$ consistently outperforms strong agentic baselines, demonstrating improved robustness and coordination in multi-turn interaction. Beyond performance, we introduce and release three hierarchical benchmark datasets, filling a long-standing gap in training and evaluating hierarchical decision-making for LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [210] [When Graph Tokens Sink: A Mechanistic Analysis of Graph Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03712)
*Ding Zhang,Runtao Zhou,Wenqing Zheng,Rizal Fathony,Bayan Bruss,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现图语言模型(GLMs)中存在"图汇令牌"现象，这些令牌在激活值上表现突出但并非图信息的核心载体，揭示了当前图令牌构建方法中激活显著性与其语义效用之间的解耦问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管图语言模型(GLMs)已成为将大语言模型适配到图学习任务的有前景的方向，但目前尚不清楚LLMs如何在内部解释图令牌，以及图令牌是否真正作为图结构的有意义载体。这一认知空白限制了对GLMs工作机制的理解和模型优化。

Method: 通过分析代表性GLM架构中图令牌的行为，采用剪枝、重定位和交换干预等实验方法，研究LLMs如何处理图信息，重点关注图汇令牌(graph sink tokens)的激活模式和注意力权重分布。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 图汇令牌在激活值上表现为异常值，具有大量激活值和偏向早期图令牌位置的特征；(2) 这些令牌不一定是图信息的主要载体，与经典注意力汇不同，它们不吸引最大的注意力权重；(3) 通过干预实验证明图汇令牌不是下游预测中最重要的语义或结构令牌。

Conclusion: 当前GLMs将图结构映射到LLM令牌空间后，生成的图令牌表示并不能自然形成完全可用的拓扑感知内部表示，而是表现出激活显著性与图语义效用之间的解耦，这揭示了现有图令牌构建、放置和对齐机制的局限性。

Abstract: Graph Language Models (GLMs) have become a promising direction for adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to graph learning tasks. By transforming graph topology and node information into graph tokens, GLMs allow LLMs to jointly process structured graph inputs and textual instructions. Yet, it remains unclear how LLMs internally interpret these graph tokens and whether graph tokens act as meaningful carriers of graph structure. In this work, we analyze how LLMs process graph information through graph-token behavior in representative GLM architectures.
  Findings. We find that the internal saliency of graph tokens in GLMs is not equivalent to graph information utilization. Graph sink tokens consistently emerge as activation-level outliers: they can be identified by massive activation values along a small set of hidden-state dimensions and are biased toward early graph-token positions. However, this activation-level saliency does not imply that these tokens are the main carriers of graph information. Unlike classical attention sinks in language and vision-language models, graph sink tokens do not necessarily attract the largest attention weights from query tokens. Through pruning, repositioning, and swapping interventions, we show that graph sink tokens are not the most important semantic or structural tokens for downstream prediction.
  Implications. Together, these results suggest that after current GLMs map graph structure into the LLM token space, the resulting graph-token representations do not naturally form a fully usable topology-aware internal representation; instead, they exhibit a decoupling between activation-level saliency and graph-semantic utility. This decoupling points to limitations in existing graph-token construction, placement, and alignment mechanisms.

</details>


### [211] [Compress then Merge: From Multiple LoRAs into One Low-Rank Adapter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03723)
*Zhengbao He,Ruiqi Ding,Zhehao Huang,Ruikai Yang,Tao Li,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CtM（先压缩再合并）流程，通过先在共享低秩子空间中投影，再进行合并，有效解决了多个LoRA适配器合并时的秩约束问题，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA使基础模型的参数高效专门化成为可能，但大量任务特定的适配器导致能力碎片化，难以复用和部署。现有的Merge-then-Compress流程在全参数空间合并后压缩会破坏低秩结构，难以恢复有效的低秩LoRA。

Method: 提出Compress-then-Merge（CtM）流程，先计算共享的r维子空间以捕获跨适配器的共同结构，将每个适配器投影到共享子空间获得r×r坐标，再在此缩减空间应用标准合并规则。该方法天然保证输出为秩-r的LoRA，避免了事后截断。

Result: 实验表明CtM在多个模型和任务上始终优于现有的单LoRA输出基线方法，同时缩小了与全参数合并方法的性能差距。

Conclusion: CtM通过强制在合并前先通过秩-r瓶颈，保证了低秩结构，避免了全参数合并后截断的信息损失，为多适配器合并提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) enables parameter-efficient specialization of foundation models, but the proliferation of task-specific adapters fragments capabilities across many adapters, complicating reuse and deployment. We study the problem of merging $T$ LoRAs into a single rank-$r$ LoRA, thereby preserving the benefits of low-rank structure. Existing Merge-then-Compress pipelines treat the rank constraint as an afterthought: they merge adapters in the full parameter space, then compress the merged result to rank $r$ via truncated SVD. However, full-parameter merging may destroy the low-rank structure, making it difficult for subsequent compression to recover an effective rank-$r$ LoRA. We propose Compress-then-Merge (CtM), a reversed pipeline that enforces the rank-$r$ bottleneck before merging: CtM computes shared $r$-dimensional subspaces using only the LoRA weights to capture cross-adapter common structure, projects each adapter into the shared subspaces to obtain $r\times r$ coordinates, and then applies standard merging rules in this reduced space. CtM guarantees a rank-$r$ LoRA by construction, avoiding post-hoc truncation, and enables efficient computation in the core space spanned by concatenated LoRA factors. Experiments across multiple models and tasks show that CtM consistently outperforms existing single-LoRA-output baselines while narrowing the performance gap to full-parameter merging methods.

</details>


### [212] [Conformal Language Modeling via Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03731)
*Nicolas Emmenegger,Theo X. Olausson,Armando Solar-Lezama,Chara Podimata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有事后过滤方法消除大语言模型幻觉存在的缺陷，本文提出一种从校准的高分区域近似后验中采样的新方法，在保持相同统计保证的同时提高了生成内容的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于共形预测的事后统计方法通过手术式修改样本来消除幻觉，这种过滤与生成过程的脱节会导致样本不连贯、不一致或不符合模型分布，且无法将概率质量转移到更有用的响应上。

Method: 提出从大语言模型后验的近似分布中进行采样，其中条件事件对应于一个校准的高分区域；开发了针对条件序列生成场景的校准程序，有效识别该区域并实现目标风险控制。

Result: 在开放式传记生成和数学问题求解的案例研究中，相比先前工作获得了相同的统计保证，同时具有更高的下游实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功弥合了过滤与生成之间的差距，在保持理论保证的同时提升了生成质量，为解决大语言模型幻觉问题提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models remain plagued by hallucinations. Recent work has sought to tame their prevalence using statistical techniques based on conformal prediction, with both theoretical and empirical success. However, these methods operate in a post-hoc fashion, treating the sampling procedure itself as atomic and then surgically altering samples to remove hallucinated claims. This disconnect between filtering and generation can result in samples that are incoherent, inconsistent, or simply unlikely under the model itself. Moreover, post-hoc surgery is unable to shift probability mass towards more useful and helpful responses. To address these issues, we propose to instead sample from approximations to an LLM posterior, where the conditioning event corresponds to a calibrated, high-scoring region. We develop a calibration procedure tailored to the setting of conditional sequential generation that effectively identifies this region and achieves target risk control. Empirically, we apply our method to case studies focused on open-ended biography generation and mathematical problem solving; compared to prior work, we obtain the same statistical guarantees, with higher downstream utility.

</details>


### [213] [Tool-Aware Optimization with Entropy Guidance for Efficient Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03762)
*Hongye Cao,Nuo Yan,Haoyuan Deng,Ziwei Wang,Tianpei Yang,Jing Huo,Yuyao Zhang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TAO-RL框架，通过工具感知轨迹过滤和熵引导探索解决智能体强化学习中工具集成导致的训练不稳定问题，在多个基准测试上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型的智能体强化学习中，外部工具的集成往往会导致训练不稳定：过度依赖工具会引起输入分布偏移，而过于保守的工具使用则限制了有效探索。这一困境促使研究者寻找一种既能保持训练稳定性又能促进有效探索的方法。

Method: 提出TAO-RL统一框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)数据层面的工具感知轨迹过滤，剔除工具调用全部执行失败的轨迹以及所有rollout都正确或都错误的轨迹，保留既具备工具能力又富含信息的训练数据；(2)算法层面的工具感知熵引导奖励，在工具调用后的token位置重塑优势函数，鼓励策略在关键决策点探索更多样化的推理路径。这两个组件相互强化，共同优化策略学习。

Result: 在3种模型规模下的7个挑战性推理基准测试上进行广泛实验，TAO-RL相比现有方法展现出显著优越性，证明了该方法在复杂推理任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: TAO-RL框架成功解决了智能体强化学习中工具集成的训练不稳定问题，通过轨迹过滤和熵引导探索的协同作用，建立了高质量训练分布并增强了关键工具交互节点的推理能力，为大语言模型的工具使用强化学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) equips large language models (LLMs) with tool-use capabilities that substantially improve reasoning on complex tasks. However, integrating external tools often destabilizes training: over-reliance on tools can induce input distribution shift, while overly conservative tool use limits effective exploration. To address this issue, we propose a unified framework TAO-RL that couples tool-aware trajectory filtering with entropy-guided exploration for efficient policy optimization. Specifically, at the data level, TAO-RL filters rollout trajectories along two criteria: discarding those where all tool invocations fail to execute, and removing those where all rollouts are either correct or incorrect, as both cases yield degenerate advantage estimates that contribute no discriminative learning signal. This joint filtering retains data that are both tool-capable and informative, establishing a high-quality training distribution. At the algorithmic level, we introduce a tool-aware entropy-guided bonus that reshapes the advantage function at post-tool-call tokens, encouraging the policy to explore more diverse reasoning paths at critical decision points. These two components are mutually reinforcing: trajectory filtering establishes a clean and informative training foundation, while entropy-guided exploration drives stronger reasoning behaviors at critical tool-interaction junctures. Extensive experiments on 7 challenging reasoning benchmarks across 3 model scales demonstrate the superiority of TAO-RL over existing methods.

</details>


### [214] [Limit Analysis of Graph Neural Networks with Wireless Conflict Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03794)
*Romina Garcia Camargo,Zhiyang Wang,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了基于稀疏随机几何图(RGG)的图神经网络(GNN)可迁移性理论，证明了在小规模图上训练的模型可泛化至大规模无线网络，并在链路调度问题上验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN在无线资源分配中表现出强大的图结构建模能力，其可迁移性使得在小规模图上训练的模型能够泛化到大规模部署而性能损失很小。无线网络具有稀疏性特征（单节点仅连接少量其他用户），但现有研究缺乏针对稀疏图结构的GNN可迁移性理论保障。

Method: 研究者聚焦于用于建模链路间干扰的RGG冲突图，利用随机几何图(RGG)与确定性网格图(DGG)之间的逼近性，建立了当模型跨尺度迁移时的性能损失边界理论框架。通过分析稀疏随机几何图的拓扑性质，推导出可迁移性的理论界限。

Result: 建立了GNN在稀疏随机几何图上的可迁移性理论边界，证明了从DGG到RGG的逼近误差可被有效控制。在链路调度问题上的实验验证表明，学习到的策略在大规模场景下持续优于现有基准方法。同时分析了理论假设对实证性能的影响。

Conclusion: 本研究为GNN在稀疏无线网络中的可迁移性提供了首个理论保障，填补了GNN在无线资源分配应用中的理论基础空白。所提理论框架不仅解释了GNN的泛化能力，还为实际大规模无线网络部署中的模型迁移提供了可靠的理论指导。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have emerged as a powerful tool for wireless resource allocation that leverages the underlying graph structure of communication networks. Their transferability property enables models trained on small-scale graphs to generalize to large-scale deployments with little performance deterioration, a desirable property for currently growing networks. Wireless networks are sparse regimes, where a single node is connected to a small number of other users. This work establishes theoretical results for transferability of GNNs over graphs derived from sparse Random Geometric Graphs (RGGs). In particular, we focus on conflict graphs of RGGs used to model interference among links. Our approach considers the closeness between RGGs and Deterministic Grid Graphs (DGG) to establish bounds in the performance loss when a model is transferred across scales. We validate our theoretical findings through the problem of link scheduling, demonstrating that our learned policies consistently outperform existing benchmarks at scale. Finally, we examine the impact of our theoretical assumptions on empirical performance.

</details>


### [215] [Trading Human Curation for Synthetic Augmentation in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03800)
*Akshansh,Leonardo Rosa Rodrigues,Michael Korostelev,Youssef Hassan,Mark E. Whiting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究验证了使用门控筛选的增强任务替代人工标注任务用于智能体语言模型RLVR训练的可行性，发现合成任务在成本调整后的替代比率达到1.4×-11.6×，且不损失泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可验证奖励(RLVR)在智能体语言模型上训练时面临高质量任务供给瓶颈：每个任务需要沙箱环境、提示词和人工编写奖励函数，达到质量标准的任务才能产生有效训练信号。人工筛选在此质量标准下无法经济地扩展到RL训练所需的任务数量，且自动生成任务变体与人工编写任务之间的替代率尚未确立。

Method: 提出使用预定义的、经门控筛选的增强任务来替代额外人工筛选任务。形式化定义了成本调整替代率$ρ_{\text{cost}}$来衡量增强任务与人工任务的权衡关系；通过控制消融实验，在具有不同增强比例的训练语料库上测量该替代率；刻画增强流水线的端到端经济学特性。

Result: 使用增强内容替代额外人工编写任务，在涵盖代码、指令遵循、推理和多轮智能体函数调用的10个基准测试套件上保持了整体留出泛化性能。在合理的$c_{\text{human}}/c_{\text{aug}}$范围内，门控合成任务与人工编写RLVR任务之间的成本调整替代率$ρ_{\text{cost}}$保持在[1.4×, 11.6×]区间内。

Conclusion: 通过门控筛选的任务增强可以经济有效地替代人工筛选，为RLVR任务供给瓶颈提供了可扩展的解决方案，在保持模型泛化能力的同时显著降低了任务构建成本。

Abstract: The supply of high-quality training tasks is a central bottleneck for reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) on agentic language models. Each task requires a sandboxed setup, a prompt, and a hand-authored reward function, and only tasks that pass a quality bar produce useful training signal. Hand-curation at this quality bar does not scale economically to the task counts effective RL training requires, and the substitution rate between automatically generated task variants and human-authored ones is not yet established. We investigate using pre-specified, gate-filtered augmentations of a small hand-authored base as a substitute for additional human curation during RLVR. We formalize the cost-adjusted trade rate $ρ_{\text{cost}}$ between augmented and human-authored tasks, measure it through a controlled ablation across training corpora with varying augmentation share, and characterize the end-to-end economics of the augmentation pipeline. Substituting augmented content for additional human-authored tasks retains aggregate held-out generalization on a ten-benchmark suite spanning code, instruction following, reasoning, and multi-turn agentic function-calling. The cost-adjusted trade rate $ρ_{\text{cost}}$ between gated synthetic and human-authored RLVR tasks stays in $[1.4\times, 11.6\times]$ across the plausible $c_{\text{human}}/c_{\text{aug}}$ range.

</details>


### [216] [Easy-to-Use Shielding for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03804)
*Stefan Pranger,Bettina Könighofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了tempestpy，一个将形式化盾牌合成工具Tempest集成到Gymnasium API的Python库，使强化学习从业者能够轻松使用盾牌技术实现安全探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盾牌技术虽然在安全探索方面具有潜力，但由于缺乏连接形式化盾牌合成与标准RL框架的端到端基础设施，导致其在RL中应用受限，通常需要形式化方法专业知识和大量工程工作。

Method: 将盾牌合成工具Tempest扩展为RL安全后端，开发tempestpy库将其集成到Gymnasium API中；扩展Tempest的算法支持以计算随机多人博弈的可靠盾牌；引入MiniGridSafe环境集合以促进建模和实验。

Result: 成功实现了盾牌合成与部署在现有RL流水线中的端到端工作流程，通过多个环境的实验对比验证了盾牌RL与非盾牌RL的效果，降低了盾牌技术的使用门槛。

Conclusion: tempestpy成功地将形式化安全探索方法转化为RL从业者可用的组件，通过提供可访问的基础设施和实验环境，推动了盾牌技术在强化学习实践中的应用。

Abstract: Safe exploration is a key challenge in Reinforcement Learning (RL) that aims to prevent agents from making harmful decisions while exploring their environment. Safe exploration is a key challenge in Reinforcement Learning (RL) that aims to prevent agents from making harmful decisions while exploring their environment. Shielding is one such technique that assumes domain knowledge in the form of an environment model to decide upon action safety. Although well-established, shielding has seen limited adoption in RL due to the lack of accessible end-to-end infrastructure connecting formal shield synthesis with standard RL frameworks. Applying shielding typically requires expertise in formal methods and substantial engineering effort, keeping it outside the typical RL workflow. We address this by extending our shield synthesis tool Tempest into a practical backend for safe RL. Our core contribution is tempestpy, a Python library that integrates Tempest-based shield synthesis directly into the Gymnasium API, allowing shields to be synthesized and deployed within existing RL pipelines. This lowers the barrier to entry for shielding and turns formal safe-exploration methods into a usable component for RL practitioners. We also extend Tempest's algorithmic support to compute sound shields for stochastic multiplayer games, preserving formal safety guarantees. We demonstrate the resulting workflow end to end and evaluate shielded and unshielded RL across multiple environments. To facilitate modeling, we provide symbolic models for MiniGrid and introduce MiniGridSafe, a collection of playground environments designed to make shielding easily accessible and experimentally transparent. MiniGridSafe extends MiniGrid with safety-oriented scenarios featuring probabilistic transitions and additional agents, enabling the study of challenging safety aspects in a simple and intuitive setting.

</details>


### [217] [PURGE: Projected Unlearning via Retain-Guided Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03808)
*Vedant Jawandhia,Daksh Ahuja,Ghufran Alam Siddiqui,Prashant Trivedi,Yash Sinha,Pratik Narang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PURGE算法，通过利用持续学习与机器遗忘的对偶性，结合梯度投影、多层表示擦除和保留混淆目标，在五个数据集上实现了高保留准确率（>96%）和接近理想的隐私保护（MIA AUROC≈0.5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法在平衡隐私保护与模型效用方面存在不足。作者发现持续学习（CL）与机器遗忘（MU）本质上是一对对偶问题：CL试图学习新任务而不遗忘旧任务，MU试图擦除特定数据而不损害保留数据性能。这一观察启发作者借鉴持续学习技术来解决机器遗忘问题。

Method: PURGE算法包含三个核心组件：（1）梯度投影约束：借鉴A-GEM方法，确保每次遗忘步骤不增加保留集损失；（2）多层表示擦除：将中间层的遗忘集激活推向保留分布，从隐藏表示层面移除信息；（3）保留混淆目标：不采用均匀分布目标，而是匹配模型在保留数据上的自然混淆模式，使遗忘模型难以与从头训练的模型区分。此外设计了两个自调节停止准则（保留损失预算和遗忘准确率目标）实现自动停止。

Result: 在五个数据集（CIFAR-10、MNIST、SVHN、STL10、PathMNIST）的22个类别级遗忘任务中，PURGE一致保持保留准确率高于96%，同时实现MIA AUROC接近0.5（理想值），在隐私-效用前沿上优于梯度上升、KL-uniform和多个已发表基线方法。

Conclusion: PURGE通过系统性地利用持续学习与机器遗忘的对偶性，成功解决了机器遗忘中的隐私-效用权衡问题。该方法不仅实现了优异的遗忘性能和保留性能平衡，还通过自动停止机制消除了手动调参的需求，为实际部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We propose PURGE, a machine unlearning algorithm built on a simple but an under-exploited observation: continual learning (CL) and machine unlearning (MU) which are fundamentally dual problems. CL tries to learn new tasks without forgetting old ones; MU tries to erase specific data without hurting retained performance representing the same underlying tension in opposite directions. PURGE leverages this duality by adapting gradient projection from A-GEM (Chaudhry et al., 2019) so that every unlearning step is constrained to not increase the retain-set loss. On top of this, it performs multi-layer representation erasure, pushing forget-set activations in intermediate layers towards the retain distribution to remove information from hidden representations rather than just suppressing it at the output. A key design choice is the retain-confusion target: rather than pushing forget outputs toward the uniform distribution, which we found to be surprisingly easy for membership inference attacks to detect, we instead target the model's natural confusion pattern on retain data. This makes the unlearned model hard to distinguish from one retrained from scratch. Two self-regulating stopping criteria (a retain-loss budget and a forget-accuracy target) let the algorithm decide on its own when to stop, removing the need for manual epoch tuning. In experiments on five datasets (CIFAR-10, MNIST, SVHN, STL10, PathMNIST) across 22 class-level forgetting tasks, PURGE consistently keeps retain accuracy above 96% while achieving MIA AUROC close to 0.5 (the ideal), outperforming gradient ascent, KL-uniform, and several published baselines on the privacy-utility frontier.

</details>


### [218] [TreeFlash: Parallel AR-Approximation for Faster Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03819)
*Peer Rheinboldt,Frédéric Berdoz,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TreeFlash通过引入MLP层和两阶段近似机制，解决了one-shot块起草器在树状起草中的非自回归分布偏差问题，在保持O(1)时间复杂度的同时实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: One-shot块起草器虽然通过消除序列token生成实现了高吞吐量，但其非自回归条件方式（仅依赖前缀上下文）导致起草分布随着起草深度增加而偏离验证器的真实自回归分布。这一问题在树状起草中更为严重，因为不同分支被迫共享后续token的边际分布。

Method: 提出TreeFlash方法，通过引入一个MLP层，基于起草器的隐藏状态和前一个token来近似自回归分布；采用两阶段近似机制，在保持one-shot起草器O(1)解码时间复杂度的同时实现分布修正。

Result: TreeFlash在多种任务和模型上达到最先进性能，相比边际树起草方法，块效率提高12%，加速比提高9%。

Conclusion: TreeFlash成功解决了one-shot块起草器在树状起草中的分布偏差问题，在保持时间复杂度优势的同时显著提升了性能，为推测解码提供了一种有效的改进方案。

Abstract: One-shot block drafters for speculative decoding generate the full draft in a single forward pass, achieving strong throughput by eliminating sequential token generation. However, they predict each draft token conditioned only on the prefix context, with no dependence on previously drafted tokens. This non-autoregressive conditioning causes the drafter's distribution to diverge from the verifier's true autoregressive distribution as draft depth grows. This problem becomes more severe in tree-based drafting, where distinct branches are forced to share the same marginal distribution for subsequent tokens. We propose TreeFlash, which addresses this by incorporating an MLP layer conditioned on the drafter's hidden state and the previous token to approximate an autoregressive distribution. TreeFlash retains the $\mathcal{O}(1)$ decoding time complexity of one-shot drafters by employing a two-stage approximation mechanism. TreeFlash achieves state-of-the-art performance across a variety of tasks and models, improving over marginal tree drafting by $12\%$ higher block efficiency and $9\%$ higher speedup.

</details>


### [219] [Finding Needles in the Haystack: Transductive Active Labeling in Ecology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03821)
*Rupa Kurinchi-Vendhan,Sara Beery*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出生态数据主动学习应采用转导式评估而非归纳式评估，关注长尾稀有类的发现，提出保守混合停止准则以减少过早停止，提升稀有类恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习在生态数据标注中采用归纳式评估（预测性能），与生态任务目标（转导式标注整个数据池）不一致，且忽视人在回路会低估继续标注的重要性，尤其对生态重要但稀有的长尾类别（稀有物种、罕见行为等）。

Method: 分析归纳式与转导式评估在长尾生态数据上的差异，提出量化采样难度的新指标来刻画稀有类在潜在空间密集区域中的发现难度，并受生态稀疏曲线启发，提出结合预测性能与发现准则的保守混合停止准则。

Result: 忽略人在回路会低估继续标注的价值；转导目标将重要性从预测转向发现，真正挑战在于在潜在几何结构中从密集区域发现稀有类别的“针”；混合停止准则减少了长尾数据池上的过早停止，在发现成为瓶颈时提升了稀有类恢复。

Conclusion: 在生态数据的主动学习实践中，应采用转导式评估，关注稀有类别的发现而非仅预测性能，通过保守混合停止准则可有效避免过早停止，改善稀有类别恢复，从而更好地满足生态监测与研究需求。

Abstract: Active learning is now standard practice in labeling ecological data, enabling ecologists to quickly process large volumes of field data to understand and monitor natural environments. Current practices evaluate active learning inductively, estimating predictive performance on a held-out test set. We argue that this evaluation is misaligned with most ecological tasks, where the goal is to transductively label an entire pool of data as efficiently as possible. We demonstrate that ignoring the human-in-the-loop underestimates the importance of continuing to label, particularly for classes in the long tail which may be of disproportionate ecological importance (rare species, uncommon behaviors, etc.). Our analysis shows that, for this long tail, the transductive objective shifts importance from prediction to discovery: the true challenge becomes finding "needles in the haystack," examples of rare classes that are embedded within dense regions of abundant classes in the latent geometry, which we quantify with a novel metric of sampling difficulty. Finally, to translate these insights to practical ecological workflows, we propose a conservative hybrid stopping criterion inspired by ecological rarefaction curves, and show that combining predictive performance with discovery criteria reduces premature stopping on long-tailed pools, improving rare-class recovery when discovery, not classification, is the limiting factor.

</details>


### [220] [Dynamic Short Convolutions Improve Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03825)
*Oliver Sieberling,Bharat Runwal,Rameswar Panda,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态短卷积作为Transformer的新组件，在保持卷积局部性偏置的同时增加表达能力。实验表明该方法在150M-2B参数规模下相比标准Transformer具有1.33×至1.60×的计算优势，并在线性RNN和混合专家架构上也有改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer架构虽已成为大语言模型的主流，但仍有改进空间。静态短卷积虽能提供局部性偏置，但表达能力有限。研究动机是设计一种既能保持卷积的局部性优势，又能根据输入动态调整滤波器以增强表达能力的机制，从而提升Transformer性能。

Method: 提出动态短卷积（dynamic short convolutions），与静态卷积不同，其使用依赖于输入的滤波器。主要将动态短卷积应用于Transformer中的key、query和value表示，或在线性层后添加。通过自定义Triton内核实现高效训练，使端到端性能损失可控。

Result: 在关联回忆任务中优于静态卷积变体。在150M至2B参数的语言建模实验中，动态卷积持续优于标准Transformer和增强静态短卷积的Transformer。拟合缩放定律显示：应用于key、query、value向量时有1.33×计算优势，在每层线性层后添加时有1.60×优势。在线性RNN（Mamba-2/Gated DeltaNet）和混合专家架构上也展现改进。

Conclusion: 动态短卷积是一种可扩展、硬件高效且具有表达能力的原语，能够有效推进基于Transformer的语言模型发展。该方法在保持实用性的同时（通过自定义Triton内核），为Transformer架构提供了有价值的增强。

Abstract: Transformers have become the dominant architecture for large language models, largely due to the scalability and flexibility of attention, feed-forward layers, residual connections, and normalization. This paper introduces dynamic short convolutions as an additional neural network primitive for improving Transformers. Unlike static short convolutions, dynamic convolutions use input-dependent filters, which preserves the locality bias of convolution while increasing expressivity. Motivating experiments show that applying dynamic short convolutions to key, query, and value representations improves performance on challenging associative recall tasks compared with static convolutional variants. Across language-modeling experiments ranging from 150M to 2B parameters, dynamic convolutions consistently outperform standard Transformers and Transformers augmented with static short convolutions. Fitting scaling laws indicates a 1.33$\times$ compute advantage over compute-matched Transformers when dynamic convolutions are applied to the key, query, and value vectors, and a 1.60$\times$ advantage when adding dynamic convolutions after every linear layer. Dynamic convolutions also offer improvements on linear RNNs (Mamba-2/Gated DeltaNet) and mixture-of-experts architectures. We make these gains practical with custom Triton kernels that enable efficient training with a manageable end-to-end slowdown. These results suggest that dynamic short convolutions are a scalable, hardware-efficient, and expressive primitive for advancing Transformer-based language models.

</details>


### [221] [Online Learning with Gradient-Variation Interval Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03831)
*Yan-Feng Xie,Shuche Wang,Peng Zhao,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了一个基于梯度变化度的区间遗憾界在线学习算法，通过双层集成结构实现了对问题相关量的自适应，并在最坏情况下保持最小最大最优率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非平稳在线学习中的区间遗憾问题，目标是让在线算法在任意时间间隔上都表现良好。现有的方法缺乏对梯度变化度这一基本度量的有效处理，且面临超参数调优的挑战，特别是在Lipschitz常数和平滑度未知的情况下。

Method: 提出一个简单高效的双层在线集成结构算法。引入了一个新颖的Lipschitz自适应元算法，使算法能够自动适应未知的Lipschitz常数和平滑度常数。该结构实现了对多种问题相关量的同时自适应。

Result: 实现了首个基于梯度变化度的区间遗憾界，该界限与问题相关量自适应，同时保持最坏情况下的最小最大最优率。算法能够自动适应未知的Lipschitz常数和平滑度常数。此外，方法还产生了区间动态遗憾的多功能界限，并为随机扩展对抗优化提供了首次分段表征。

Conclusion: 提出的算法在理论上具有强保证，实验验证了理论发现。该方法不仅解决了区间遗憾问题，还对区间动态遗憾和随机扩展对抗优化等更广泛的问题具有重要意义，其中Lipschitz自适应元算法具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates non-stationary online learning using the metric of interval regret, which requires an online algorithm to perform well over every time interval. We propose the first online learning algorithm that achieves an interval regret bound scaling with gradient variation, a fundamental measure of the cumulative change in online function gradients, which relates to various problem-dependent quantities and is closely connected to stochastic optimization and other problems. Our method employs a simple and efficient two-layer online ensemble structure that achieves strong theoretical guarantees. Specifically, it enjoys a regret bound that simultaneously adapts to various problem-dependent quantities while also preserving the minimax-optimal rate in the worst case. Moreover, recognizing the challenge of hyperparameter tuning, we introduce a Lipschitz- and smoothness-agnostic variant that automatically adapts to these potentially unknown constants. This is primarily enabled by a novel Lipschitz-adaptive meta algorithm, which may be of independent interest. Beyond interval regret, our method also yields broader implications: it provides versatile bounds for interval dynamic regret, a stronger measure that competes with changing comparators over any interval, and yields the first piecewise characterization for stochastic extended adversarial optimization. Theoretical findings are validated by experiments.

</details>


### [222] [Text-attributed Graph Condensation via Text Selection and Attribute Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03839)
*Haowei Han,Yuxiang Wang,Guojia Wan,Hao Wang,Shanshan Feng,Hao Huang,Jiawei Jiang,Xiao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TAGSAM方法，通过子图文本选择和属性相似度匹配压缩文本属性图，在仅1%压缩规模下保持训练精度，准确率平均提升4.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本属性图（TAG）模型联合训练图神经网络（GNN）和语言模型导致巨大的空间和时间开销，尤其在大型数据集上。现有基于匹配训练轨迹（MTT）的图拓扑压缩方法存在高方差问题，影响准确性。

Method: 提出TAGSAM压缩方法，包含两个核心设计：（1）子图文本选择：通过最大化互信息从多个相关文本描述中选择并合并代表性文本块；（2）属性相似度匹配：通过对齐稳定的相似度矩阵来解决MTT方法的高方差问题，压缩图的拓扑结构。

Result: 在六个最先进基线方法的对比评估中表现优异。在相同压缩规模下，TAGSAM相比最佳基线方法准确率平均提升4.9%。即使将TAG压缩至原始规模的1%，仍能保持竞争性的训练准确率。

Conclusion: TAGSAM成功实现了文本属性图的高效压缩，在保持训练准确性的同时大幅降低存储和计算成本，为大规模TAG数据处理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Text-Attributed Graph (TAG) is an important type of graph structured data, where each node has a text description. TAG models usually train a Graph Neural Network (GNN) and language model jointly, which leads to high space and time consumption, especially on large datasets. To mitigate this, we propose TAGSAM, a condensation method that compresses TAGs while preserving training accuracy. TAGSAM comes with two key designs, i.e., subgraph text Selection and Attribute similarity Matching, which compress the text description and graph topology of TAG, respectively. For the texts, subgraph text selection selects and merges representative text chunks from multiple related text descriptions by maximizing mutual information. For the graph topology, popular condensation methods based on Matching Training Trajectories (MTT) suffer from high variance, which hinders accuracy. Our attribute similarity matching mitigates this issue by aligning stable similarity matrices. We evaluate TAGSAM against six state-of-the-art baselines, where it showcases superior performance. For the same compressed size, TAGSAM improves upon the best-performing baseline by an average of 4.9% in accuracy. Furthermore, it maintains competitive training accuracy even when the TAG is condensed to just 1% size. Our code is available at https://github.com/SundayVHan/TAGSAM

</details>


### [223] [Re-Evaluating Continual Learning with Few-Shot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03843)
*Amogh Inamdar,Matthew So,Vici Milenia,Richard Zemel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用小样本评估替代传统的零样本评估，以更全面地衡量持续学习模型的稳定性和可塑性，并提出了一种新指标——单次可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的零样本评估要求模型对所有先前任务保持完美回忆，无法充分衡量模型保留已学信息或快速适应新信息的能力，因此需要更全面的评估范式。

Method: 提出小样本评估方法用于持续学习系统的稳定性和可塑性评估，引入新指标"单次可塑性"，并在持续图像分类任务序列上进行细粒度评估。

Result: 通过小样本评估获得了关于流行持续学习策略性能的新见解；发现通过对未来任务短序列的元学习来增加"前瞻性"，能够在任务序列上诱导出学习如何学习的行为。

Conclusion: 小样本评估相比零样本评估能更全面地衡量持续学习系统的稳定性和可塑性，为持续学习方法的分析提供了新视角。

Abstract: Continual learning methods aim to maximize the stability and plasticity of machine learning models that are trained on a sequence of tasks. The standard measure of stability (i.e., forgetting) is the 0-shot performance of a model on previously learned tasks, and plasticity, the performance on the most recently learned task. However, 0-shot evaluation does not fully measure a model or method's ability to retain learned information or adapt quickly to new information, as it requires perfect recall across multiple tasks. In this paper, we propose few-shot evaluation as a more comprehensive assessment of the stability and plasticity of a continual learning system. We conduct a fine-grained assessment on task sequences for continual image classification and find that this paradigm produces novel insights into the performance of popular continual learning strategies. Through few-shot evaluation with a novel metric -- per-shot plasticity -- we show that adding `foresight' to continual learning methods via the meta-learning of a short sequence of future tasks induces learning-to-learn behavior over the task sequence.

</details>


### [224] [Two-Action Apple Tasting with Switching Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03851)
*Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了带切换成本的双动作"苹果品尝"问题在遗忘对手下的最小最大期望遗憾为Θ(√T)，推翻了此前认为该问题存在Ω(T^{2/3})下界的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般反馈图算法在带切换成本情况下只能提供Õ(T^{2/3})的遗憾保证。双动作苹果品尝反馈图曾被认为可能是在切换成本分类中缺失的Ω(T^{2/3})阻碍；若此下界成立，将传递到一大类尚未分类的反馈图中。本文旨在验证这一猜想。

Method: 研究带切换成本的双动作苹果品尝问题。在等价归一化表述中，每轮学习者选择揭示动作（奖励为0，揭示隐藏值x_t∈[-1,1]）或盲动作（奖励为x_t，但不揭示任何信息）。每当切换动作时需支付一单位成本，遗憾以事后最佳固定动作为基准进行衡量。

Result: 证明了该问题的遗忘对手最小最大期望遗憾满足(1/(2√3))·√T ≤ R_T* ≤ 2√3·√T，即Θ(√T)量级，而非此前猜测的Ω(T^{2/3})。这意味着此前预期的阻碍并不存在。

Conclusion: 双动作苹果品尝问题在带切换成本情况下并不构成切换成本分类中的Ω(T^{2/3})障碍，这为反馈图的切换成本分类研究提供了新的认识，表明某些看似可能阻碍更优遗憾界的问题实际上可以达到更低的遗憾界。

Abstract: We study the two-action apple-tasting problem with switching costs against an oblivious adversary. In an equivalent normalized formulation, at each round the learner chooses between a revealing action and a blind action: the revealing action gives reward $0$ and reveals the hidden value $x_t\in[-1,1]$ of the blind action; the blind action gives reward $x_t$ but reveals nothing. The learner pays one unit whenever they switches actions, and regret is measured against the best fixed action in hindsight.
  General feedback-graph algorithms with switching costs give $\widetilde O(T^{2/3})$ regret guarantees for this problem. The two-action apple-tasting graph was the natural candidate for the missing $Ω(T^{2/3})$ obstruction in the switching-cost classification: such a lower bound would have transferred to a large family of still-unclassified feedback graphs. We prove that this obstruction is not there: the oblivious minimax expected regret for this problem satisfies \[
  \frac{1}{2\sqrt3}\cdot\sqrt T
  \le
  R_T^\star
  \le
  2\sqrt{3}\cdot \sqrt{T}. \]

</details>


### [225] [Attribution via Distributional Paths for Information Revelation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03885)
*Kieran A. Murphy,Shameen Shrestha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Reveal-IG方法，将路径归因从输入空间提升到结构化探测分布空间，通过逐步揭示输入信息来归因模型期望输出的变化，避免了输入空间路径方法中的伪影问题，产生更稳定的符号归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于路径的特征归因方法（如积分梯度）在输入空间定义轨迹，存在三个问题：无法控制特征查询的分辨率；基准点附近轨迹与输入本身贡献权重相同；容易产生路径伪影。这些限制影响了归因解释的质量和稳定性。

Method: 提出Reveal-IG方法，将路径归因从输入空间提升到结构化探测分布空间。该方法逐步揭示输入信息，对模型期望输出沿分布路径的变化进行归因。方法保留了相对于期望模型响应的完整性，并能自然适应多尺度图像探测和表格数据中特征级的不确定性。

Result: 在合成诊断实验中，Reveal-IG避免了影响输入空间方法的路径伪影。在ImageNet分类和表格回归任务上，该方法产生稳定的符号归因，在使用归因符号的指标上表现领先，在其他指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过将路径归因提升到分布空间，Reveal-IG成功解决了传统输入空间路径方法的局限性，提供了更稳定、可靠的模型解释，同时保持了完整性这一重要理论性质。

Abstract: Feature attribution methods explain predictions by assigning importance scores to input features. Path-based methods such as Integrated Gradients are especially appealing because they satisfy \textit{completeness}: attributions sum to the change in model output between a reference state and the input. Yet most path methods define this trajectory in input space, explaining a model through pointwise perturbed inputs along a chosen path. An input-space path integrates the model's raw response at each point it passes through, with no control over the resolution at which a feature is queried; the early, baseline-adjacent part of the trajectory contributes to the explanation on equal footing with the input itself. Here, we lift path attribution from input space to a space of structured probe distributions around the example of interest, and call our method Reveal-IG. Rather than traversing raw input values, Reveal-IG progressively reveals information about the input and attributes changes in the model's expected output along this distributional path. The result is a path-attribution framework that retains completeness with respect to the expected model response, and naturally accommodates multiscale image probes and feature-wise uncertainty in tabular data. Synthetic diagnostics show that Reveal-IG avoids path artifacts that affect input-space methods, and across ImageNet classification and tabular regression it produces stable, signed attributions -- leading on metrics that use attribution sign while remaining competitive on the rest.

</details>


### [226] [Denoise First, Orthogonalize Later: Understanding Momentum in Muon via Spectral Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03899)
*Xianliang Li,Zihan Zhang,Weiyang Liu,Han Bao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上揭示了Muon优化器中动量的作用机制：动量充当谱滤波器，抑制扰动同时保留主导信号，从而增大谱间隙并稳定奇异子空间，使得正交化步骤更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在LLM训练中展现出优异的实证性能，但其中动量的理论作用尚不明确。现有分析要么移除动量单独研究谱更新，要么保留动量却无法解释其提升性能的原因，存在理论空白。

Method: 在结构化信号加扰动梯度模型下进行理论分析，证明动量作为谱滤波器的作用；比较动量在正交化之前应用与相反顺序或移除动量的效果；通过理论推导分析动量对谱间隙和奇异子空间稳定性的影响。

Result: 动量抑制扰动成分同时保留主导信号成分，增大两者间的谱间隙；增大的谱间隙稳定了传递给Muon正交化步骤的矩阵的奇异子空间；在正交化之前应用动量，相比相反顺序或移除动量，能实现与梯度信号成分更强的对齐；实验验证涵盖LLM预训练等多任务。

Conclusion: 动量在Muon中起到关键的谱滤波作用，通过稳定奇异子空间使更新更可靠；该理论为理解其他基于矩阵的优化器中动量的优势提供了起点。

Abstract: Muon has recently demonstrated strong empirical performance in large language model training, but the theoretical role of momentum in Muon remains unclear. Existing analyses of Muon either remove momentum to study spectral updates in isolation, or retain momentum without explaining why it improves empirical performance. Our work bridges this gap by showing momentum in Muon acts as a spectral filter. Under a structured signal-plus-perturbation gradient model, we prove that momentum suppresses perturbations while preserving the dominant signal, thereby enlarging the spectral gap between them. This enlarged gap stabilizes the singular subspaces of the matrix passed to Muon's orthogonalization step, making the resulting update more reliable. We further show that applying momentum before orthogonalization achieves provably stronger alignment with the signal component of the gradient than either reversing this order or simply removing momentum. Experiments across diverse tasks, including LLM pretraining, support our theoretical analysis. More broadly, our theory offers a starting point for understanding the benefits of momentum in other matrix-based optimizers.

</details>


### [227] [MAdam: Metric-Aware Multi-Objective Adam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03904)
*Fengbei Liu,Rachit Saluja,Sunwoo Kwak,Ruibo Wang,Ruining Deng,Heejong Kim,Johannes C. Paetzold,Mert R. Sabuncu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多目标优化(MOO)求解器与Adam优化器耦合时存在的两个系统性缺陷（权重失配和几何失配），并提出了MAdam作为即插即用的解决方案，在多个任务上取得了持续改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化问题在机器学习中广泛存在，但现有MOO求解器（包括损失平衡、梯度平衡和基于Pareto的方法）几乎都依赖Adam优化器执行更新。作者发现这种耦合会导致求解器意图与优化器执行之间存在系统性差距，需要深入分析并解决这一问题。

Method: 提出MAdam（Metric-Aware Multi-Objective Adam），这是一种即插即用的包装器，无需修改求解器或优化器。MAdam通过标量化目标的偏好条件曲率对调和方向进行预处理，使得Adam的二阶矩在此白化输入上坍缩为单位矩阵，从而使实际更新由偏好条件的度量控制。

Result: 在多任务学习、Pareto前沿恢复、物理信息神经网络和医学成像等多个任务上的实验表明，MAdam在所有求解器家族上都比Adam有持续的性能改进。

Conclusion: MAdam成功解决了MOO求解器与Adam之间的权重失配和几何失配问题，提供了一个通用、有效的解决方案，能够提升多目标优化在各种机器学习任务中的表现。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) underlies many machine learning problems, yet MOO solvers across the loss-balancing, gradient-balancing, and Pareto-based families almost universally hand their reconciled directions to Adam~\cite{kingma2015adam}. We show this coupling introduces two systematic gaps between the solver's intent and the optimizer's execution. The first is a \emph{weighting mismatch}: Adam's second-moment denominator entangles the time-varying preference vector with gradient statistics, marginalizing the preference into a history average and collapsing distinct Pareto trade-offs toward a near-uniform mixture. The second is a \emph{geometric mismatch}: Adam's adaptive metric distorts the Euclidean geometry MOO solvers assume, turning aligned objectives into apparent conflicts. To resolve both jointly, we introduce \textbf{MAdam} (Metric-Aware Multi-Objective Adam), a drop-in wrapper that leaves both solver and optimizer unchanged. MAdam preconditions the reconciled direction by the preference-conditioned curvature of the scalarized objective; on this whitened input, Adam's second moment collapses to identity, so the realized update is governed by the preference-conditioned metric. Across multi-task learning, Pareto-front recovery, physics-informed neural networks, and medical imaging, MAdam consistently improves over Adam for every solver family.

</details>


### [228] [Contrastive Neural Algorithmic Reasoning for Graph Coloring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03923)
*Thien Le,Tianyu Zhao,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对比学习的GNN框架来解决近似k-着色问题，通过学习可迁移的着色几何结构，实现了跨图规模和分布的泛化，并在实验中匹配或优于贪心方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的无监督图神经网络(GNN)方法直接优化每个图实例，无法在不同规模和分布的图之间进行泛化。现有的图着色方法缺乏通用性，需要一种能够学习可迁移着色表示的方法。

Method: 提出对比学习框架，学习可迁移的着色几何结构：使相同颜色节点的嵌入对齐，同时将相邻节点的表示推向不同方向。对于单位范数嵌入，证明了最优解具有线原型结构（同一颜色节点的表示塌缩到共享的一维子空间，边连接正交子空间）。分析了监督设置下的平稳性条件，以及平衡着色假设下投影次梯度动力学保持该几何结构。对于非归一化变体，证明了梯度下降具有由商图硬间隔问题控制的最大间隔偏差。

Result: 理论和实验结果：1）理论上证明了最优解的线原型结构性质；2）在合成图和真实世界图上的实验表明，对比GNN编码器能够有效泛化；3）生成的着色方案冲突少，匹配甚至有时优于贪心方法。

Conclusion: 通过对比学习框架成功学习了图着色的可迁移几何结构，解决了现有方法泛化能力不足的问题。理论分析揭示了学习动力学的内在结构，实验验证了方法的有效性，为图着色问题提供了一种新的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Graph coloring seeks to assigns colors to a graph's nodes so that adjacent nodes receive different colors, using as few colors as possible. Here, we study approximate $k$-coloring, where the goal is to use at most $k$ colors while minimizing the number of monochromatic edges. This problem is central to graph theory and has applications in areas such as scheduling and resource allocation. Recent unsupervised GNN approaches optimize each instance directly, precluding generalization across graph sizes and distributions. We instead propose a contrastive learning framework that learns transferable coloring geometry where the embeddings of same-color nodes align, while adjacent nodes' representations are pushed toward distinct directions. We analyze the resulting population objective over bounded-size graphs. For unit-norm embeddings, we show that its optima have a line-prototype structure: Representations of nodes of the same color collapse to a shared one-dimensional subspace, and edges connect orthogonal subspaces. This geometry yields stationarity conditions in the supervised setting and is preserved by projected subgradient dynamics under a balanced-coloring assumption. In an unnormalized variant, gradient descent has a max-margin bias governed by a quotient-graph hard-margin problem. Experiments on synthetic and real-world graphs show that contrastive GNN encoders generalize effectively and produce low-conflict colorings, matching and sometimes improving on greedy approaches.

</details>


### [229] [FFR: Forward-Forward Learning for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03927)
*Xinyang Liu,Xuanyu Liang,Shiqi Ding,Boyang Li,Zhiqiang Que,Jiayang Li,Guosheng Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将Forward-Forward算法扩展至回归任务，提出FFR框架，通过序数竞争性优良度函数、分层阶梯架构和层次化预测三个创新，在5个真实回归数据集上平均恢复BP 98.6%的精度，同时将峰值训练内存降至BP的27%（深度8）和8%（深度32），迭代时间约为BP的72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Forward-Forward (FF)算法为反向传播提供了计算高效且生物学合理的替代方案，但其固有设计仅适用于通过对比正负样本对进行分类任务。扩展FF至回归任务面临根本性挑战：连续目标空间缺乏自然的"对立面"用于对比学习，且标准优良度函数不包含目标幅度或序数信息。

Method: 提出FFR框架，引入三个核心创新：(1) 序数竞争性优良度函数，用分区神经元组之间的竞争学习替代对比样本对，在距离感知的序数监督下进行优化；(2) 分层阶梯架构，浅层学习粗粒度序数判别，深层细化至细粒度回归，并通过多尺度特征聚合实现层间协作；(3) 层次化预测与不确定性估计，多尺度预测器联合提供鲁棒预测，并作为副产品给出预测置信度。

Result: 在五个真实世界回归基准数据集上的实验表明，FFR平均恢复BP 98.6%的精度；峰值训练内存降至BP的27%（深度8）和8%（深度32）；每次迭代时间约为BP的72%；显著优于所有无BP竞争对手。

Conclusion: FFR成功将Forward-Forward算法扩展至回归任务，在保持竞争力的预测精度的同时，显著降低了内存消耗和计算开销，证明了该框架在实际回归应用中的有效性和实用价值，为局部学习的回归应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm offers a computationally efficient and biologically plausible alternative to backpropagation (BP) by training neural networks through purely local, layer-wise optimization. However, FF is inherently designed for classification via contrastive positive-negative sample pairs, and extending it to regression poses fundamental challenges: continuous target space lack natural "opposites" for contrastive learning, and the standard goodness function carries no information about target magnitude or ordering. We propose FFR (Forward-Forward for Regression), to our knowledge, the first framework to extend FF to real-world regression and demonstrate competitive performance across diverse real-world datasets. FFR introduces three key innovations: (1) an ordinal competitive goodness function that replaces contrastive pairs with competitive learning between partitioned neuron groups under distance-aware ordinal supervision; (2) a stratified ladder architecture where shallow layers learn coarse ordinal discrimination and deeper layers refine into fine-grained regression, with multi-scale feature aggregation for inter-layer collaboration; and (3) hierarchical prediction with uncertainty estimation, where multi-scale predictors jointly provide robust predictions and prediction confidence as a free-lunch. Extensive experimental results show FFR recovers on average 98.6% of BP's accuracy across five real-world regression benchmarks while reducing peak training memory to only 27% of BP's at depth 8 and 8% at depth 32, with per-iteration time around 72% of BP's, and substantially outperforms all BP-free competitors.

</details>


### [230] [Value-Aware Stochastic KV Cache Eviction for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03928)
*Ting-Yun Chang,Harvey Yiyun Fu,Deqing Fu,Chenghao Yang,Jesse Thomason,Robin Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了VaSE，一种无需训练的KV缓存驱逐方法，通过保护大范数价值状态和引入随机性，在保持高效的同时显著提升推理模型的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型通过扩展思维链提升准确性，但长输出导致内存和计算瓶颈。现有的KV缓存驱逐方法虽然降低成本，但准确率往往低于保持完整缓存的选择性稀疏注意力方法。研究旨在解决驱逐方法准确率不足的问题，识别影响驱逐准确率的关键因素。

Method: 提出Value-aware Stochastic KV Cache Eviction (VaSE)方法。该方法基于两个核心发现：1) 识别并保护小部分异常大范数的价值状态，防止驱逐导致模型陷入重复推理循环；2) 在驱逐过程中引入随机性，通过增加缓存多样性提升准确率。该方法无需训练，可直接应用于FlashAttention2。

Result: 在六个推理任务上，Qwen3模型使用VaSE实现4倍KV缓存压缩，在相同稀疏度下平均准确率优于SOTA选择方法，并比最强的驱逐方法高出4%以上。VaSE成功弥合了效率与准确性之间的差距，支持静态内存占用。

Conclusion: VaSE通过保护关键价值状态和引入随机驱逐策略，有效解决了KV缓存驱逐中的准确率损失问题，为推理模型提供了一种高效且准确的缓存管理方案，在保持内存效率的同时达到了接近完整缓存的性能表现。

Abstract: Reasoning models improve accuracy through extended chains of thought, but their long outputs create a memory and compute bottleneck. KV cache eviction methods reduce this cost by evicting unimportant key-value pairs from the cache, yet they often yield worse accuracy than selection-based sparse attention alternatives, which keep the full KV cache. We identify key factors crucial to KV cache eviction accuracy. First, a small fraction of value states have abnormally large magnitudes, and evicting them causes catastrophic failure where models enter repetitive reasoning loops. Second, introducing stochasticity during eviction improves accuracy by increasing cache diversity. Based on these findings, we propose Value-aware Stochastic KV Cache Eviction (VaSE), a training-free recipe that protects large-magnitude value states and promotes diverse eviction decisions. Across six reasoning tasks, Qwen3 models using VaSE with 4x KV cache compression yield higher average accuracies than SOTA selection method at the same sparsity, while outperforming the strongest eviction method by more than 4%. Overall, VaSE bridges the gap between efficiency and accuracy, supporting FlashAttention2 and enabling a static memory footprint for reasoning models.

</details>


### [231] [Correcting Neural Operator Spectral Bias via Diffusion Posterior Sampling with Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03936)
*Niccolò Perrone,Fanny Lehmann,Stefania Fresca,Filippo Gatti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FreqNO-DPS通过频率自适应的扩散后验采样框架，有效解决了神经算子代理模型在PDE求解中的谱偏差问题，在稀疏传感器约束下实现了全频段高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子代理模型（NO）虽然能比数值求解器快数个数量级地近似PDE解，但存在谱偏差问题：系统性地衰减高频内容，在需要精细结构的场景中可靠性受限。虽然稀疏传感器测量能提供无谱失真的逐点精度，但仅覆盖域的极小部分。

Method: 提出FreqNO-DPS方法，将NO预测视为扩散后验采样框架中的辅助观测。结合在高质量仿真数据上训练的无条件分数扩散先验，以稀疏观测为条件并受冻结神经算子引导。关键创新是提出闭式、谱形状的引导分数，根据频率相关的精度对代理进行加权，无需去噪器反向传播，并提供了分布无关的近似误差分析。

Result: 在3D弹性波场预测任务中（5%和2%传感器覆盖率），该方法在所有频带上达到接近零的谱偏差，而纯代理模型和仅传感器DPS均显示系统性高频衰减。各向同性引导基线虽提高逐点精度，但将偏差几乎完整地带入后验，证明频率相关校准是必需的而非仅仅有益。

Conclusion: 该框架仅需成对的代理/参考数据，除残差的近似谱对角性外不利用问题特定结构，并通过相干性诊断工具可验证新代理模型的适用性，成功解决了神经算子代理模型的谱偏差问题。

Abstract: Neural operator surrogates (NO) approximate PDE solutions orders of magnitude faster than numerical solvers, but suffer from spectral bias: high-frequency content is systematically attenuated, limiting reliability where fine-scale structure matters. Sparse sensor measurements of the field are often available too, offering pointwise accuracy without spectral distortion but covering only a small fraction of the domain. We address this by treating NO predictions as auxiliary observations in a diffusion posterior sampling framework.
