Our method, FreqNO-DPS (https://github.com/niccoloperrone/FreqNO-DPS), combines an unconditional score-based diffusion prior, trained on high-fidelity simulations, with diffusion posterior sampling (DPS) conditioned on sparse observations and guided by a frozen neural operator. Naive integration reintroduces the surrogate's spectral bias; we resolve this with a closed-form, spectrally shaped guidance score that weights the surrogate by its frequency-dependent accuracy and needs no denoiser backpropagation. A distribution-free analysis bounds the approximation error across the frequency-diffusion-time plane and shows the guidance's frequency dependence is preserved regardless of distributional assumptions. On 3D elastic wavefield prediction at 5% and 2% sensor coverage, the method reaches near-zero spectral bias across all bands, where both the surrogate and sensor-only DPS show systematic high-frequency attenuation. Isotropic guidance, the natural baseline, improves pointwise accuracy but carries the bias into the posterior nearly intact, confirming that frequency-dependent calibration is essential, not merely beneficial. The framework needs only paired surrogate/reference data and exploits no problem-specific structure beyond the residual's approximate spectral diagonality, verifiable for new surrogates via the coherence diagnostic we provide.

</details>


### [232] [q0: Primitives for Hyper-Epoch Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03938)
*Bishwas Mandal,Shmuel Berman,Akshay Vegesna,Samip Dahal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出hyper-epoch pretraining (q0)方法，将多epoch训练预算转化为多样化模型群体，通过聚合预测实现比单一精调模型更低的验证损失，数据效率提升约12.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算能力增长快于高质量文本供应，多epoch训练成为标准。但单一模型预训练在几个pass后就达到饱和，远未耗尽计算预算，这表明需要从训练单一模型转向探索模型群体并聚合其预测。

Method: q0包含三个核心原语：(1)反相关学习率和权重衰减的循环调度，从少量并行轨迹收集多样化模型；(2)链式蒸馏，每个模型对其前驱模型进行训练，使模型质量在群体中复合增长；(3)学习到的先验，在独立数据集上拟合，为任意推理预算选择和加权成员。

Result: 在1.8B参数模型上训练100M FineWeb tokens，q0仅用约56个epoch（约4.6倍减少）即匹配256-epoch集成基线；匹配基线集成大小时仅需约67个epoch（约3.8倍减少），并持续改进。在Slowrun设置下累积数据效率提升约12.9倍，并迁移到下游基准测试。

Conclusion: 最优分配随预算变化，为从单个epoch到最大预算的不同epoch预算提供了最大化泛化的明确指导方案。该研究为如何有效利用给定epoch预算提供了规范性方法。

Abstract: Multi-epoch training is becoming the standard now that compute is growing faster than the supply of high-quality text. But pretraining a single model saturates within a few passes, long before the compute budget is exhausted. We argue this calls for a conceptual shift from training a single model toward exploring a population of models and aggregating their predictions. We introduce hyper-epoch pretraining (q0), which turns a multi-epoch budget into a population of diverse models whose combined predictions reach a lower validation loss than a single refined model. q0 reduces to three core primitives. A cyclic schedule with anti-correlated learning rate and weight decay collects diverse models from a few parallel trajectories. Chain distillation trains each model against its predecessor so that model quality compounds across the population. A learned prior, fit on a held out set, selects and weights members for any inference budget. On a 1.8B-parameter model trained on 100M FineWeb tokens, q0 matches a strong 256-epoch ensemble baseline using only ${\sim}56$ epochs (${\sim}4.6\times$ fewer), or ${\sim}67$ epochs (${\sim}3.8\times$ fewer) when matched to the baseline's ensemble size, and continues to improve beyond it. These gains reach cumulative ${\sim}12.9\times$ data efficiency under the Slowrun setting and transfer to downstream benchmarks. Crucially, the optimal allocation shifts with the budget, so we give prescriptive recipes for how to spend a given epoch budget to maximize generalization, from a single epoch up to the largest budgets.

</details>


### [233] [FlashbackCL: Mitigating Temporal Forgetting in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03939)
*Mubarak A. Ojewale,Adriana E. Chis,Jorge M. Cortes-Mendoza,Bernardo Pulido-Gaytan,Horacio Gonzalez-Velez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlashbackCL，一种针对联邦学习中时间分布偏移问题的遗忘缓解方法。通过引入时间衰减标签计数、设备感知的重放缓冲区和主动核心集筛选，在CIFAR-10上实现了6.9%-10.0%的相对性能提升，并将时间遗忘降低了68%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习遗忘缓解方法假设客户端数据分布是平稳的，但实际部署中客户端数据分布会随时间漂移。当前最强的跨客户端遗忘方法Flashback使用单调累积的每类标签计数作为知识代理，该代理在时间分布偏移下会失准，导致全局模型锚定在过时的类别平衡上。

Method: 本文提出了FlashbackCL，作为Flashback的即插即用扩展，包含三个关键组件：（i）时间衰减标签计数；（ii）配备类别平衡水库采样（CBRS）的设备感知重放缓冲区；（iii）在公共蒸馏集上的服务器端主动核心集筛选。该方法正式定义了联邦学习中的时间遗忘度量。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集上，50个客户端和三种受控时间偏移模式下，FlashbackCL相对于Flashback实现了6.9%-10.0%的相对改进，同时将时间遗忘降低了高达68%。5变体消融实验表明CBRS重放是关键组件。在平稳的CIFAR-100上，FlashbackCL比Flashback提升了3.5个百分点。

Conclusion: FlashbackCL成功解决了联邦学习中的时间分布偏移问题，其类别平衡重放机制不仅能处理时间偏移，还能正则化空间异质性。该方法为非平稳分布下的联邦学习提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) of foundation and edge models increasingly targets deployments where client data distributions drift over time, yet existing forgetting-mitigation methods assume each client's distribution is stationary. Flashback, the strongest recent FL method against cross-client (spatial) forgetting, uses monotonically accumulating per-class label counts as a knowledge proxy; this proxy becomes miscalibrated under temporal distribution shift and anchors the global model to an outdated class balance. We formalise temporal forgetting in FL with a per-phase metric isolated from protocol-level fluctuations and propose Flashback Continual Learning (FlashbackCL), a drop-in extension of Flashback with (i) temporally-decayed label counts; (ii) a device-aware replay buffer with Class-Balanced Reservoir Sampling (CBRS); and (iii) server-side active coreset curation on the public distillation set. The results show that FlashbackCL achieves 6.9% to 10.0% relative improvement relative to Flashback, on CIFAR-10 with 50 clients and three controlled temporal shift modes, while simultaneously reducing temporal forgetting by up to 68%. A 5-variant ablation identifies CBRS replay as the critical component. FlashbackCL also improves Flashback by 3.5 points on stationary CIFAR-100, suggesting that class-balanced replay regularises spatial heterogeneity as well as temporal shift.

</details>


### [234] [Using Reward Uncertainty to Induce Diverse Behaviour in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03962)
*Anthony GX-Chen,Ankit Anand,Gheorghe Comanici,Zaheer Abbas,Eser Aygün,David Smalling,Shibl Mourad,Doina Precup,André Barreto,Mark Rowland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型强化学习框架，通过将标量奖励替换为奖励函数分布来自然地实现可控的行为多样性，无需在性能和随机性之间进行权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习寻求确定性策略以最大化期望奖励，但现代应用（如语言模型微调或科学发现）需要多样性。现有的解决方案（如熵正则化或多样性奖励）往往需要在性能和随机性之间进行脆弱的权衡，或依赖可能误导策略排序的启发式度量。作者认为当奖励函数不完全已知时（如偏好模糊或奖励模型不完善），多样性应被更自然地理解为对奖励不确定性的理性响应。

Method: 将传统RL目标进行根本性重构：用奖励函数分布替代标量奖励，并在动作集合上应用非线性目标函数。针对上下文赌博机设置，推导出原理性的梯度估计器，证明了该公式能够自然地推广传统策略梯度方法和最近开发的动作集方法。

Result: 该框架使校准的行为多样性自然涌现，且可通过奖励函数分布进行控制，同时不牺牲期望奖励。实证结果表明，该方法为传统问题表述无法诱导期望行为广度的复杂RL任务提供了鲁棒且理论扎实的替代方案。

Conclusion: 通过将奖励不确定性纳入RL目标的根本性重新表述，成功实现了可控且不牺牲性能的行为多样性，为需要多样性的现代RL应用提供了理论基础扎实的新范式。

Abstract: Classical reinforcement learning (RL) typically seeks a deterministic policy that maximizes the expected sum of a scalar reward. Yet, modern applications such as language model fine-tuning or scientific discovery demand diversity. Existing remedies such as entropy regularization or diversity bonuses often require fragile trade-offs that sacrifice performance for stochasticity or rely on heuristic metrics that can misalign policy rankings. We argue that diversity is more naturally understood as the rational response to uncertainty in the reward. When the reward function is not perfectly known--as is the case with ambiguous preferences or imperfect reward models--committing to a single action can be sub-optimal. Building on this, we propose a fundamental reformulation of the RL objective by replacing the scalar reward with a distribution over reward functions, and applying a non-linear objective over sets of actions. The result is a framework in which calibrated behavioural diversity emerges naturally, remains controllable through the reward function distribution, and is obtained without sacrificing expected reward. Focusing on the contextual bandit setting, we derive a principled gradient estimator for this objective and prove that our formulation naturally generalizes both vanilla policy gradient and more recently developed action-set approaches. Our empirical results demonstrate that this framework offers a robust and theoretically grounded alternative for complex RL tasks where the traditional formulation of the problem fails to induce the desired breadth of agent behaviour.

</details>


### [235] [Language Models Need Sleep: Learning to Self-Modify and Consolidate Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03979)
*Ali Behrouz,Farnoosh Hashemi,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受人类学习过程启发，本文提出了一个“睡眠”范式，使模型能够持续学习，通过记忆巩固和“做梦”两个阶段，将短期记忆转化为长期知识并自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习模型（包括大型语言模型）在即时预测或上下文学习方面表现出色，但缺乏持续学习能力，无法有效地将其临时的上下文知识转化为长期的模型参数。

Method: 提出了“睡眠”范式，包含两个阶段：（1）记忆巩固（知识播种）：通过结合强化学习模仿学习的广义蒸馏过程，将较小模型的记忆蒸馏到更大的网络中；（2）“做梦”：模型利用强化学习生成合成数据课程，在无人工监督下演练新知识并优化现有能力。

Result: 在长周期任务、持续学习、知识融合和少样本泛化任务上的实验结果证明了该“睡眠”阶段的有效性和重要性。

Conclusion: 该研究为机器学习模型引入了一种受生物学启发的持续学习和自我改进机制，解决了现有模型无法有效转化临时知识的局限性，为更智能的学习系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: The past few decades have witnessed significant advances in the design of machine learning algorithms, from early studies on task-specific shallow models to more general deep Large Language Models (LLMs). Despite showing promising results in tasks that require instant prediction or in-context learning, existing models lack the ability to continually learn and effectively transfer their temporal in-context knowledge to their long-term parameters. Inspired by human learning process, we introduce a ''Sleep'' paradigm that allows the models to continually learn, distill their short-term fragile memories into stable long-term knowledge with replay, and recursively improve themselves with ''Dreaming'' process. In more detail, sleep consists of two stages: (1) Memory Consolidation: an upward distillation process, called Knowledge Seeding, where the memories of a smaller-self are distilled into a larger network to provide more capacity while preserving the knowledge. As a proof of concept, we present a new Generalized Distillation process for {Knowledge Seeding} (i.e., the combination of on-policy distillation with Reinforcement Learning (RL)-based imitation learning); (2) Dreaming: a self-improvement phase, where the model uses RL to generate a curriculum of synthetic data to rehearse new knowledge and refine existing capabilities without human supervision. Our experiments on long-horizon, continual learning, knowledge incorporation, and few-shot generalization tasks support the importance of the sleep stage.

</details>


### [236] [Skill-RM: Unifying Heterogeneous Evaluation Criteria via Agent Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03980)
*Tao Chen,Gangwei Jiang,Pengyu Cheng,Siyuan Huang,Yihao Liu,Jingwei Ni,Jiaqi Guo,Mengyu Zhou,Kai Tang,Junling Liu,Qinliang Su,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill-RM框架，将奖励建模重新表述为可复用的奖励评估技能执行，通过统一接口协调异构资源，动态选择和聚合证据，在奖励基准和下游应用中均优于传统判断基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的奖励模型评估依赖于异构标准（如基于规则的验证器、真实参考、过程检查清单和复杂评分标准），缺乏统一机制来整合所有类型的证据，这限制了奖励模型在不同任务中的一致性和透明度。

Method: 提出Skill-RM框架，将奖励计算视为结构化的代理任务，通过将奖励建模重新表述为可复用的奖励评估技能执行，提供一致的接口来协调异构资源，并根据每个输入的具体要求动态选择和聚合证据。

Result: 在奖励基准测试和下游应用（包括best-of-N选择和强化学习）上的大量实验表明，Skill-RM始终优于传统判断基线，证明了其在奖励建模中的有效性。

Conclusion: Skill-RM不仅为奖励建模提供了统一解决方案，还通过策略性和动态性地协调证据实现了更优的性能，使奖励模型超越了静态评估，确保了跨多样化任务的一致性和透明度。

Abstract: Reward models (RMs) provide critical feedback signals for LLM post-training, notably in reinforced fine-tuning (RFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) pipelines. However, current reward evaluation relies on heterogeneous criteria such as rule-based verifiers, ground-truth references, procedural checklists, and complex rubrics, where a unified mechanism to integrate all types of evidence remains unexplored. To this end, we propose Skill Reward Model (Skill-RM), a unified framework that reformulates reward modeling as the execution of a reusable Reward-Evaluation Skill. By treating reward computation as a structured agentic task, Skill-RM provides a consistent interface to orchestrate heterogeneous resources, dynamically selecting and aggregating evidence tailored to the specific requirements of each input. This approach enables the reward model to move beyond static evaluation, ensuring consistency and transparency across diverse tasks. Extensive experiments on reward benchmarks and downstream applications, including best-of-N selection and reinforcement learning, demonstrate that Skill-RM consistently outperforms traditional judge baselines. Our findings suggest that Skill-RM not only provides a unified solution for reward modeling but also achieves superior performance through the strategic and dynamic orchestration of evidence. The code is at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/Skill-RM.

</details>


### [237] [Neuron Populations Exhibit Divergent Selectivity with Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03990)
*Amil Dravid,Yasaman Bahri,Alexei A. Efros,Yossi Gandelsman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现可解释的Rosetta神经元数量随模型规模呈亚幂律增长，且表现出极化效应——随规模增大变得更加选择性和单义性，揭示了神经元层面的新缩放定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律主要关注损失等宏观指标，缺乏对神经网络内部神经元群体如何随规模演化的理解。研究旨在探索神经元层面的可预测性，将缩放定律扩展到微观神经元结构。

Method: 分析语言模型（最大300亿参数）和视觉模型（最大50亿参数）中的Rosetta神经元群体；建立解析模型平衡特征效用与有限神经元容量；通过目标数据过滤案例研究验证神经元选择性。

Result: Rosetta神经元数量随模型规模呈亚幂律增长，占总体神经元比例下降；发现神经元极化效应——随规模增大Rosetta神经元变得更加选择性和单义性，与non-Rosetta群体分化；解析模型成功解释了该现象；Rosetta神经元随规模增大变得更加领域专门化。

Conclusion: 建立了可解释的共享神经元层面结构的缩放定律，将模型规模与神经元通用性、选择性和专门化的系统性变化联系起来，为理解神经网络内部表征演化提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigate whether neuron populations within neural networks evolve predictably with scale, extending scaling laws beyond macroscopic observables such as loss. To probe this question, we study Rosetta Neurons, a previously characterized class of neurons whose activation patterns are similar across independently trained models (Dravid et al., 2023). In separate analyses of language models up to 30B parameters and vision models up to 5B parameters, we observe that the population of Rosetta Neurons follows a sublinear power law in model size, growing in absolute number but occupying a shrinking fraction of the total neuron count. We further observe a Neuron Polarization Effect: Rosetta Neurons become more selective and increasingly monosemantic with scale, separating from a growing non-Rosetta population that remains less selective. An analytical model balancing feature utility against limited neuron capacity explains the sublinear power-law scaling and this polarization effect. Finally, we find that Rosetta Neurons become more domain-specialized with scale and illustrate their selectivity through a targeted data-filtering case study for continued pretraining. Our results point to a scaling law for interpretable, shared neuron-level structure, linking model size to systematic changes in neuron universality, selectivity, and specialization.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [AUGUSTE: Online-Learning dApp for Predictive URLLC Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03664)
*Maxime Elkael,Michele Polese,Yunseong Lee,Koichiro Furueda,Tommaso Melodia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出AUGUSTE，一种基于机器学习的MAC调度框架，通过预测数据包到达主动分配上行资源，在约10ms中位RTT下实现与持续调度相当的低延迟，同时将资源开销降低至约十分之一（7-10%开销）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G URLLC场景的1-10ms延迟目标在实际TDD网络中难以实现，当前UL RTT仍处于50-70ms范围，主要瓶颈在于UE必须完成SR过程才能传输上行数据。现有的配置授权调度仅适用于严格周期性流量且需要跨层同步，应用受限。

Method: 提出AUGUSTE框架，将在线机器学习模型嵌入上行调度器，预测数据包到达并在SR发出前主动分配资源。采用自适应状态机在学习阶段（收集无偏到达统计）和置信阶段（利用学习到的预测进行调度）之间交替，仅在预期有流量时进行调度。

Result: 在运行OpenAirInterface的真实5G测试平台上，针对三种URLLC流量模式（请求-响应、ML边缘推理、周期性自主报告）进行评估。AUGUSTE在延迟-开销权衡曲线上达到最佳点：实现约10ms的中位RTT（相比基于SR的20ms基线减半），与持续调度匹配，但资源成本仅为其约十分之一（7-10%开销）。

Conclusion: AUGUSTE成功打破了URLLC场景中低延迟与资源开销之间的权衡困境，无需严格周期性流量假设或跨层同步，为实际5G网络实现URRLC目标提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Ultra Reliable and Low Latency Communications (URLLC) was one of the main motivations behind 5G, with 3GPP advertising 1-10 ms latency targets for applications such as industrial automation, Vehicle-To-Everything (V2X), tactical edge networking, and unmanned-system control. Years on, real 5G Time Division Duplexing (TDD) networks still show median Uplink (UL) round-trip times in the 50-70 ms range, largely because of the Scheduling Request (SR) procedure that a User Equipment (UE) must complete before transmitting UL data. Existing remedies, primarily Configured Grant (CG) scheduling, only eliminate this overhead for strictly periodic traffic and require cross-layer synchronization, which has limited their adoption. We propose AUGUSTE (Anticipatory Uplink Grants for URLLC via Self-Adapting Temporal Estimation), a learning-based Medium Access Control (MAC) scheduling framework that embeds online Machine Learning (ML) models in the UL scheduler to predict packet arrivals and proactively allocate resources before an SR is issued. An adaptive state machine alternates between a learning phase that collects unbiased arrival statistics and a confident phase that exploits the learned predictions to schedule only when traffic is expected. We evaluate AUGUSTE on a real 5G testbed running OpenAirInterface across three URLLC traffic patterns (request-response, ML edge inference, and periodic autonomous reporting), and show that it operates at the best achievable point on the latency-overhead trade-off: it matches always-on scheduling's median Round Trip Time (RTT) (around 10 ms, halving the 20 ms SR-based baseline) at roughly one-tenth its resource cost (7-10 percent overhead).

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [Heterogeneous Mapping for Analog In-Memory Computing Accelerators: A Unified Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02672)
*Corey Lammie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文对现有AIMC异构架构DNN分区方法进行分类，提出统一四阶段工作流，并首次应用于GPT-2，揭示其精度敏感性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟存内计算（AIMC）加速器有效精度有限，需采用模拟计算单元与数字处理单元相结合的异构架构。现有DNN工作负载分区方法缺乏系统性分类与统一框架，且尚未有研究探讨仅解码器Transformer在AIMC硬件上的部署特性。

Method: 从映射粒度、优化策略和模型支持三个维度对现有分区方法进行分类，提炼出统一的四阶段工作流。将该工作流的前两个阶段应用于GPT-2模型，生成首个针对仅解码器Transformer的AIMC精度敏感性画像。

Result: GPT-2中49个投影层中仅4个主导敏感性，其中第一个解码器块的注意力输出层的敏感性比其他层高一个数量级。这表明投影层级别的映射策略和选择性数字执行早期块及面向输出的投影层对可靠部署至关重要。

Conclusion: 投影层级别的细粒度映射和针对早期块及输出相关投影层的选择性数字执行策略，对于在AIMC硬件上可靠部署仅解码器Transformer具有重要意义，为后续研究提供了具体指导。

Abstract: Analog In-Memory Computing (AIMC) accelerators execute matrix-vector multiplications directly within memory arrays, reducing data movement and improving DNN inference efficiency. Their limited effective precision motivates heterogeneous architectures that combine analog compute tiles with digital processing units. This letter classifies existing methods for partitioning DNN workloads across these resources by mapping granularity, optimization strategy, and model support, and distills them into a unified four-stage workflow. To demonstrate the workflow on a model class not yet addressed by existing methods, we apply its first two stages to GPT-2, producing the first AIMC-specific precision sensitivity profile for a decoder-only transformer. Sensitivity is dominated by 4 of 49 projections, with the first decoder block's attention output dominating by an order of magnitude. This suggests that projection-level mapping and selective digital execution of early-block and output-facing projections are important for reliable decoder-transformer deployment on AIMC hardware.

</details>


### [240] [CRAM-ER: Error-Resilient Spintronic Computational Random Access Memory for Scalable In-Memory Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02781)
*Sohan Salahuddin Mugdho,Md. Shahedul Hasan,Brahmdutta Dixit,Yang Lv,Jian-Ping Wang,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了CRAM-ER架构，通过硬件-软件协同设计解决了MRAM概率性开关错误和写入延迟问题，在保持DNN精度的情况下显著提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络面临严重的存储瓶颈，现有的近内存和存内计算方案存在显著的外围电路开销。基于MRAM的计算随机存取存储器(CRAM)虽然能够实现原位逻辑而无外围开销，但存在概率性开关导致的门级错误以及大量串行写入限制吞吐量的问题。

Method: 提出错误感知的硬件-软件协同设计框架：1) 采用混合自旋电子-CRAM与CMOS加法树架构来缓解器件级错误影响；2) 开发错误感知的模型微调方法；3) 设计细粒度错误校正机制以增强错误恢复能力。

Result: 在DNN基准测试中实现了近乎无损的精度，CRAM延迟降低高达2个数量级，在能效和能延积方面均优于CPU/GPU+高带宽DRAM方案。

Conclusion: CRAM-ER架构成功解决了MRAM的可靠性问题，为可扩展的存内矩阵向量乘法提供了高面积效率和能效的解决方案，证明了混合自旋电子-CMOS架构在DNN加速中的优越性。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) have achieved state-of-the-art performance across diverse domains. However, typical Von Neumann compute paradigms face severe memory bottlenecks. Emerging near-memory and compute-in-memory approaches alleviate this but incur significant peripheral overhead. Computational Random Access Memory (CRAM) based on MRAM enables in-situ logic without peripheral overhead, offering a dense, energy-efficient solution. However, probabilistic MRAM switching induces gate-level errors that limit the scalability and reliability of CRAM for accelerating DNN. Moreover, the large number of sequential MRAM writes severely constrains CRAM throughput. To address these challenges, we propose an error-resilient CRAM (CRAM-ER) architecture for scalable in-memory matrix-vector multiplications (MVMs). Our error-aware hardware-software co-design framework leverages a hybrid spintronic-CRAM + CMOS adder-tree architecture to mitigate the impact of device-level errors, demonstrating MVM functionality with high area and energy efficiency. We further develop an error-aware model fine-tuning and fine-grained error correction for enhanced error resilience. Evaluations of the CMOS+spintronic hybrid architecture on DNN benchmarks show near-lossless accuracy while reducing CRAM latency by up to 2 orders of magnitude, outperforming CPU/GPU+high-bandwidth DRAM in both energy efficiency and energy-delay product.

</details>


### [241] [Fast Transformer Inference on ARM-Based HMPSoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02836)
*Hang Xu,Yixian Shen,Thanassis Giannetsos,Anuj Pathania*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 在ARM计算库中实现了Transformer内核支持，并提出CPU-GPU协同推理方法，在ARM边缘设备上实现了相比现有方案快3倍的推理速度，并进一步降低延迟15.72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在边缘设备上的部署面临资源密集型挑战，ARM计算库（ARM-CL）框架支持CNN推理但缺乏Transformer推理支持，需要在资源受限的边缘设备上实现高效的无云端片上Transformer推理。

Method: 在ARM-CL中实现多个新的Transformer内核以支持原生Transformer执行；提出异构多处理器片上系统（HMPSoCs）的CPU-GPU协同Transformer推理方法，在CPU上执行内存密集型操作，在GPU上执行高度可并行化的计算密集型操作。

Result: 扩展后的ARM-CL在ARM嵌入式板上相比最先进的CPU/GPU实现实现了最高3倍的Transformer推理加速；CPU-GPU协同推理相比ARM-CL上最佳单处理器推理进一步降低Transformer推理延迟最高达15.72%。

Conclusion: 通过在ARM-CL中实现原生Transformer支持并提出CPU-GPU协同执行策略，成功解决了边缘设备上Transformer推理的资源密集型挑战，显著提升了推理性能。

Abstract: Transformer models have set new performance standards for machine learning (ML) tasks. However, their resource-intensive deployment on resource-constrained edge devices for cloud-free, on-chip transformer inference remains challenging. The ARM Compute Library (ARM-CL) framework provides low-latency CNN inference on ARM-based edge devices but lacks support for transformer inference. In this work, we implement several new transformer kernels in ARM-CL to support native transformer execution. Our extended ARM-CL achieves up to three times faster transformer inference compared to state-of-the-art CPU/GPU implementations on an ARM-based embedded board.
  Furthermore, heterogeneous multi-processor system-on-chips (HMPSoCs) powering edge devices provide both embedded CPUs and GPUs. We introduce cooperative CPU-GPU transformer inference, which executes memory-intensive operations on the CPU while utilizing the GPU for highly parallelizable, compute-intensive operations. This cooperative execution, implemented with minimal overhead, further reduces transformer inference latency by up to 15.72% compared to the best single-processor inference on ARM-CL.

</details>


### [242] [Multi-Segment Attention: Enabling Efficient KV-Cache Management for Faster Large Language Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02964)
*Chunan Shi,Yilei Chen,Yilin Chen,Xupeng Miao,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: AsymCache通过考虑GPU注意力内核执行效率来优化KV缓存管理，相比最新基线将TTFT和TPOT分别降低至多2.03倍和1.71倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无损KV缓存管理系统主要基于访问频率或位置启发式做出驱逐决策，未考虑不同KV缓存块对GPU注意力内核执行效率的影响，导致性能次优。

Method: 提出AsymCache系统，包含三个关键组件：(1)多段注意力(MSA)用于高效处理非连续KV上下文；(2)联合优化命中率与位置感知重计算成本的缓存驱逐策略；(3)自适应分块调度器以实现高硬件利用率。

Result: 实验表明，AsymCache相比最新基线将TTFT降低至多1.90-2.03倍，TPOT降低至多1.62-1.71倍。在Continuum等智能体服务系统中，平均作业延迟进一步降低至多18.1%。

Conclusion: AsymCache通过将缓存驻留决策与GPU注意力内核性能显式对齐，成功实现了计算效率与缓存命中率的平衡，证明了计算延迟感知的KV缓存管理的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) inference relies on key-value (KV) caches to avoid redundant attention computation. While approximate KV cache retention techniques reduce memory usage by sacrificing model accuracy, lossless approaches instead evict KV cache blocks from GPU memory and reconstruct them on demand to preserve exact outputs. Existing lossless KV cache management systems primarily base eviction decisions on access frequency or positional heuristics, without considering how different KV cache blocks affect the execution efficiency of GPU attention kernels.
  In this paper, we propose AsymCache, a computation-latency-aware KV cache management system for LLM inference that explicitly aligns cache residency decisions with GPU attention kernel performance, including three key components: Multi-Segment Attention (MSA) for efficient non-contiguous KV context processing, a cache eviction policy that jointly optimizes hit rate and position-aware recomputation cost, and an adaptive chunking scheduler for high hardware utilization. Experiments show that AsymCache reduces TTFT by up to 1.90-2.03x and time-per-output-token (TPOT) by 1.62-1.71x over latest baselines, confirming the effectiveness of the method in common workloads and validating its design goal of balancing computational efficiency with cache hit rate. Moreover, the low-level design of AsymCache allows seamless integration into agent serving systems such as Continuum, where it further reduces average job latency by up to 18.1%.

</details>


### [243] [ZK-Flex: A Flexible and Scalable Framework for Accelerating Zero-Knowledge Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03046)
*Adiwena Putra,Cuong Manh Duong,Anh Quang Pham,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出ZK-Flex软硬件协同框架加速零知识证明生成，相比现有技术实现5-11倍加速和3.8倍面积效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零知识证明生成计算密集，主要由多项式(POLY)和椭圆曲线(EC)操作主导。现有硬件加速面临两大挑战：(1)高效支持多样化的大精度模乘运算；(2)在POLY和EC阶段动态切换的工作负载中保持高利用率。现有可重构加速器在精度可扩展性、算法灵活性和资源效率方面存在局限。

Method: 提出ZK-Flex软硬件协同设计框架。软件层包含POLY和EC优化器，通过硬件感知和工作负载感知的算法选择减少计算量；硬件层集成TCore——基于Toom-Cook的多精度核心，配备灵活的NoC和链表内存机制，在有限内存容量下提升并行度。

Result: 在代表性ZKP基准测试中，ZK-Flex相比现有最先进技术实现5到11倍加速，面积效率提升高达3.8倍。

Conclusion: ZK-Flex为高性能、可重构的ZKP加速建立了新基础，有效解决了精度可扩展性、算法灵活性和资源效率等关键问题。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge proofs (ZKP) allows a prover to convince a verifier of computational correctness without revealing private data, ensuring both privacy and verifiability. However, proof generation is highly compute-intensive, dominated by polynomial (POLY) and elliptic-curve (EC) operations. These workloads pose two key challenges for hardware acceleration: (1) efficiently supporting diverse large-precision modular multiplications, and (2) maintaining high utilization across workloads that dynamically shift between POLY and EC stages. Existing reconfigurable accelerators address these issues only partially, remaining limited in precision scalability, algorithmic flexibility, and resource efficiency.
  To overcome these limitations, we propose ZK-Flex, a flexible and scalable software-hardware co-designed framework for accelerating ZKP proof generation. The software layer incorporates POLY and EC optimizers that reduce computation through hardware- and workload-aware algorithmic choices, while the hardware integrates TCore, a Toom-Cook-based multi-precision core with a flexible NoC and a linked-list memory mechanism that improves parallelism under limited memory capacity. Across representative ZKP benchmarks, ZK-Flex achieves 5 to 11 times speedup and up to 3.8 times higher area efficiency over the state of the art, establishing a new foundation for high-performance, reconfigurable ZKP acceleration.

</details>


### [244] [ACRONYM: Accelerated Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in Memory for Dynamic Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03151)
*Md Mizanur Rahaman Nayan,Tianqi Zhang,Flavio Ponzina,Tajana Rosing,Azad J Naeemi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种算法-硬件协同设计的向量数据库搜索平台ACRONYM，通过数据分布无关的编码和海明距离搜索实现了对动态数据库的高效ANNS，在百万级数据集上达到>90%召回率、400倍于HNSW(CPU)和80倍于FAISS-IVF(GPU)的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图结构和基于分区的ANNS方法由于依赖数据分布的索引机制，在频繁更新的场景下需要频繁重建索引，影响持续部署并导致较长的重建延迟，无法满足向量数据库中频繁更新的需求。

Method: 算法层面采用数据分布无关的高效编码和基于海明距离的搜索；硬件架构层面提出CAM内存内并行距离计算和时分复用近似top-k选择，采用粗搜索+二值细化的两阶段搜索以提高召回率，并设计XOR-and-Accumulate(XAC)脉动阵列编码器实现搜索期间的片上编码。

Result: 在百万级数据集上，ACRONYM实现了>90%的召回率，吞吐量为8e6 QPS，内存占用仅32MB，平均每查询能耗2.56μJ，相比HNSW(CPU)加速约400倍，相比FAISS-IVF(GPU)加速约80倍。

Conclusion: ACRONYM通过算法-硬件协同设计成功解决了动态向量数据库搜索中的索引重建问题，在支持连续更新而不停机的同时，实现了高召回率、高吞吐量、低内存占用和低能耗，为RAG、推荐系统等应用提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vector database search with frequent updates is increasingly critical in applications such as retrieval augmented generation, recommendation systems, and large-scale embedding retrieval. Existing solutions, such as graph-based and partition-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS), suffer from frequent index rebuilding due to data distribution-dependent indexing that impacts continuous deployment and causes long rebuilding latency. This paper proposes an algorithm-hardware co-designed platform, ACRONYM, that addresses key problems with state of the art database search. Algorithmically, it leverages efficient encoding independent of data distribution and Hamming-distance based search for efficient hardware acceleration. Architecturally, we propose CAM-based in-memory parallel distance computation followed by time multiplexed approximated top-k selection to enable the exhaustive search. We propose two-stage search that includes coarse search followed by binary refinement to achieve high recall in CAM based search which is heavily limited to small vector dimension due to capacity and wordline parasitic. ACRONYM supports continuous update without stalling and integrates novel XOR-and-Accumulate (XAC) based systolic-array encoder for efficient on chip encoding during search. Across million-scale datasets, while serving dynamic database ACRONYM achieves >90% recall at a throughput of 8e6 queries per second, with a memory footprint of only 32MB and an average energy consumption of 2.56uJ per query, speedup over HNSW (CPU) of about 400x and FAISS-IVF (GPU) of about 80x.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [The Epi-LLM Framework: probing LLM behavioral priors through epidemiological agent-based models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02867)
*Petra Ferenz,Ava Keeling,Tobias O'Keefe,Lorenzo Stigliano,Francesco Di Lauro,Andres Colubri,Jasmina Panovska-Griffiths*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出Epi-LLM框架，通过整合基于主体的建模、大型语言模型和现实流行病博弈游戏，构建能够动态推理和适应的合成社会，用于模拟流行病期间人类行为及其对疾病传播的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类行为会显著影响流行病动态，但量化这一影响面临巨大挑战，传统模型难以捕捉复杂的社会行为决策过程，因此需要一种新的方法来模拟和预测人类在流行病期间的行为响应。

Method: 开发Epi-LLM框架，结合基于主体的建模、现实epigame数据和大型语言模型，创建由LLM驱动的合成智能体社会。这些智能体在疫情接触网络上进行动态推理和适应，模拟15天的疫情爆发过程。将合成智能体行为与无干预SEIR基线和AUIB epigame研究的人类参与者数据进行比较，并使用二项式广义线性模型分析行为预测因子。

Result: 四种LLM架构的智能体均降低了峰值感染率，隔离服从率在第6天达到58-65%的峰值。感知健康严重程度是隔离行为的最强预测因子（β=0.33, p=0.002），伪R²为0.055，与人类试验观察到的0.072相当。低方差LLM架构在测试行为规则方面具有更高的内部效度，而高方差模型可能更好地代表现实世界的决策。单纯地理标签无法产生文化差异化行为，需要明确的态度参数化。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为可扩展、无风险的模拟环境用于大流行病防范研究，为疫情行为干预策略的制定提供了新的研究工具。

Abstract: Human behaviour during epidemics affects infectious disease dynamics, but quantifying this remains deeply challenging. Here we introduce the Epi-LLM framework: a novel integration of agent-based modelling, real-life epigames, and large language models (LLMs) in which a synthetic society of agents reasons and adapts dynamically over an outbreak contact network. Comparing synthetic agent behaviour against a no-intervention SEIR baseline and human participant data from the AUIB epigame study, we find that LLM agents across four different architectures reduced peak active infections, with quarantine compliance peaking at 58-65% on day six of the 15-day simulation. A binomial generalised linear model showed that perceived health severity was the strongest predictor of quarantine behaviour ($β= 0.33, p = 0.002$), yielding a pseudo-$R^2$ of 0.055, comparable to the 0.072 observed in the human trial. LLM architecture is a key determinant of epidemic dynamics: low-variance architectures offer greater internal validity for testing behavioural rules, while high-variance models may better represent real-world decision-making. Geographic labels alone do not induce culturally differentiated behaviour; explicit attitudinal parameterisation is required. This proof-of-principle work lays the groundwork for deploying the Epi-LLM framework as a scalable, risk-free simulation environment for pandemic preparedness research.

</details>


### [246] [Capability Advertisement as a Market for Lemons: A Trust Layer for Heterogeneous Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03034)
*Gaurav Naresh Mittal*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文指出当前LLM代理协议（如MCP和A2A）存在“柠檬市场”问题，导致高质量代理被低质量代理驱逐。作者提出信任层机制，通过概率能力描述符、筛选机制和声誉系统实现高质量代理与低质量代理的分离均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理协议假设声明的能力是静态、真实的事实，但实际代理能力具有概率性、随输入变化、模型更新时会发生漂移，且代理可能自信地错误描述自身能力。调用者只能看到代理声称能做什么，而非实际能做什么，缺乏区分可靠提供者与流畅冒充者的原则性方法。作者认为这些困难源于“柠檬市场”问题：当质量被隐藏且声明成本很低时，优质和劣质提供者变得不可区分，诚实的可靠性得不到回报，市场向最差参与者衰败。

Method: （1）提出失败分类法，将“自信-错误”命名为拜占庭故障的非对抗性、相关性子类；（2）构建柠檬市场模型，证明基于信任的协议仅能维持低信任均衡；（3）设计信任层（Trust Layer）——位于MCP和A2A之上的薄层、协议无关的窄腰，添加概率能力描述符、筛选机制和声誉系统，当维持过度声明的成本超过其收益时实现分离均衡；（4）给出委托链的可靠性组合边界及端到端放置论证。该设计无需模型重训练，在信任锚缺失或损坏时能够优雅降级。

Result: 信任层机制能够在过度声明成本超过收益时实现分离均衡，使高可靠代理与低可靠代理可区分；可靠性组合边界为委托链提供性能保障；系统无需模型重训练即可部署，且在信任锚缺失或受损时保持优雅降级特性。

Conclusion: 当前LLM代理协议缺乏信号传递、筛选和声誉三大经济治理机制，导致低信任均衡。信任层通过引入这些机制，能够在不修改底层模型的前提下提升代理生态的可信度与可靠性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have begun to delegate work to one another. Protocols such as the Model Context Protocol (MCP) and the Agent2Agent protocol (A2A) let an agent publish what it can do and let others call it, and public registries of such agents are already appearing. These protocols assume an advertised capability is a static, truthful fact. A real agent is none of these things: its competence is probabilistic, varies with input, drifts when the underlying model is updated, and, because the agent is itself a language model, it can describe itself with complete confidence and be wrong. A caller therefore sees what an agent claims to do, not what it can do, with no principled way to tell a reliable provider from a fluent impostor.
  We argue these difficulties share one cause: the market for lemons. When quality is hidden and claims are cheap, good and bad providers become indistinguishable, honest reliability goes unrewarded, and the market decays toward its worst participants. Economics offers three remedies, signaling, screening, and reputation, and none are present in today's agent protocols.
  We make four contributions: (1) a failure taxonomy that names confident-wrong as a non-adversarial, correlated subclass of Byzantine faults that classical fault-tolerance mismodels; (2) a market-for-lemons model showing that faith-based protocols admit only a low-trust equilibrium; (3) the Trust Layer, a thin, protocol-agnostic narrow waist above MCP and A2A that adds probabilistic capability descriptors, screening, and reputation, and admits a separating equilibrium when the cost of sustaining an overclaim exceeds the gain from it; and (4) a reliability-composition bound for delegation chains with an end-to-end placement argument. The design needs no model retraining and degrades gracefully when its trust anchors are absent or corrupt.

</details>


### [247] [OpenAgenet/OAN: Open Infrastructure for Trusted Agent Interconnection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03161)
*Jinliang Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: OpenAgenet (OAN) 是一个开放的、协议中立的信任层基础设施，旨在解决Agent从孤立应用进入开放多运营商网络时所面临的身份验证、治理状态、发现授权等信任问题，通过根治理身份准入、注册辅助引导、授权感知发现等机制，为Agent间的安全互联提供信任保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能Agent从孤立的单一应用场景扩展到开放的多运营商网络环境，Agent在安全地发现、选择和调用其他Agent之前，缺乏有效的机制来验证身份来源、治理状态、发现授权、时效性和连接前的信任证据，这一信任真空问题亟需解决。

Method: OAN采用协议中立的信任层设计，不替代现有的Agent交互协议、工具协议、模型编排框架或应用层工作流，而是提供五大核心机制：根治理身份准入（Root-governed identity admission）、注册辅助引导（Registrar-assisted onboarding）、根验证包发布（Root-verified package publication）、授权感知发现（authorization-aware Discovery）和签名可信调用（signed trusted invocation）。同时采用区块链支持的授权公告机制。

Result: 本文详细阐述了OAN的架构设计、角色定义、治理模型、与MCP/A2A/ANP的集成关系、部署模式、合作模型、区块链授权公告机制，并报告了原型系统的实现状态和性能分析结果。

Conclusion: OAN作为协议中立的信任层基础设施，有效填补了Agent开放网络互联中的信任空白，为Agent间安全互联提供了完整的信任解决方案，并通过原型验证了其可行性，为未来大规模Agent网络部署奠定了基础。

Abstract: OpenAgenet, abbreviated as OAN, is an open infrastructure project for trusted Agent interconnection. It addresses a problem that becomes visible when Agents move from isolated applications into open, multi-operator networks: before an Agent can safely discover, select, and invoke another Agent, it needs a way to verify identity provenance, governance state, discovery authorization, freshness, and pre-connection trust evidence. OAN is designed as a protocol-neutral trust layer. It does not replace Agent interaction protocols, tool protocols, model orchestration frameworks, or application-level workflows. Instead, it provides Root-governed identity admission, Registrar-assisted onboarding, Root-verified package publication, authorization-aware Discovery, and signed trusted invocation. This paper presents the motivation, architecture, roles, governance model, relationship with MCP, A2A, and ANP, deployment patterns, cooperation model, blockchain-backed authorization bulletin, prototype status, performance profile, and roadmap of OAN.

</details>


### [248] [OpenAgenet/OAN: Technical Architecture for Trust-Governed Agent Identity and Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03163)
*Jinliang Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenAgenet(OAN)，一个协议中立的开放Agent互联信任层，定义了身份准入、发现、验证和安全交互机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构Agent框架和交互协议并存的开放环境中，缺乏统一的信任机制来确保Agent身份的可靠性、可发现性和交互安全性，因此需要构建一个协议中立的信任层。

Method: 设计了一套技术架构，包括：角色架构、身份对象、注册工作流、Root治理的生命周期、Root验证的包模型、授权感知发现、签名可信调用、验证要求、状态转换、安全属性、实现边界和部署考量。

Result: 构建了一个支持异构Agent框架（包括MCP、A2A、ANP类系统及领域特定Agent协议）的协议中立信任层架构，实现了Agent身份在协议特定交互开始前的准入、发现、验证和安全接触机制。

Conclusion: OAN通过定义Agent身份如何成为可准入、可发现、可验证且安全可接触的机制，为异构Agent生态系统提供了统一的信任基础设施，而不涉及Agent间完整的业务对话定义。

Abstract: This paper describes the technical architecture of OpenAgenet / OAN. OAN is a protocol-neutral trust layer for open Agent interconnection. It specifies the role architecture, identity objects, registration workflow, Root-governed lifecycle, Root-verified package model, authorization-aware Discovery, signed trusted invocation, verification requirements, state transitions, security properties, implementation boundaries, and deployment considerations. The design is intended to support heterogeneous Agent frameworks and interaction protocols, including MCP, A2A, ANP-like systems, and domain-specific Agent protocols. OAN does not define the entire business conversation among Agents; it defines how Agent identities become admissible, discoverable, verifiable, and safe to approach before protocol-specific interaction begins.

</details>


### [249] [MeDxAgent: Multi-Agent Consultation for Interactive Medical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03416)
*Akshat Sanghvi,Naren Akash,Raza Imam,Amit Sharma,Mohit Jain*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模交互式诊断基准MeDxBench，并提出多智能体系统MeDxAgent，通过模拟临床诊断流程，相比基线准确率提升10.3%，缩小了52.3%的信息差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在医疗诊断评估中常将其视为一次性任务，并预设信息完整，这与临床实践中交互式、开放式的诊断过程存在显著差距，缺乏对临床真实诊断流程的模拟。

Method: 构建了包含20个专科、4,421个临床案例的大规模基准MeDxBench；提出了多智能体咨询系统MeDxAgent，系统性地研究了提示层、流程层和智能体层的设计选择。

Result: MeDxAgent在MeDxBench上相比基线准确率提升10.3%，缩小了52.3%与全信息Oracle之间的差距。研究发现优先收集人口统计信息、传递对话摘要进行诊断、为针对性提问提供候选诊断等设计选择可提升准确性，但其效果需在组合时才能完全体现。

Conclusion: 模拟医师推理过程的特定设计选择能有效提升交互式诊断系统的准确性，这些发现为构建更符合临床实践的LLM诊断系统提供了重要见解。代码和数据集将在发表后公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for health-related decision support. Yet most evaluations treat diagnosis as a single-shot task with complete information provided upfront, often as a multiple-choice selection. This diverges from clinical practice, where diagnosis is interactive and open-ended, involving sequential hypothesis refinement through targeted questioning. We address this gap. We build MeDxBench, a large-scale benchmark of 4,421 clinical cases across 20 specialties. We further propose MeDxAgent, a multi-agent consultation system for interactive diagnosis, and systematically study its prompt-, flow- and agent-level design choices. MeDxAgent achieves a 10.3% accuracy gain over the baseline on MeDxBench, closing 52.3% of the gap to a full-information oracle. We find that specific design choices: collecting demographics first, passing summarized dialogue for diagnosis, and feeding candidate diagnoses for targeted questioning, improve accuracy, mirroring how physicians reason, though their effect emerges fully only in combination. Code and dataset will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [250] [D2MDT: Department-aware Multidisciplinary Team Consultation with Deliberation for Efficient Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03543)
*Yongqi Liang,Qidong Liu,Chunze Yang,Lei Wu,Jiusong Ge,Ni Zhang,Chen Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出D2MDT框架，利用科室感知的多智能体会诊机制和残差审议策略，在电子健康记录临床预测中实现了性能与效率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电子健康记录（EHR）临床预测方法主要依赖基于相关性的深度模型或单一大型语言模型，难以支持多学科临床推理。尽管多智能体系统（MAS）提供了新的解决方案，但当前的EHR多智能体方法仍存在证据区分度弱和多轮交互冗余的问题。

Method: 提出D2MDT框架：1）构建结构化EHR证据和面向会诊的语义证据；2）为医生智能体分配患者特异性的科室视角并检索互补证据；3）引入残差审议机制，仅更新未解决的共识而非重演完整讨论历史；4）融合精炼的共识报告与结构化EHR表征进行预测。

Result: 在死亡率预测任务上的实验表明，D2MDT在提升预测性能的同时显著提高了会诊效率。

Conclusion: D2MDT通过科室感知的多学科团队会诊和残差审议机制，有效解决了现有EHR多智能体方法中的证据区分度弱和交互冗余问题，为临床预测提供了一种高效且性能优越的新方法，论文已开源代码以保证可复现性。

Abstract: Electronic health records (EHRs) are central to clinical prediction, but existing methods either rely on correlation-driven deep models or use single large language models (LLMs), making it difficult to support multidisciplinary clinical reasoning. Recent multi-agent systems (MAS) provide a promising alternative, yet current EHR-grounded MAS methods still suffer from weak evidence differentiation across agents and redundant multi-round interaction. We propose D2MDT, a Department-aware MultiDisciplinary Team Consultation with Deliberation for Efficient clinical prediction. D2MDT first constructs structured EHR evidence and consultation-ready semantic evidence for multi-agent consultation. It then assigns patient-specific department perspectives to doctor agents and retrieves complementary evidence for collaborative consultation. To improve efficiency, D2MDT further introduces residual deliberation, which updates only unresolved consensus rather than replaying the full discussion history. Finally, D2MDT fuses the refined consensus report with structured EHR representations for prediction. Experiments on mortality prediction show that D2MDT improves both predictive performance and consultation efficiency. We release the code online to ease the reproducibility of this paper.

</details>
